Chapter 2601: News Below the Ground

"Wuying Qi is truly out of his mind!"

Li Yao also mumbled, "Today is different from the past. The crust of the capital planet already changed drastically ten thousand years ago. It is now in a man-made unstable state. If he makes things worse, it is possible that the whole continent will sink into the ocean, and the bottom of the ocean will be heaved by several hundred meters, transforming into a new continent.

"When such huge changes take place on the surface of the planet, it remains to be seen whether or not the residents living in the cities could survive, but the ten billion residents living below the ground, including the people of the Great Iron Plants, will certainly be killed in the accident. Will Wuying Qi really be heartless enough to do that?"

"Of course he is!"

Li Jialing gnashed his teeth and said, "Considering Lei Chenghu's coldness and brutality as an Immortal Cultivator, the casualties of a few hundred million will definitely be regarded as 'acceptable price' or even 'necessary sacrifice'. It's not a big deal. However, since Wuying Qi does not dare to inform Lei Chenghu of the truth, it is evident that the victims to be sacrificed in his plan will far exceed three to five hundred million. Chances are that ten billion people will die!"

Such an appalling number silenced all three of them.

The quakes in the underground gaps that happened once in a while also gave them the intense fear that the rocks would close soon and bury them.

Thankfully, whenever the intense quakes happened, there would be no need to worry that Wuying Qi's alarm magical equipment would detect them, and Li Yao could take out his drill of mystic rays to press forward boldly. Their speed was significantly increased. In only a couple of hours, they already reached the spacious and flat tunnel that they stopped by last time.

The tunnel was already up ahead. Judging from the sounds, heavy tractors were rumbling by every once in a while. Hiding in a corner in the gap among the rocks, Li Yao released a few crystal cameras the size of flies to explore the environment.

The few crystal cameras flew out of the gap without a sound and happened to catch an overwhelming team of tractors that extended from this end of the tunnel all the way to the other end. It was impossible to see the star or the end of the tractors.

The team was mostly made of super-heavy tractors with a capacity of more than a hundred tons. They were like moving hills.

The tractors were covered in cloth. Based on the traces of the tracks, they were all fully loaded, and they had been driving for a long time.

Holding his breath, Li Yao released his telepathic thoughts and controlled a few crystal cameras to dig into the gaps of the cloth.

"Crystals? So many ultimate crystals of high purity?"

Li Yao was slightly stunned by the dazzling pictures sent back by the crystal cameras.

Beneath the seemingly plain cloth were loads of colorful, high-purity crystals, which had been repetitively purified and were readily absorbable for the trainers.

Li Yao even discovered quite a few marrow crystals. They were all rare treasures!

It was obvious that the crystals came from a myriad of sources and were wrapped very hastily. A lot of them were not stored in standard boxes but exposed to the air randomly.

In doing so, not only would the quality of the crystals be affected, but they might also cause inflammations or explosions. The excessive radiations they unleashed would hurt the shippers too.

Also, Li Yao felt that some of the crystals were whetted in a very strange way. They had all been built into an octagonal shape, with a hundred attack rune arrays carved on the surface.

Li Yao thought for a long time. For what purposes would a crystal be whetted in such a way, and why were so many attack rune arrays carved on them?

Three minutes later, it finally occurred to him that those high-purity crystals should've been used to power the massive anti-air positions. They were the ammunitions of the crystal cannons!

The capital, being the capital, naturally boasted a perfect defense network with comprehensive anti-air facilities. The whole planet was brimming with anti-air cannons that protruded at space. It was almost like a supermassive iron hedgehog.

The crystals and ammunitions of all sorts stored in the capital were naturally astronomical too.

However, right now, Wuying Qi had removed the crystals and even the marrow crystals that powered the anti-air positions, shipping them to the underground world.

Controlling the crystal cameras, Li Yao selected another few random tractors and checked their carriage. The result was the same. Tremendous crystals for anti-air positions and even for starships had been detached and moved to the underground world.

"What's the meaning of this?"

Li Yao blinked quickly. "The four families' ace fleets have arrived and will launch a general attack at the capital planet soon. Such a critical moment is exactly when the anti-air positions are needed most. However, Wuying Qi destroys his own defense by transporting the crystals for anti-air purposes to the underground world. Has he given up on the atmosphere defense?"

Li Yao shared the pictures he saw and his questions with Long Yangjun and Li Jialing. The three of them discussed for a moment and reached two conclusions.

Firstly, Wuying Qi truly trusted the secret weapon deeply buried below the ground. He believed that the other defensive measures were unnecessary as long as the secret weapon existed. Not even a single anti-air crystal cannon was needed on the capital planet. He would win the war the moment the secret weapon was activated.

Secondly, the activation of the secret weapon required astronomical energy. That was why Wuying Qi was sparing no efforts gathering crystals.

Over the past few months, he must've drained the whole capital planet. All the crystals in the arsenals, the warehouses, and among the civilians had been confiscated by him. That was why those crystals came from a myriad sources and were not appropriately preserved before they were shipped. In the end, he did not even let go of the anti-air positions but moved all the crystals away.

Of course, not all the crystals were supplied for the secret weapon. A proportion of them must've been used to blow up the most vulnerable parts in the tectonic structure to tear apart the land so that the secret weapon could be raised.

"What kind of magical equipment needs to be powered by the crystals of a whole planet?"

Li Yao found it hard to imagine. "Is it a piece of the legendary 'stellar magical equipment'?"

After checking the assets loaded in the tractors, Li Yao paid special attention to the soldiers who were carrying out the secret mission to ship the crystals.

That was because Li Yao had been haunted by one question, which was the identity of Wuying Qi's accomplices who helped him with the insane, appalling crime.

Yes, Wuying Qi's fundamental supporters were the Imperial Guards and the militarists who craved to restore the glory of the Imperium like Lei Chenghu.

As long as Wuying Qi proved that he was "Blackstar the Great", most of the Imperial Guards would certainly follow their ancestor determinedly.

However, there should be a limit to their inhumanity.

The capital planet was home to most of the Imperial Guards, who had lived here for dozens of generations. Right now, their wives, their children, and their parents lived in this place too. This planet was their home and their everything!

If Wuying Qi asked them to fight the four families, Li Yao believed that most of the Imperial Guards would not cringe.

However, if Wuying Qi asked them to detonate the capital planet, the home that they had lived on for a thousand years, and blow up their family members by the way, they couldn't have obeyed such a command even if something were wrong with their heads!

Li Yao did not believe that Wuying Qi could've fooled so many Imperial Guards into working for him, to the point of destroying their own hometown and families, only through deception.

It was highly unreasonable that so many crystals were shipped to the underground world and that the anti-air positions on the ground were all abandoned.

Most of the Imperial Guards were Immortal Cultivators. Which of them would charge forward relentlessly without figuring out what was behind it first?

Therefore, Li Yao paid attention to the Imperial Guards who were escorting the tractor team, particularly the officers with flowers on their epaulets who were surrounded by powerful vibes.

As he scanned the officers more carefully, Li Yao had a very uncanny feeling.

How should he put it? While soldiers were supposed to be grave and serious, the Imperial Guards who were wearing the pure-black, first-generation uniform of the army appeared to be lifeless.

They looked so determined, and their movements were so synchronized that they were almost no different from machines had it not been for their breaths.

Li Yao wouldn't say that they were devoid of the feelings of human beings. However, 90% of their feelings had been filled up by certain zealotry and piety.

"Come and take a look. What are those people?"

Li Yao shared the pictures that his crystal cameras scanned with Long Yangjun and Li Jialing.

"They look like…"

After watching for a long time, Li Jialing said, "They look a lot like the Nepenthe believers in the Great Iron Plants."

"Exactly. The magnetic fields from their brains are very strange. Interferences to a certain extent have been imposed on them, adjusting their brainwaves to the same frequency. They are in some sort of 'reverberation'."

Long Yangjun said, "You can say that they have been collectively hypnotized, or that they are in a certain state of collective unconsciousness or massive hysteria. Of course, it is not wrong to say that they have been brainwashed either."

"So, this is the way that Wuying Qi has adopted to make the Imperial Guards work for him!"

Li Yao recalled the riot of the captives of the Covenant Alliance that he had seen in the Godly Might Prison. The brains of those people of the Covenant Alliance were indeed similar to that of the Imperial Guards that he saw before his eyes.

Wuying Qi tried to brainwash him in the Seven Seas Grand Market. Although it did not work out, it implied that the man mastered some sort of terrifying brainwashing technologies. He was almost a duplicate of the Covenant Alliance!

It was not easy to brainwash Li Yao, but it was certainly not a problem to hypnotize and control a few Imperial Guards who had a deep trust in him in the first place.

"However, we cannot jump to any conclusion only by observing remotely."

Long Yangjun said, "It will be best if we can secretly capture a few Imperial Guards and scan their brains from every perspective. That way, we will know what is wrong with them."

Li Yao was thinking about how to catch a few captives when noises echoed ahead in the tunnel.

Chapter 2602: Brain Dissection

"Ahhhhhhhhhhhhhh!"

Creepy howls came over from the end of the tunnel.

It sounded like a ferocious beast on a rampage that had been skinned alive.

Very soon, a daunting shadow rushed into Li Yao and his companions' sight after a few leaps.

It was also an officer of the Imperial Guards. Judging from his epaulet, he was of a rather high military rank.

His face, however, couldn't have been more appalling. His head had expanded to twice the normal size. Quite a few lumps were rising high on his forehead and his temples, fluctuating like beating hearts.

His eyes were so red that they were almost purple, so purple that they were almost black. From the festering eyes, tremendous colorful, suspicious fluids were flowing out, as if his brain had entirely been turned into mucus that was being squeezed out.

Even so, he still boasted unparalleled strength. After a wave of his hands, he already tore two Imperial Guards who went forward to stop him into two pieces, their guts flowing all over the ground.

If the vitality field of the other Imperial Guards was like a lifeless river that was running at a fixed speed, the vitality field of this mad officer would be as violent as a colorful, surging vortex.

After killing a couple of soldiers in a row, he threw his head at a heavy tractor. He even tried to lift the heavy tractor that weighed hundreds of tons. His arms were squeaking, his legs deeply sank into the ground, and even his arm bones were protruding out of his elbows, but he couldn't care less about it.

The officer had completely gone mad.

The "normal" Imperial Guards around were utterly fearless. They took the opportunity to charge forward. After paying a price of more than ten victims, they finally dealt a fatal blow at the mad officer.

The mad officer, however, was unbelievably tough. His internal organs were almost burnt up by the heating guns and the crystal guns. A devastating hole appeared on his torso. However, his wails did not die down until more than half a minute later.

At this moment, his head had expanded to three times the normal size. The few lumps on his temples and the center of his eyebrows were as swollen as grapes that might explode at any moment.

A team of soldiers wearing special helmets that looked like anti-epidemic personnel immediately went forward and dragged the corpses of the officers and the dead soldiers away into an inconspicuous branch next to the tunnel.

The rest of the Imperial Guards seemed to be too familiar with such a scene to be surprised. It took them only half a minute to restore their order and continue shipping the crystals mechanically.

Li Yao and Long Yangjun looked at each other and said at the same time, "Follow them!"

Li Yao split a few crystal cameras and followed the team of "anti-epidemic soldiers" from not far behind them.

In the meantime, he scanned the tunnel and the branch and simulated the structure in his crystal processor. When another quake took place, he powered the drill of mystic rays and crawled toward the branch.

In the end, together with Long Yangjun and Li Jialing, he put on his crystal suit and turned on the stealth mode. The three of them were melted into the environment like chameleons.

The branch was zigzagging and now high, now low. It took the anti-epidemic soldiers more than twenty minutes to finish a thousand meters and reach their destination while carrying a massive amount of bodies with floating discs.

At the end of the branch was a hole from which fire and smoke were jetting out nonstop. There seemed to be a magma pool somewhere deep down.

Huala!

The anti-epidemic soldiers threw the corpses of a few soldiers in first. The dead bodies were immediately taken away by the fire and disappeared into the smoke. Melted by the magma, they did not have a single cell left.

"Let's do it!"

The anti-epidemic soldiers were about to throw the mad officer's body into the magma when Li Yao and Long Yangjun dashed down. Flashing into almost ten clones, they controlled the four anti-epidemic soldiers instantly and made them fall to the ground without any sound.

For safety, Li Yao set up an alarm rune array and a sound-blocking rune array on the branch, in case the sounds here might spread out.

"Do you want me to do it, or do you want to do it yourself?"

Li Yao asked Long Yangjun.

"Let me. I have a more profound understanding of the so-called 'brainwashing' than you."

Long Yangjun took out a few scalpels that were as thin as paper from her Cosmos Ring. They danced among her fingertips agilely.

Li Yao and Li Jialing helped her move the rest of the bodies, and they only kept that of the mad officer on the floating disc. Then, adjusting the anti-gravity rune array, Li Yao set the altitude of the floating disc to Long Yangjun's chest, turning it into a simple operation stable.

Long Yangjun's fingers turned into ten streaks of brilliance and operated on the officer's uneven head accurately. Very soon, the shattered skull was removed, and all the broken bones were picked out, but the brain tissues inside were not damaged at all.

However, although Long Yangjun did not injure the brain tissues of the mad officer, they were already as messy as churned tofu. If Long Yangjun had not covered and supported it with spiritual energy, they would've spurted all over the ground.

Thankfully, Li Yao and Li Jialing were both ruthless men, or they would've thrown up.

Focusing her attention, Long Yangjun separated the muddled brain tissues. Three minutes later, she found what she wanted.

"Do you see it? The brain tissues here went out of control. The brain cells proliferated crazily thousands of times faster than the speed of normal metabolism, unleashing abundant heat and causing severe internal bleeding."

Pointing at the abnormally swollen brain tissues with forceps, Long Yangjun said, "That's the reason why his head expanded twice and why he went mad."

Li Yao nodded to show that he understood it.

The symptom of the officer was not rare among the trainers. Many classic diseases in mental derangement would cause simple problems.

The cells inside a human body, both the body cells and the brain cells, died and grew every day. After every seven to ten days, the cells all over the body would be renewed. This process was known as "metabolism".

Metabolism was an indispensable function to ensure the vitality and vigor of human beings.

One of the most important training methods for both the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators was to absorb abundant spiritual energy and accelerate the speed of metabolism of a certain region or their entire bodies through special techniques.

The renewal of all the cells in the body was known as "one cycle". On the premise of enough resources, an expert of training could have dozens of cycles when they mediated for hours, renewing their cells dozens of times. That was why they boasted abilities far exceeded what the ordinary people were capable of.

However, it was not necessarily a good thing that the speed of metabolism was too fast.

That was because the renewal of cells would consume abundant resources and spiritual energy and also generate and discharge tremendous wastes, including the dead cells, the blood that covered the deal cells, the mucus, and even additional heat.

Such wastes had to be absorbed by the capillary vessels and shipped to the skin, where they were discharged through the pores.

That was why one would be spurting out hot steam and secreting stinky, black fluids when they were training hard.

If the speed of discharging could not keep up with the speed at which the wastes were produced, the poisonous wastes would be gathered inside the body, blocking the veins and nerves and disrupting the normal circulation of spiritual energy and body fluids. Tremendous damage would be caused, even including severe consequences such as mental derangement.

It was exactly the case for the officer.

His brain had too many "cycles" too quickly. The brain cells in a region were updated countless times, but the dead brain cells and blood were not discharged in time. Also, the heat released during the metabolism pressed and tore apart the regular brain tissues and cerebral vessels. Even the skull was blown up by the force. As a result, he was mentally deranged and went mad.

"How did it happen?"

Li Jialing couldn't help but touch his own head. "Such abnormal expansion of the head usually happens only when one is practicing powerful techniques or advancing into a new level, right? This officer was carrying out a mission. How on earth was he mentally deranged for no good reason?"

"It's not exactly 'for no good reason'. Look, it was this cluster of brain tissues that had abnormalities. Many brain specialists and Meditation Healers believe that this is the area responsible for memories. They are known as 'memory cells'."

Long Yangjun opened the abnormal brain cells with the forceps softly and said, "Focus your spiritual energy on your eyes and set the resolution of your eyes to the highest. What can you see from here?"

Li Yao took out a pair of hazel glasses from his Cosmos Ring and handed it to Li Jialing. With the glasses, Li Jialing narrowed his eyes and observed carefully for a long time before he remarked in surprise, "Bleeding points! So many bleeding points!"

"Yes. Those bleeding points were not formed recently. They were formed because somebody repetitively stimulated the area of memory cells over the past few days, if not months."

Long Yangjun said, "The memory cells of this officer had already been ravaged and riddled with holes. They were essentially a mess, even more vulnerable than the memory cells of the ordinary people. When something went wrong, it was possible that the repair genes of the memory cells would be triggered, leading to abnormal expansion and internal bleeding. Eventually, they caused mental derangement."

"Human beings boast a rather powerful self-recovery system. The recovery functions of the trainers are even more impressive."

Li Yao explained, "The memory cells of the officer have been repetitively stimulated and traumatized. So, his brain sent a special order to the cells, asking them to 'repair the damaged cells and proliferate more cells'. However, when the order was carried too many times, things went out of control, and the crazy repair and proliferation became unrestrained, making him end up like this.

"In fact, the proliferation of body tissues is commonly seen on ordinary people too, as could be seen by the scars after wounds heal. However, for the Immortal Cultivators, their speed of metabolism and cell recovery are dozens of times higher than that of the ordinary people. Once the cells lose control, the consequences will be much more severe.

"Not just the memory cells, there's also this spot."

Long Yangjun began to dissect the brain tissues on the other side. "Similar phenomena of swelling and proliferation can be found here too. Some of the brain cells have been expanded more than ten times its normal size. How can he not go mad?"

Chapter 2603: A Full Purge

"I know that."

Li Jialing said, "This is the right hemisphere of the human brain that controls feelings. The left hemisphere and the right hemisphere of the human brain have different functions. The left hemisphere is responsible for memories, logical thinking, and analysis, while the right hemisphere is responsible for feelings, music, painting, and other functions. Since the left hemisphere and the right hemisphere both have abundant bleeding points and abnormal swelling, it means that the man's memories and feelings are controlled, or at least under the effect of high-intensity stimulation."

"It's basically correct, but I need to slightly rectify something."

Long Yangjun nodded her head and said, "I know that many brain experts and Meditation Healers have proposed the asymmetric functionality of the left hemisphere and the right hemisphere in their masterpieces, and they have divided the brain into many different modules such as 'memory zone' and 'emotion zone', but as a matter of fact, human beings' memories, feelings, and self-awareness are all very mysterious and unpredictable. 'Control' does not even begin to describe their purpose."

"It must be noted that even if the brain is completely destroyed and the human turns into a ghost, as long as more than 50% of the soul is retained and accommodated by an anti-radiation shell and a high-precision crystal processor, most of the memories and feelings before their death will be kept. Therefore, the theory that the brain tissues have complete control over the soul is not necessarily true.

"In my opinion, the brain is to the soul what the soil is to a tree or even to the fruits on the tree.

"The quality of the soil will certainly decide the quality of the tree and whether the fruits are sweet or sour. However, once the tree grows up and yields abundant fruits, even if the soil is removed, it will be possible to transplant the tree to a certain 'soilless' environment or to pick the fruits and preserve them in a vacuum environment with cooling rune arrays. They will not go bad in decades.

"That is to say, the brain is the soil to gather the soul as well as the consciousness. However, once the soul and the consciousness take shape, their demands on the brain tissues will not be as strong.

"We can only say that stimulating this area on the left hemisphere can interfere with the memories to some extent, and stimulating that area on the right hemisphere can briefly alter one's personality.

"If the two areas are completely wiped out through surgeries, human beings will lose their memories and feelings, turning into numb, emptyheaded machines."

Li Jialing thought for a moment and asked, "Like the people of the Covenant Alliance?"

"Judging from the density of the bleeding points and the qualitative changes of the brain, he hadn't reached the point where his feelings were entirely excised. The two areas in the brains of the people of the Covenant Alliance are shrunk but not swollen. This officer must've rather intense feelings. However, about to whom his feelings are aimed at, I have no idea."

Long Yangjun thought for a moment and said, "The changes are similar to the Nepenthe believers who practiced Serenity Arts. The degree is more or less the same although the directions are different."

Li Jialing was stunned. "Will the Serenity Arts cause qualitative changes to the brains?"

"Even if they hadn't practiced the Serenity Arts, their brains would still go through qualitative changes given the fact that they lived in extreme fear every second!"

Long Yangjun glanced at Li Jialing and said, "Respect the brain, but don't imagine the brain to be too enigmatic. Getting to the bottom of it, the brain is just a regular organ on one's body.

"Consider such an example. If a person grows up in famines and feeds on grasses and rinds eight out of ten days, will his body suffer qualitative changes? Will he be emaciated, skinny, and have all kinds of diseases?"

Li Jialing blurted out, "Of course!"

"Therefore, if it is perfectly normal that your stomach shrinks when there is not enough food, is it something to be surprised at that some areas inside the brain also shrink and mutate?"

Long Yangjun said, "The so-called Serenity Arts merely guided and helped them to adapt to the changes peacefully and safely."

"Alright, you are both the god and the devil. You have left nothing for me to say."

Li Yao said, "There's no need to embroider yourself. We'll talk about your problem later. Let's focus on Wuying Qi for now. The cause of this officer's madness has been found. It seems that he was brainwashed, and he went mad because of the side effects and the sequela of the brainwashing. I wonder what's the situation with other people. Let's take a look at the healthy soldiers."

Li Jialing was rather shocked. "Brother Yao, you are not cutting the heads of the four living soldiers, are you?"

"What are you thinking about?"

Li Yao flicked Li Jialing's forehead. "Aren't there quite a few bodies? Here, this body's skull has been broken in the first place. Not much extra work is needed!"

The two of them carried the second body to the floating disc. Long Yangjun dissected him again and found the target areas accurately.

"He's the same. There are dense bleeding points on both sides of the brain. Also, the brain tissues had slightly expanded, like the proliferation of body tissues when the same spot is wounded a hundred times."

Li Jialing observed carefully and drew the conclusion. "However, it seems that the soldier's conditions were not as serious as the mad officer's. He was still controllable."

"It means that all of the officers and soldiers in Wuying Qi's secret weapon base have been brainwashed by him to different degrees."

Li Yao summarized, "The damaged areas in the soldiers' brains are exactly the same areas that Wuying Qi attacked me back in the Seven Seas Grand Market. It is exactly the technology of the Covenant Alliance.

"The short-time, high-intensity 'hard brainwashing' causes huge damage to the brain. However, whether or not one is mentally deranged depends on his body quality, his level, and his luck. This officer must've had a lot of injuries in his brain during the high-intensity training beforehand. He had been suppressing the injuries by force, but after he was repetitively brainwashed, his cerebral vessels exploded, and his brain cells proliferated, resulting in his death."

"Such a terrifying brainwashing method!"

Li Jialing was rather shocked. "This is rather unbelievable."

"Nothing is unbelievable here. Brainwashing is not anything bizarre or mysterious. It is a real thing that can be studied, analyzed, and duplicated."

Li Yao pointed at Li Jialing's head and said, "Since Wuying Qi's ultimate weapon base is more and more related to 'brainwashing technologies', we must figure out what the so-called 'brainwashing' is first.

"In my opinion, there are two kinds of brainwashing, namely 'soft brainwashing' and 'hard brainwashing'.

"What is 'soft brainwashing'? Let me give you the simplest example. The two of us have been in each other's company for a long time. During the time, I preach to you every day and hum like a fly, trying to imprint the beliefs of the Cultivators into your head, and I set up a role model for you by saving the people from disasters often. The mighty and glorious image of the Cultivators is deeply stamped in your heart. As a result, a couple of years later, you are really transformed into a Cultivator. Then, can I say that I performed certain brainwashing on you?

"In my opinion, that is certainly true. However, this is a kind of soft brainwashing whose influence is very insignificant. It's like the ads you see every once in a while on the Spiritual Nexus, telling you how interesting an item is or how intriguing a game is. They are all the softest brainwashing methods."

"I understand it now if you put it that way, Brother Yao!"

Li Jialing clapped his hands. "The soft brainwashing is the gentlest and worst brainwashing method that is like the bite of a mosquito. Brother Yao has brainwashed me for such a long time but still cannot set up a glorious image in my head. You can say whatever you want, but I often find you hilarious!"

Li Yao: "… Okay, let's forget the details. Another example is when children learn the mainstream values and worldviews of the society in school. They are taught with the beliefs of the Cultivators or the Immortal Cultivators over a period of decades. Is that a kind of brainwashing, too?

"I think it is, if we are being strict. It all depends on whether or not everything is told truthfully and nothing is hidden during the education, and whether or not the children are forbidden from accessing different opinions through other channels.

"If the advantages and disadvantages of all beliefs, as well as their serious mistakes and glorious achievements in the past, are all told to the children with an open mind, such brainwashing will be soft. If the facts are twisted or hidden, and the education comes with physical abuse or material stimulation, then such brainwashing will be harder and more violent.

"Think about it. It has been inculcated into the heads of multiple generations of the Imperium that Wuying Qi is supreme and invincible during the past hundreds of years. That definitely counts as brainwashing too. However, we are not very concerned about that, are we?

"The brainwashing methods above have two things in common. First of all, the time cost is too huge. It sometimes takes decades before someone is completely fooled.

"Secondly, those brainwashing methods will not result in qualitative changes in the brain. Or rather, the changes are slow and gradual. During the prolonged brainwashing process, even if the brains had been damaged, they would've recovered by themselves.

"Therefore, the ways of brainwashing that I have listed above can all be categorized into 'soft brainwashing'. In many cases, it is even barely possible to distinguish 'soft brainwashing' from 'education'.

"The Imperial Guards who have been killed miserably before us, on the other hand, are clearly not the victims of 'soft brainwashing', but have received high-intensity 'hard brainwashing'. Some of the areas inside their brains received excessive radiations or were exposed to powerful magnetic fields in only a couple of days or weeks at most, which resulted in serious qualitative changes inside the areas inside their brains. Their memories and feelings were affected, and their self-awareness was twisted. This is the brainwashing of the most narrow sense, and it could even be called the real brainwashing!"

Chapter 2604: The Impossible Truth!

Li Yao asked Long Yangjun and Li Jialing to step aside. As he took a deep breath, his eyes immediately spread out like crimson pools of blood.

Focusing his spiritual energy on the tip of his finger, he stabbed one of the unconscious soldiers softly by using the finger as a needle, waking up the guy.

"Look at my eyes."

Li Yao first mumbled with a voice as gentle as a feather, before he repeated it in a high-pitched cry, "Look at my eyes!"

The soldier shuddered, and his eyes that just became clear fell into confusion again, as if his entire soul had been absorbed into Li Yao's eyes.

"Now, you are lying on a fluffy and fragrant land. The sky is blue, the clouds are white, and the sun is shining warmly. You feel extremely comfortable…"

Weird brilliance beamed out of Li Yao's eyes, and his hands drew glamorous patterns like unpredictable flowers.

The anti-epidemic soldier was completely hypnotized by Li Yao. As Li Yao talked, the look of corresponding feelings popped up on his face.

When Li Yao spoke of the blue sky and the white clouds, his face was full of relief and ease. When Li Yao described beautiful girls, there was nothing but greediness there. When Li Yao talked about carrions and skeletons, it was replaced by disgust and fear.

"What is Brother Yao doing?"

Li Jialing asked Long Yangjun in a low voice.

"He has deeply hypnotized the target to test if the target still has the normal abilities to feel and think logically."

Long Yangjun explained, "If his feelings had been completely wiped out, like the people of the Covenant Alliance, he would not react so obviously to the illusions that Li Yao constructed. At the very least, his face would be showing a blank expression.

"Also, Li Yao is now asking him questions that can only be answered with a strong sense of logic, and he can answer them without any hesitation. It is evident enough that his feelings, thinking ability, and memories are not entirely destroyed. Wuying Qi only disrupted and modified a tiny area inside his brain.

"It does make sense. Those Imperial Guards are loyal to Wuying Qi and ready to die for him in the first place. Therefore, there is no need to brainwash them deeply."

Li Jialing pondered for a moment. "But there's still no telling what tricks Wuying Qi has played on their brains."

Long Yangjun smiled and said softly, "You will know it soon enough."

At this moment, Li Yao had finished the basic test on the anti-epidemic soldier. Thinking for a moment, he snapped next to the guy's ear to let him regain consciousness.

The light in the eyes of the anti-epidemic soldier was clear and bright again.

Based on his companions and the dead bodies lying next to him, he immediately realized what was going on. He struggled to lunge at Li Yao. Chapter 2606: Absolute Mental Defense!

Li Jialing thought about all the procedures, but he still failed to figure out how Wuying Qi was going to play the trick. He said somewhat in embarrassment, "That explains a lot. It seems that I severely underestimated the difficulty of massive brainwashing. Sorry for the waste of your time."

Li Jialing was rendered speechless by Li Yao's righteous and determined face.

He accidentally saw Long Yangjun who was next to him, only to discover that she was enjoying it.

Li Jialing was dumbfounded. "Sister Long, you-you are actually watching this?"

"They are well shot. Why can't I watch them?"

Long Yangjun combed her hair and gave Li Jialing a meaningful smile. "Did you know that Sister Long used to be a powerful eunuch for decades? Do you know what the most important job for such powerful eunuchs is? That is to distract the emperor and make the emperor have fun every day and ignore the national affairs so that they can take over the power.

"Therefore, Sister Long has learned a lot about the tricks of ultimate euphoria. I even know a thing or two about how to train those soul-stirring girls.

"Li Yao was not wrong. With Wuying Qi's wisdom, he must've blocked all the loopholes possible, making it impossible to resolve his scheme. If we are going to crack his brainwashing technique, this… decadent way may be the only solution.

"Come on, don't be shy. Those videos were found from your portable magical equipment in the first place. There's no need to pretend to be innocent, right?"

Li Yao and Long Yangjun, each from one side, dragged Li Jialing to the ground. They all sat down in their most comfortable postures.

The super experts of their level were mostly in possession of self-hypnotization and self-purification techniques.

Li Yao had the assistance of the mental devil, and Long Yangjun had the primeval heritages. They were adept at mental attacks in the first place. Although Li Jialing was not as good as them, he was able to accept the inculcation of the two seasoned experts, allowing him to transform the morally unacceptable pictures into seeds that were injected into the deepest part of his brain.

Li Jialing was more or less resistant at first. However, as Li Yao and Long Yangjun's brainwaves and vitality fields spread out incessantly, he finally couldn't resist it anymore and stepped into a new world led by the two seniors. His face was in a trance, but his eyes became focused.

The dozens of holograms seemed to have turned into brooks of light that flowed into their brains.

As he watched, Li Jialing suddenly grunted, with red blood flowing out of the corners of his mouth.

However, he felt none of it but was still attracted by Blackstar the Great who was playing with water in a swimsuit in the holograms.

"The kid can't deal with it any longer. He is already on the verge of mental derangement."

Narrowing her eyes, Long Yangjun gritted her teeth and struggled to say, "Li Yao, don't cross the line, in case the pictures are stamped on his brain and result in qualitative changes. In that case, those pictures will haunt him for the rest of his life. There's no way that he can forget them!"

"I-I know that."

Li Yao's facial muscles were twitching violently. Sweat that was almost crimson leaked out of his forehead, and his eyes were bloodshot as if they were going to explode in the next second. He managed to say, "To be honest, not just him, even a tough, iron-made man like me can't deal with it any longer either. The mission this time is truly troublesome. Even if Wuying Qi is indeed taken down, I'm afraid that we will not recover without a couple of years of rest.

"However, the 'mental shield' that we have forged at such a cost should be solid enough. Cancel! Let's cancel them!"

Focusing his determination, Li Yao turned off the dozens of pictures, allowing the three of them to regain their consciousness.

The three of them looked at each other in bewilderment. They were all soaked in sweat and exhausted.

Their brains, on the other hand, seemed to be heavily armored, with certain additional stuff.

"I…"

Li Jialing touched his head and said, "My head is rather dizzy. I don't feel very comfortable. Those holographic pictures seemed to have entered the deepest part of my soul. Is it necessary to bury the scenes so deeply?"

"It certainly is. Just now, Long Yangjun and I intentionally hypnotized each other and gave you a hint. Those scenes were essentially compressed and encapsulated into a 'secret weapon' that was buried deeply."

Li Yao explained, "We cannot think about it all the time. Otherwise, it will affect our combat ability. Also, those pictures are simply too absurd and contradict with reality. If you think about it all the time, you will realize subconsciously that it is false."

"Yes, this is a secret weapon that can only be used once. We cannot stimulate our brains with it all the time. It can only be used at the most critical moment to catch Wuying Qi unprepared."

Long Yangjun added, "In order to cover the secret weapon, we have implanted another scene on the external level of the brain, which is the idea that 'Wuying Qi is vicious and unpardonable' like you said just now. It is a 'fake defense' that is designed to be discovered and cracked by Wuying Qi. Let's hope that he will think that we have been completely hypnotized and controlled by him after he cracks the defense so that he will be less vigilant of us."

"Unless it is absolutely necessary, we must not let Wuying Qi know that we have done such a thing with his standard image."

Li Yao said solemnly, "Otherwise, I'm pretty sure that he will be so infuriated that his combat ability will soar by twenty times, allowing him to burn each and every one of our brain cells into ashes. In that case, we will be the victims of our own scheme.

"All in all, I have set up a psychological hint for myself. Whenever I sincerely adore and submit to Wuying Qi, shouting 'long live Wuying Qi, long live Blackstar the Great, and long live the Imperium of True Human Beings', a certain area in the deepest part of my brain will be automatically activated and play the pictures just now to my entire brain. I hope such intense stimulation can disrupt Wuying Qi's brainwashing.

"Alright, you must be getting back to yourselves now. Are you cool now? Especially you, Jialing, considering your rigorousness, is everything alright?"

Chapter 2608: The Real Power of the Gold Crystal Pyramid!

"No!"

Bulging his eyes, Li Jialing asked in fury, "Why were you so sure that there were such things in my portal crystal processor?"

"Jialing, you are a passionate, hotblooded young man in your twenties, and unlike Han Te, you don't have so many girls around you all the time. Isn't it perfectly normal that you have such things in your crystal processor?"

Li Yao patted the young man's shoulder considerately. "There's no need to feel embarrassed. Your Sister Long and I both went through the phase before. We understand you."

"Cut the crap!"

Li Jialing blew Li Yao's hand away. "I am a passionate, hotblooded young man, but you are a middle-aged uncle who is somewhere far away from your wife. Don't you have any of those things in your own portal crystal processor?"

"Like I said just now, with the level I am at right now, I don't need such vivid things anymore."

Li Yao said casually, "Nothing is everything, and everything is nothing. I know it is too sophisticated for you to understand right now, so let me translate it in a way that you can understand—I prefer to delete them after I watch them. Do you understand?"

"…"

Li Jialing massaged his neck and mumbled, "Are the three of us really qualified to save the civilization of mankind and even the Pangu universe?"

"Let's cut the crap and concentrate!"

Li Yao suddenly changed his facial expression and hinted to Li Jialing and Long Yangjun to be quiet. "The few remote crystal cameras that I sent into the heavy tractors have entered the underground hole along with the tractors. We'll see what Wuying Qi is up to in a minute!"

Li Jialing and Long Yangjun immediately dropped the minor episode a moment ago.

Fine. It was perhaps not a "minor episode", and it could not be completely dropped behind. All in all, they managed to focus 90% of their attention on the hologram that Li Yao displayed just now.

The hologram this time, however, was mottled and blurry. The details were obscure.

"So unclear?"

Li Jialing asked, "Is it possible to make it clearer?"

"No. Wuying Qi must've deployed certain defense rune arrays that can disrupt and block unusual waves in his secret base. Our signals have been terribly affected."

Li Yao said, "If I were to increase the power output by force to increase the clarity, we would likely be discovered by the defense rune arrays. So, let's deal with it!"

The three of them calmed down and observed, all shocked at the scale of the underground hole and the boundless darkness.

Even though they had foreseen that Wuying Qi would dig the underground base regardless of the cost, they did not see it coming that he would almost dig through the entire shell of the planet in only a couple of months.

When the crystal cameras were used to the new environment and sent back clearer pictures, a vague, magnificent building was gradually revealed.

It could be seen that Wuying Qi had asked the Imperial Guards to dig quite a few spiral channels on the cliff. Every channel was wide enough for two heavy tractors to pass at the same time. They extended all the way to the top of the cave.

Many heavy tractors unloaded the crystals on the top of the gigantic cave.

"Wuying Qi must've dug countless holes in the rocks above this gigantic cave and planted tremendous crystal bombs into them."

Li Yao analyzed, "When the time is ripe, the rocks between the cave and the ground will be blown up. Together with the carefully calculated tectonic movements, it will be indeed possible to lift the secret base up to the surface of the planet."

The few crystal cameras that arrived at the top of the cave with the tractors observed the general picture of the gigantic cave from the high stance, and they soon discovered an oval building with various floors. It was like a huge and abnormal iron heart. Countless tubes that were as thick as boas extended out of the "heart". Smoke was also popping up from the joints of the tubes and the building.

Many heavy tractors entered the building slowly.

"This… seems to be a massive energy center."

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao snapped his fingers. "Got it. The crystals that Wuying Qi has gathered by draining the whole capital planet are of different sources and qualities. They cannot be used directly. Therefore, they have to be shipped to this energy center to be purified and melted, before they are supplied to his brainwashing factory.

"Judging from the size of this energy center and the number of tractors that enter it every minute, the energy that it can steadily output is almost astronomical. Is Wuying Qi out of his mind to wager all his chances on a brainwashing factory below the ground?"

In the next moment, the crystal cameras scanned the platforms and the caves deployed on the cliff. The three of them all clicked their tongues when they saw the cubic defense system and the garrisons in the place.

"The defense here is almost flawless and impenetrable."

Li Yao vaguely felt a toothache. "How many soldiers did Wuying Qi summon to defend the underground base? An Imperial Guard is basically squatting on every stalagmite! It is needless to mention the dense crystal cannons. Even if we activate our Colossi and march over at the same time, we will only be blown back by the overwhelming firepower. The firepower density here is higher than that of an Adamantine Super Arsenal Warship!"

"It seems that Wuying Qi has almost given up the defense on the ground and the orbit. He's betting his future on this secret weapon."

Long Yangjun glanced at Li Yao and smiled. "Has Wuying Qi established any massive ventilation system that supports the oxygen of the Imperial Guards living in this place?"

"Forget it. There's no chance."

Li Yao sighed and said helplessly, "The plan to attack the Godly Might Prison was made by me and Li Linghai together. Li Linghai knew very well that there was nothing I could do about the ventilation tubes of the Godly Might Prison, so Wuying Qi must know it too. Do you think he will not adopt the same design?"

"It does make sense. It seems that we have to find a different way."

Long Yangjun said, "However, let's locate the brainwashing factory by the tubes that extend from the energy center first… Huh?"

As the few crystal cameras were turned to the deepest part of the cave, the glittering pyramid with esoteric runes carved on the surface was displayed in front of them.

It was glowing so splendidly that even the space around was illuminated clearly.

"What's this?"

Li Yao couldn't help but get hooked by the mysterious, vintage building that was filled with power. For some reason, the runes carved on it reminded him of billions of pieces of the sun. "Is that… the brainwashing factory. It seems entirely different from the 'human manufacture center' that we discovered in Kunlun."

"Judging from the structure, it looks like some sort of massive cannon."

Li Jialing also said, "The kind of cannon that is designed to penetrate through the atmosphere and deal with starships."

Long Yangjun, however, fell silent and was in a trance. The runes on the pyramid seemed to have been carved on her retina and then leaked into her soul.

"I… remember the building."

Her pupils and her eyes were melted together, turning her eyes into two glittering swirls. She said gloomily, "Or rather, my previous self saw similar magical equipment somewhere else and accepted the mission to destroy it!"

"Huh?"

Li Yao and Li Jialing looked at each other and hurried to ask, "What is that? A piece of brainwashing magical equipment to perform massive mental attacks?"

"No, no, it doesn't seem to be one. Let me think. Let me think carefully…"

Long Yangjun held her forehead in great pain. She breathed heavily, and hundreds of lights seemed to have travelled hundreds of thousands of years and glowed in her eyes, making her utter broken moans. "The Houyi Clan… the sun… the song of the stars, fire, the eternal fire that burns up a whole space zone… Gold… Gold Crystal Pyramid. Yes, I remember it now. This is a Gold Crystal Pyramid, a piece of super magical equipment that can boost the energy of the star for a battle. Its power can sweep across the whole space zone. It's an out-and-out stellar-level weapon!"

"A piece of stellar-level magical equipment?"

Li Yao and Li Jialing were both greatly alarmed.

However, they were rather puzzled too, because the magical equipment had nothing to do with brainwashing but was related to the stars, or more specifically, the sun.

"The Gold Crystal Pyramid was an invention of the Houyi Clan, a member of the Pangu civilizations' alliance. It can cast minor interferences to the magnetic field of a star and increase the star's activity, allowing the ultra-intense radiations, spiritual waves, particles, and heat to spread throughout the space zone. All the magical equipment in the range will be paralyzed. The Spiritual Nexus and the spiritual roots of the trainers will be affected. The carbon-based lives may even be wiped out directly. It is a rather dominating weapon."

The gate deep inside Long Yangjun's memories was opened, spurting out infinite information, but her face was filled with fear. "However, while the superweapon is very powerful, it has obvious shortcomings. First of all, the attack it launches aims at everybody in the range indiscriminately. It is barely possible for the allies to defend against it.

"In other words, once the Gold Crystal Pyramid ignites the fury of a star, it will usually lead to a lose-lose outcome. After a solar storm, it is possible that half of the troops of both sides, as well as the civilians living in the stellar system, will be killed instantly. It will be a war that has no winners.

"What is more dreadful is that such a superweapon has a terrible controllability. It is easy to start a fire, but it is not as easy to distinguish one. While infuriating a star was not difficult, restoring the calmness of the star was beyond the technological limits of the Pangu civilization.

"Once a certain star enters the intensely active period because of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, it may last dozens if not hundreds of years. During the hundreds of years, the star will spurt powerful radiations and high-energy particles incessantly, reducing the previously vigorous Sector into a burning inferno where no intelligent lives can be found. Even the Houyi Clan and the Kuafu Clan who worshipped the sun couldn't have survived in such a rigorous environment!

"Do you understand it now? Using the Gold Crystal Pyramid once may be enough to destroy one Sector. At least within several hundred years, the Sector will not be able to be used by any civilization again!"

Chapter 2609: Politics in the Primeval Age

Li Yao had never seen such a look on Long Yangjun's face before.

It was even hard for him to imagine that Long Yangjun, reincarnated from the primeval age with the heritages of both Pangu and Nuwa, could look as dreadful as now.

"Is it really so horrible?"

Influenced by Long Yangjun, Li Yao grew anxious too. "The superweapon is really so intimidating?"

"It is not the Gold Crystal Pyramid that is intimidating, but the star. Neither the civilization of mankind nor the Pangu civilization has figured out 1% of the mysteries of stars. Infuriating a sun recklessly under such circumstances is exactly like playing with fire. You will only get burnt!"

Taking a deep breath, Long Yangjun said in a shivering voice, "When a three-year-old throws a pebble into a pond, it is always easy to raise a few ripples, but can he control the number of the ripples he raised or the spreading speed and distance of the ripples? It's absolutely impossible, right?

"Now, we are not talking about a 'pond', but the most violent pool of magma!

"The mechanism of the Gold Crystal Pyramid equals tossing a 'pebble' into the sun to raise the ripples. Once a chain reaction is triggered, the destructive waves will spread out incessantly. There will be no stopping it!

"Don't think that it is a long period when I say that the active period of a star may last several hundred years. It must be noted that every star has a life of tens of billions of years. From the perspective of a star, extending the active period from several months to several hundred years, and increasing the power output in the meantime by one percent, are just insignificant, unperceivable changes.

"However, for a civilization that lives and prospers based on the star, such 'insignificant changes' will be a catastrophe!

"When the radiation of a star is increased by one percent—no, even if only one thousandth—the ocean on the habitable planets will be vaporized, the atmosphere will be gone, the percentage of oxygen and carbon dioxide will change drastically, the magnetic fields will be disrupted, the ecological circles will be destroyed, and billions of creatures will die in the most miserable ways!"

Li Yao was creeped out, not by what Long Yangjun said but by her freezing tone.

After a long daze, he raised a meaningless question. "You said just now that the Gold Crystal Pyramid was built by the Houyi Clan? Why would the Houyi Clan build such a dreadful thing that may likely destroy themselves?"

"Don't human beings have a lot of super magical equipment that can destroy themselves too?"

Long Yangjun smiled. "It seems to be a common disease for all carbon-based intelligent lives. They all have the tendencies to self-destruct. Do you know how the Pangu civilizations' alliance defined 'civilization' and accepted new members?

"In the eyes of the Pangu Clan, only when a carbon-based intelligent life created a weapon that could destroy themselves could they be called a 'civilization'."

Li Yao was silent.

He was going to argue that such a definition was ridiculous, but on second thought, he realized that it was very reasonable.

Weapons were the best essence of the abilities of a lifeform in addition to wisdom, physical strength, logical thinking, social division, etc.

If an intelligent life created a weapon that could destroy themselves but was not destroyed by such a weapon in the next hundreds of years, it would suggest that they had contained their tendencies to self-destruct, and that they were a mature, powerful civilization.

"At first, the Houyi Clan did not expect that the Gold Crystal Pyramid could be so powerful. Or rather, the specialists of the Houyi Clan were so arrogant that they thought they had completely grasped a 'simple and clear energy model', which allowed them to control every sun 100%."

Long Yangjun sighed and said, "Therefore, after the primeval war began, the Houyi Clan built a lot of Gold Crystal Pyramids in the three thousand Sectors.

"Of course, it remains a mystery whether or not the Houyi Clan hoped to expand their influence in the alliance with the super weapon that was the Gold Crystal Pyramid, or even to replace the Pangu Clan as the leader of the alliance.

"The Houyi Clan was best known for their craziness, toughness, fearlessness, and recklessness in the Pangu civilizations' alliance. Their origin was a life that was similar to moths but had a shell that could absorb solar energy. 'Moths darting at the fire' was an idea that was written in their genes.

"It was said that a long, long time ago, before the Pangu civilizations' alliance was established, the Houyi Clan and the Kuafu Clan nearly died with each other in competition for several steady stars. The Houyi Clan would rather destroy the stars, which were extremely precious for themselves, than give them away to the Kuafu Clan. It was indicative enough of the Houyi Clan's bloodthirst and madness.

"Later, the Pangu Clan arrived. With a civilization thousands of years more advanced, they forced the Houyi Clan to surrender and accept the mediation however reluctantly. The Houyi Clan were in fact tamed and diminished by the Pangu Clan.

"Therefore, the Houyi Clan had been strongly biased against the Kuafu Clan and dissatisfied with the Pangu Clan. They jumped out now and then to challenge the authority of the Pangu Clan after the establishment. Of course, those are just tales.

"All in all, when the Gold Crystal Pyramids were activated in the space battlefield and the fire of destruction from the stars savaged countless Sectors, both parties involved in the war were deeply awed by the horror of the super weapon.

"The purpose of a war is to loot resources and bend the enemy.

"However, the Gold Crystal Pyramid destroyed all the resources in a Sector and blew friends and foes alike into smithereens, wasting all the value of a Sector for the next hundreds of years. Could such a weapon help anything, however 'super' it was?

"That was still the early stage of the primeval war. Compared with the lone Nuwa Clan, the other twelve clans in the Pangu civilizations' alliance had obvious advantages. There was no need to resort to such a horrible weapon.

"Besides, the superweapon was too destructive for the ideology of the Pangu civilizations' alliance. As we all know, it was the purpose of the Pangu civilizations' alliance to make use of every drop of resources reasonably, and to make the carbon-based intelligent lives live in this tiny universe as long as possible.

"However, the Gold Crystal Pyramid could destroy a whole Sector whenever it was activated, obliterating astronomical resources. Wouldn't it be the opposite of the Pangu civilization's purpose?

"Therefore, until the middle phase of the primeval war, most of the Gold Crystal Pyramids were either destroyed by the Nuwa Clan or dismantled by the Pangu civilizations' alliance itself. A great purge targeting the zealots of the Houyi Clan was performed too. Those guys who created the Gold Crystal Pyramids and who intended to use the Gold Crystal Pyramids to increase the Houyi Clan's influence were all killed.

"There wasn't a choice. For the Pangu Clan, such a superweapon was as great a threat as the 'feelings' or 'tenebrum energy' brought by the Nuwa Clan. How could they tolerate it?

"All in all, by the end of the primeval war, when my previous self lived and fought, few Gold Crystal Pyramids were left in the three thousand Sectors. My previous self once received a mission to destroy a Gold Crystal Pyramid. According to the intelligence, it was 'one of the few last Gold Crystal Pyramids' in the Pangu civilizations' alliance. However, after we reached the battlefield, we discovered that the Gold Crystal Pyramid was just an empty shell. All the internal facilities had been removed by the Pangu Clan.

"I did not expect that a Gold Crystal Pyramid was buried so deeply below the capital planet. Also, it seems rather intact and is running at full power.

"Did it slowly sink during the hundreds of thousands of years of tectonic movements? That's not right. Judging from the structure of the cave, this Gold Crystal Pyramid did not seem to have sunk naturally. It's more like somebody intentionally buried it here. What happened exactly? The Gold Crystal Pyramid can only be activated when it touches the rays of the sun directly!"

Frowning, Long Yangjun considered the reason.

"Although there is no evidence, allow me to take a wild guess based on what you said just now."

Li Yao said, "You mentioned just now that the Houyi Clan was a tough and even insane people in the Pangu civilizations' alliance, who tried to replace the Pangu Clan's leadership all the time. That's why part of the zealots of the Houyi Clan created the Gold Crystal Pyramids. The weapon was meant to deal with the Nuwa Clan on the surface, but it undoubtedly could've been aimed at the Pangu Clan too

"After realizing that, because they feared the power of the Gold Crystal Pyramids, or because they were wary of the ambitions of the Houyi Clan, the Pangu Clan destroyed most of the Gold Crystal Pyramids as well as the ambitions of the zealots of the Houyi Clan.

"It's very easy to understand what happened next. The zealots of the Houyi Clan, in order to protect their secret weapon, had to bury their last few Gold Crystal Pyramids carefully so that they could hide them from the Pangu Clan. Those weapons were the greatest leverage for them to rise again and deal with the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan!

"The Empyreal Terminus Sector is an intersection in the cosmos that is brimming with wormholes. It was of great military and political significance even in the primeval war. It was not strange that the zealots of the Houyi Clan built and buried a Gold Crystal Pyramid in this place. If I were one of them, I would've chosen this place too."

"If that is the case—"

Greatly alarmed, Long Yangjun said, "There must be some sort of 'self-lifting' system below the Gold Crystal Pyramid to ensure that it can rip apart the shell, rise on the surface of the planet, and infuriate the sun when it is necessary!"

"That's right. If this Gold Crystal Pyramid was truly the last leverage for the zealots of the Houyi Clan's wild gamble, it must be inestimably powerful."

Li Yao said gloomily, "Then, it is obvious why the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God fought below the capital planet over the Gold Crystal Pyramid ten thousand years ago. Perhaps the Supreme Emperor intended to destroy the Gold Crystal Pyramid whereas the Blood God tried to destroy the Star Ocean Imperium with the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

"However, where exactly did Wuying Qi learn the information about the Gold Crystal Pyramid, allowing him to excavate it so accurately? What is on his mind? Does he really know what kind of terrible destruction the Gold Crystal Pyramid could bring? Or he simply doesn't care even if he knows?"

Chapter 2610: Approaching the Truth!

"Sister Long—"

Li Jialing, who had been listening quietly the whole time, suddenly interjected and asked, "The types of damage you just described were too abstract. Can you describe it in detail, about the influences the Gold Crystal Pyramid will cause to the Empyreal Terminus Sector after it is activated?"

"About that…"

Thinking for a moment, Long Yangjun said, "The Gold Crystal Pyramid's interferences on the star have different levels. Depending on the power output, the consequences may be vastly different."

"Then, assuming that the power output is the highest."

Li Jialing opened his arms and made a gesture of hugging. "Assuming that Wuying Qi collected all the crystals on the entire Empyreal Terminus Sector and compressed the crystals into the purest energy to power the Gold Crystal Pyramid to shoot at the sun!"

"If the level of this Gold Crystal Pyramid is high enough and it has not been destroyed by the passage of time, if Wuying Qi grasps all the ways to control it, and if he has accurately calculated the cycle of activity and the energy model of the main star of the Empyreal Terminus Sector… All in all, if all the conditions are met—"

Long Yangjun swallowed hard and said, one word after another, "It will be possible for him to raise the energy, radiations, and high-energy particles that the sun releases by 1% to 5%. An overwhelming solar storm that sweeps across the whole space zone will be caused.

"Nothing—not the spiritual shield of starships, the alloy shells, or the atmosphere of the capital planet—can completely resist the scourge of the solar storm. People who are exposed to the powerful sun will suffer radiation thousands of times more than what a human body could accept. Their blood will be boiled, and their viscera will be withered. They will be as dead as can be. Not the slightest of their souls will remain after the storm.

"That, however, is almost the most merciful way of death.

"If the radiation is slightly lower and only tore apart the gene strands deep inside the soul, the victims will not be killed immediately. Instead, their cells will wither and their flesh will rot in the next few months. It's like their physical bodies have died but their souls are still trapped in the putrid cage. They will watch their bodies turning into a bloody mud but can do nothing about it. Trust me. It will be the worst way to die in the entire universe."

After a brief silence, Li Jialing asked, "How many people will suffer the disaster?"

"All of them. Did you not hear what I said just now? The shell of starships, the atmosphere, or even hiding on the back of a planet—nothing can completely resist the solar storm. Some people will be killed by the solar storm on the spot, and others will suffer from the radiation in the next few weeks and months and rot because their cells lose the function of self-renewal. The few 'lucky dogs' will bear insufferable pain for the rest of their lives."

Long Yangjun sounded extremely cruel as she said, "Among them, the Immortal Cultivators whose bodies are tough and whose cells can recover quickly have a better chance of survival. However, for the ordinary people who take up more than 95% of the population, there is no escape for them. None at all!

"Besides, mankind is highly dependent on a social system today, which can be quite vulnerable. The solar storm will destroy everything that a modern civilization lives on. How are the hundred billion people on the capital planet going to survive in the doomsday when there is no energy, no order, and no resources?

"Even the Imperium of True Human Beings in its heyday couldn't evacuate a hundred billion people from one Sector to a different one in only several weeks, not to mention that the Imperium is in the middle of a civil war right now. Who cares about the lives of those people except for you two fools?

"Right. What I talked about was only the imminent influence. Nobody knows the consequences of the Gold Crystal Pyramid's stimulation on the star. Perhaps the active period of the star will be extended to decades. In that case, the whole Empyreal Terminus Sector will be completely obliterated!"

Li Yao and Li Jialing were silent again.

No words could describe their shock and fury at this moment.

"What about the lower boundary?"

Li Jialing asked in a hoarse voice, "Sister Long said just now that it was the maximal damage. I want to know what the minimal damage will be like."

"I'm very suspicious whether Wuying Qi could control the damage of the Gold Crystal Pyramid accurately, considering that he is just an amateur. He is just like a three-year-old wielding a chainsword that he does not understand at all. God knows what he will chop apart."

Long Yangjun said, "Common military theories hold that a troop will collapse after 10% of the soldiers are lost. If it is a battle-tested elite troop, the threshold can be improved to 30%. Assuming that Wuying Qi's purpose is to raise a solar storm with the Gold Crystal Pyramid and deprive the four families' ace fleets of 30% of their combat abilities, the 'collateral damage' that the solar storm will cause to the capital planet will be no lower than the number. 30% of the population, or specifically, thirty billion people, will die.

"That is not counting the population that would plummet as a result of the natural disasters, social chaos, famines, and plagues that would happen after the battle."

"It means that thirty billion people will die at the very least?"

Li Jialing asked gloomily, "And most of the seventy billion survivors will suffer radiation diseases for the rest of their lives?"

"Yes. That is the most understated power. Otherwise, it will be meaningless for Wuying Qi to activate the Gold Crystal Pyramid through all the trouble!"

Pondering for a moment, Long Yangjun suddenly changed her expression greatly. "Right, there may be another 'collateral loss', but I'm not sure whether it will happen. It's about the person who controls the Gold Crystal Pyramid."

Li Jialing was slightly stunned. "What do you mean?"

"The Gold Crystal Pyramid is a piece of stellar-level magical equipment, whose power almost touches the limits of the Pangu civilizations' alliance. Naturally, launching such magical equipment comes with a great cost."

Long Yangjun said, "Also, such superweapons were designed by and for the Houyi Clan, which was one of the races that were most different from human beings in the Pangu civilizations' alliance.

"While abundant genetic fragments of the Houyi Clan have been embedded into human beings, the disparities between them are still too huge.

"If a human being intends to activate the super magical equipment built by the Houyi Clan, don't you think that he will have to pay a high price?"

Li Jialing and Li Yao looked at each other and nodded at the same time.

Li Jialing said, "There are no techniques in the universe that can be activated without a price. In order to infuriate the sun, it will be quite normal if the performer has to be sacrificed. What's next?"

"Do you think that Wuying Qi will ask somebody else to control such a piece of critical magical equipment? He will definitely do it himself!"

Long Yangjun said coldly, "But don't forget that he is now residing inside your mother's body. If there are indeed severe consequences such as excessive exposure to radiation, it will be Li Linghai who actually shoulders it, right?

"Huh, I seem to realize why Wuying Qi needed to develop you as a 'perfect carrier'. He had seen it coming that his old shell would be broken and shriveled during the activation of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Therefore, he carefully selected and developed a life seed decades ago, planning to use it as his new body.

"Of course, Li Linghai's love for you, Li Lingfeng's ambitions, and Li Yao's arrival might've been beyond Wuying Qi's expectations, and he failed to capture you. However, it is not important. As long as his plan works, Li Linghai's body will not necessarily decay in a couple of days. He will still have plenty of time to catch you, or to change to a not-so-perfect carrier if he can't find you."

"A*shole…"

The young lion's eyes were redder than the infuriated sun. He clenched his fists so hard that his bones were almost broken. "I'm going to stop you! I'm going to crunch your soul bit by bit, Wuying Qi!"

"I still don't think it's as simple as you said."

Thinking for a long time, Li Yao said cautiously, "Your speculation only takes care of the four families' ace fleets that come at the capital, but the price is that Wuying Qi's nest will be almost entirely ruined. How is he going to deal with the remaining forces of the four families?

"Therefore, Wuying Qi must have deeper plans. We have to get closer… Wait, look. Another tractor approached the Gold Crystal Pyramid. It seems to be unloading people!

"They look very familiar. I must have the files of those people in my memories. They should be the celebrities of the Imperium. What is happening?"

"They are Meditation Healers!"

Long Yangjun found their files in her memories. "The one in the lead is 'Illusionary Eye' Wu Tian, a famous expert in the circle of the Meditation Healers. He is almost as famous as Wuying Qinxin."

"Exactly. Now that you mention it, I remember them now. Many of them have the tattoos of a brain made of vipers on the back of their hands. It is a symbol of the 'Super Brain Association', a famous organization that comprises Meditation Healers. Those people are all the members of the Super Brain Association and the best experts at mental warfare!

"So many Meditation Healers, cerebral specialists, and experts in mental warfare have been captured by Wuying Qi and shipped to the Gold Crystal Pyramid in secret. Why? Does the activation of the Gold Crystal Pyramid require the sacrifice of Meditation Healers?"

"I don't think so."

Long Yangjun replied suspiciously, "The Gold Crystal Pyramid is supposed to launch a powerful wave to interfere with the star. It has nothing to do with… Meditation Healers!"

"However, it seems that they must be connected in a certain way."

Li Yao held his breath and narrowed his eyes, allowing the glittering pieces that were scattered in his head to be joined.

The most advanced brainwashing technology, the enhancement and magnification of brainwaves, the gathering of Meditation Healers, the brainwashing attack that Wuying Qi launched by blowing up Wuying Qinxin's head, the Gold Crystal Pyramid that could disrupt the star by releasing waves, Wuying Qi's trump card, a superweapon that could win the war in a battle… What was it exactly?

"I suddenly have an extremely ominous feeling."

Li Yao shuddered hard and spoke in a chilling voice.

"Long Yangjun, you mentioned just now that the mechanism of the Gold Crystal Pyramid is to send a stream of ultra-powerful energy to interfere with the sun. The sun will be used as some sort of magnifier and energy source, improving the waves by hundreds and thousands of times before spreading them out, right?"

"Yes."

Long Yangjun said, "Why do you ask?"

"Nothing."

His face gloomy, Li Yao said, one word after another, "I only want to know one thing. Is 'brainwave' some kind of wave, too?"

Chapter 2611: Die to Live!

Hearing Li Yao's freezing voice, Long Yangjun and Li Jialing both looked as if they were just woken up from a nightmare, only to fall into another one that was even more horrible.

The three of them looked at each other in thoughtfulness.

"Are you saying—" Long Yangjun struggled to say, "—what I'm thinking?"

"In order to perform massive brainwashing on billions of people in a whole space zone, a most powerful energy source and a 'magnifier' or 'enhancer' with a high performance are required."

Li Jialing also said in a shivering voice, "We thought that it was impossible for Wuying Qi to get those things, but from what we have seen just now, he is much crazier and more talented than we imagined. He… He can regard the sun as an 'energy source' and 'magnifier'. No expert can possibly compete with an exasperated sun!

"Is it technologically possible?"

Long Yangjun was more or less overwhelmed. "I don't know. I don't know."

"Nobody does. That's why I have to get in."

Li Yao pointed at the Gold Crystal Pyramid. With a pair of eyes as sharp as those of an eagle, he said, "I have to get into this pyramid to observe its mechanism, collect more concrete proofs, send them to more people, and figure out a way to resolve it."

"Are you out of your mind? There are so many people around the Gold Crystal Pyramid, as well as many Imperial Guards who are armed to teeth and who are utterly fearless of death thanks to being brainwashed!"

Long Yangjun bellowed, "Open your eyes and watch! This is a narrow cave below the ground. The speed and agility of the Colossi are highly limited! In the boundless space, you are free to divide and conquer through guerilla warfare as fast as lightning, but in this place, when the iron tide is made of a thousand soldiers who were flooding forward, you will have nowhere to run to!

"It is needless to say that Wuying Qi may show up at any moment. You wouldn't think that you are capable of challenging Wuying Qi because you have touched the ceiling of the Divinity Transformation Stage after Xiaoming and Wenwen's modification, would you?"

"I'm not out of my mind. On the contrary, I have never been more conscious!"

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao pressed his forehead and stimulated his brain cells to jump crazily. "Listen, the most dangerous place is the safest place. One can only live if they are ready for death! We are beleaguered by Wuying Qi's army in the first place after sneaking into the capital planet in secret. It's difficult to sneak away now. Our only opportunity is to break in and hijack the 'Gold Crystal Pyramid'!

"The Gold Crystal Pyramid will be the best 'hostage' and the greatest bargaining chip for us to deal with Wuying Qi. Also, judging from the solid shell of the stellar-level magical equipment, it is impossible to destroy it from the outside. Even if we are equipped with Colossi, we can't shoot through the shell. Then, the only way to stop Wuying Qi's scheme is to dig into the pyramid, find the most critical core, and blow up its heart!"

Long Yangjun and Li Jialing looked at each other. They were about to persuade Li Yao to try other approaches when Li Yao's portable crystal processor suddenly shivered.

Li Yao raised his wrist, only to look even more awful.

"Many people just came from the teleportation array at the end of the tunnel. No, not the heavy tractors that ship crystals, but the abundant Exos as well as fully-armed crystal tanks and battle puppets. Judging from their emblems and epaulets, they are the best troop in the Imperial Guards. It is possible that Wuying Qi is leading them in person.

"It appears that he has learned that we snuck into the base, and he is about to activate his… crazy plan of brainwashing with the sun in advance!

"There's no time for any hesitation. Just listen to me on this one. Trust me, no matter how idiotic I usually pretended to be, I never let you down at critical moments, did I? So, trust me. We will succeed! We will!"

Looking at Li Yao's eyes, which seemed to be spurting magma, Long Yangjun sighed and said helplessly, "How are you going in?"

"There's no time for a prudent sneak-in."

Li Yao's chest moved fast. "Let's resort to the emergency plan!"

Long Yangjun's eyelids twitched fast. She bit her lips hard and said, "I swear that this is the last time that I will ever team up with you. The last time!"

"If we fail, there won't be a next time."

Li Yao chuckled and turned to Li Jialing. He hesitated for a moment but still asked, "Jialing, you are the target that Wuying Qi is determined to capture. Regarding the plan we discussed last time, are you… ready?"

Li Jialing nodded in silence and declared resolutely, "I will figure out the queen's real stance. If she did have understandable reasons, or if she was completely controlled by Wuying Qi, I will return my life to her by dying together with Wuying Qi!"
