"Only by injecting abundant knowledge, mutual memories, perfect morals, and the will of the leader into billions of brains directly can the real and perfect new human beings be born—the golden humans who have the wisdom, courage, and strength to march into the grand universe!

"Only such flawless true human beings can allow for a technological boom under the boost of unified thoughts and highly-efficient interaction. It will allow us to digest and absorb the precious legacies left by the primeval civilizations in only several hundred years and make us a real grand-universe civilization!

"Li Yao, for someone with your Cultivation, you are essentially standing on the peak of the evolution of mankind, only second to few people. It will be a crime if you cling to the obsolete ideas and morals to restrain and even strike the propagation of new technologies; it will be the greatest crime against mankind.

"We are running out of time. Really. The civilization of mankind does not have any time now. We have to seize every second to break out of this place and at least take a look at what it looks like outside in order to decide the direction of development of our civilization in the next million years. In order to achieve that, we will need a powerful, united, loyal, and devoted Imperium. Only the technologies of enlightenment can possibly create such a new Imperium. So, come on, stand on my side just like you used to stand on Li Linghai's side. Build a new Imperium and create new human beings together with me so that our civilization can climb to the summit of the universe!"

Chapter 2619: Soar From the Shoulders of Giants!

The black hair that spread out of the back of Wuying Qi's head—or rather, the nerve clusters that had directly grown out of the depths of his soul—expanded abruptly by several times.

A dark force was injected into Li Yao's soul incessantly, covering his whole body with black stripes and spots.

The dark area proliferated nonstop, enshrouding Li Yao completely like some sort of glittering gelatin and dragging him into a bottomless abyss.

Li Yao struggled and resisted hard. The fire of his soul jumped out of the dark gap and burnt up the black nerves that connected him to Wuying Qi.

"Your speech is convincing and your slogan is motivating, and the dilemma you proposed is too hard for me to answer immediately."

Gritting his teeth, Li Yao said, one word after another, "But no matter how fancily you talk about it, you cannot change the fact that the Pangu civilization, which was able to enlighten another civilization instantly with your so-called 'most advanced information interaction technologies', perished in the end!

"Hehe, I don't believe that the Pangu Clan was as 'peace-loving and kind-hearted' as you described or that it was willing to help other civilizations to survive for no reason. Perhaps it was by way of brainwashing that the Pangu Clan imprinted the idea that the 'Pangu Clan is our supreme leader' into the heads of other carbon-based lives. That's how they established a 'Pangu civilizations' alliance' with themselves as the center. A country that was built through half coercion and half deception, however, had hidden problems. As time went by, the hidden problems grew larger and larger. Both the Nuwa Clan and the Houyi Clan realized part of the truth later. Perhaps that was the reason for the eventual division of the Pangu civilizations' alliance and the trigger of the primeval war!

"Since the Pangu civilizations' alliance failed to walk out by way of brainwashing, why do you think we can? Why wouldn't we repeat the same mistake that they made?"

"You are very smart, but too paranoid. Even though the Pangu Clan had other motives, it had nothing to do with the enlightenment technology itself. Technologies are neither good nor bad by themselves. It's like how a knife cannot kill anybody without being wielded. Whether it is good or bad depends on how people use it."

Wuying Qi seemed to have considered the problem a long time ago. Not shaken by Li Yao at all, he said casually, "When a new technology is just born, they will inevitably head toward the wrong direction more or less. How can you deny the value of a new technology just because of certain immature parts?

"As for the destruction of the Pangu civilizations' alliance, we should view the incident critically. We need to absorb the experience of the Pangu Clan, carrying on their shining parts and dropping their flaws, in order to achieve greater success on their shoulders.

"As we look to the future, we can learn from our past. Although the civilization of mankind today is far weaker than the Pangu civilization in the past, we have the greatest advantage, which is that we are on the shoulder of giants, and we do not need to walk on the paths that have been proved to be dead ends by them.

"What are 'dead ends'? The simplest example is the enlightenment technology that the Pangu Clan invented. However, they probably had a lot of stubborn people who were terrified by the power of the new technology just like you. They did not develop the technology to the highest level and merely used it for the simple communication among the different carbon-based lives at the beginning. Later, they used it to wash away the feelings, emotions, and will of the people, turning the people into a blank paper without injecting anything in.

"They did that only because they were horrified by the universe beyond the universe. They lost the ruthlessness for evolution. They had no idea what to inject into the head.

"A person without bones will be a pile of mud, and a civilization without spirits is destined to die! If the Pangu Clan did not occupy the head, the extraterrestrial devils certainly would. How could it be kept blank all the time?

"Do you understand it now? The enlightenment technology was not wrong by itself. The mistake was that the Pangu Clan only carried out half of it. They merely 'cleaned' the wrong, complicated, messy thoughts and desires of the people, but they did not inject the new, healthy, positive thoughts and desires. The glorious and brilliant carbon-based lives in the past had all been transformed into zombies. How could they survive?

"The Covenant Alliance is the same. They found the most valuable treasure in the entire universe, and they had the technology that could change the world, but they are completely bound by the Path of Ultimate Benevolence. They merely injected the admiration for the Pangu Clan into their heads and the fear for the beyond of the boundless cosmos. They are just a duplicate Pangu Clan of a smaller size, and they are destined to perish!

"However, my enlightenment will not stop halfway like them. Not only will I wash off the selfishness, greediness, shortsightedness, cowardness, and all the other unhealthy thoughts in the traitors, but more importantly, I will also inject bright, glittering pure ideas and help them forge the real beliefs so that they can be real men, great warriors, and pure Immortal Cultivators!"

Li Yao's voice shivered. "Do you expect me to believe that?"

"Why not? Imagine such a beautiful future—"

Wuying Qi narrowed his eyes and waved his hands. Vivid holograms surfaced from all directions again. Inside every picture, countless Immortal Cultivators who seemed warm, enthusiastic, tall, and handsome were either dedicated to their studies or work wholeheartedly or fighting in the space battlefields fearlessly, except that their enemies were not human beings but some hideous alien species that Li Yao had never met before. The pictures seemed to be suggesting that they had already left the tiny swamp that was the Pangu universe and was fighting for a vaster space of survival in the grand universe.

"I will not, nor is it necessary for me to, deprive everybody of their thinking ability and your so-called 'free will'. Really, 95% of the parts inside your brain will not be changed by me. I will only perform a minor, non-invasive 'mind surgery' on the 5% of evilness, removing the dark and selfish parts and replacing them with the noble, selfless, and righteous ideas. They will be deeply carved on the brains and the souls and will never be forgotten."

Wuying Qi elaborated, "After such a minor mind surgery, the Immortal Cultivators will retain their previous memories and self-awareness, but they won't be as selfish, shortsighted, cowardly, and dark as before. Instead, they will be able to summon all their courage and loyalty and devote themselves to the entire civilization. They will forever abide by the rules that I have established for the Imperium. Any dark thought that occurs to them will be destroyed by themselves. Even when they are faced with the indescribable fear beyond the universe, they will still be able to summon their courage and fight till the end!

"How much can be changed depends on how corrupted they are. The more corrupted they are, the more they will be changed. For the people who have firm beliefs and great ambitions like you and General Lei Chenghu, you won't even feel the slightest change!

"You don't believe me? That's alright. I am not a monarch of the old days who decides everything by myself. In the future, we may even establish an 'Enlightenment Committee'. We will gather the knowledgeable people in all industries to discuss the main ideas that are to be inculcated into the heads of the citizens this year. It will be like the Education Committee and the teams of editors for textbooks today. When that day comes, you can be one of the supervisors of this committee and monitor the thoughts that are to be poured into the heads of the citizens in person.

"Of course, we're in the middle of an emergency right now. Unusual circumstances call for unusual methods. There is no time for any democracy or supervision right now. I believe you can certainly understand that, right, Cultivator Li Yao?"

"The Enlightenment Committee?"

Li Yao almost bit his own tongue off. "Are you kidding me?"

"An emperor does not joke. Do you think that I have the time to joke with you? You are underestimating my vision, Li Yao. After learning the vastness of the grand universe and the dangers in it, the competition between the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators is already meaningless in my eyes. It is just a minor conflict among a bunch of kids."

Wuying Qi sighed. "Villains are everywhere, but frank advisors are hard to come by. A thousand years ago, I was surrounded by villains. In my carelessness, I felt too good about myself under their adulation and did not carry on the reform to the end, which turned out to be a huge mistake. Those villains also grew into the behemoths like the four Kurfürst families. A thousand years later, now that I've come back again, I've decided that I will not make the same mistake. I will not keep any of those villains. What I want is honest advisors like you. Li Yao, you can totally keep your beliefs as a Cultivator. You can stay by my side as a mirror so that I can know myself better.

"Furthermore, do I really have any reason to destroy your country of Cultivators? The deepest contradiction between the Immortal Cultivators and the Cultivators, regarding the treatment for the ordinary people, is resulted from the shortage of resources and the population boom, isn't it?

"As long as the shortage of resources and the increase of population are not resolved, the conflict will always exist, and there will always be Cultivators and Immortal Cultivators. Neither of us can change that.

"However, if my plan works out and we do jump out, we may find ten times and even a hundred times more resources than we have right now in the grand universe. By then, we will have copious materials and mental satisfaction that the contradictions between the Immortal Cultivators and the ordinary people will be gone. The ordinary people that you can never forget will all live a life of the deities. All of them can be given a planet each where they can be kings and queens. Then, is there any need for the Immortal Cultivators to exploit the ordinary people? Do we still have any contradictions? No, none whatsoever!

"So, if you are a real Cultivator and you are considering the happiness and future of humanity, you will have fewer reasons to add to my trouble at such a critical moment, in which case we will both suffer heavy losses, and the villains and brainless puppets will take the advantage! You should stand on my side unwaveringly! When we join our hands, we will be able to rage in the entire universe!"

Chapter 2620: True Love Is Boundless!

Wuying Qi's thoughts were truly turning into a whole sky of brilliant stars, and the stars were corrupted into the color of black instantly by his ambitions before they flooded into Li Yao's soul nonstop.

As if hit by lightning and drowned in an abyss, Li Yao struggled to put up the final resistance. "But… But your plan will kill a lot of people. In the Empyreal Terminus Sector alone, thirty to fifty billion people will die. What about them?"

"They are the slits during the operation, the throes when a new life is born, the fuel to boost the civilization of mankind forward, and a price that we have to pay."

Wuying Qi's eyes became profound and antique, as if he could see the changes in billions of years. He said in a completely inhumane tone, "We are all going to die in the long run. Not just us, even the ultimate experts in the thirteen carbon-based clans that made up the Pangu civilizations' alliance. No, not just Pangu, all the formidable civilizations that once existed in this universe during the tens of billions of years before Pangu have all been swallowed by time. There is absolutely nothing left now.

"In the past billions of years, hundreds of the most prosperous primordial civilizations perished, and the civilization of mankind will repeat their course soon. In such a magnificent background, does the life or death of a few individuals really matter?

"I can tell that you also believe we cannot wait and be killed but have to march out in search for a way of survival. Then, have you considered how dangerous the universe out there is? Has it occurred to you that, even though we may reach a final destination, allowing the civilization of mankind to reach a certain…'glorious eternity', the journey will still be such an arduous march that perhaps 99% of the people will die on the way and only 1% of them will be the ancestors of new human beings?

"It can't be helped. Even though we know for sure that those people will be sacrificed, we have to go forward relentlessly, because that is the significance of life.

"Billions of years ago, when the first fish crawled onto the continent clumsily, I believe that it was not unique but had many companions. However, 99% of the other fish died of drought in the lethal air.

"By the same logic, when the ancient apes reached the plains from the forests timidly, they must've paid tremendous sacrifices under the claws and tusks of the ferocious beasts on the plains too.

"If the fish and the apes were intelligent, and if you were the commander of the fish and the apes, would you tell your partners that it is too dangerous up ahead and that you should return to your warm and safe nest because you are reluctant to pay the 'minor sacrifices'? Yes, your partners may be kept safe for now, but the future of the civilization will be completely obliterated by your petty kindness!

"Trust me, if possible, I want to reduce the casualties as much as possible, but this is a war. It is not just a war between the reformists and the nobles, or one between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance, much less one between the Immortal Cultivators and the Cultivators, but a war between new human beings and old human beings. It is a war that will change the world! Such a conflict between the old and the new can't be gentle, harmless, and gradual as you imagined. It is destined to raise the most intense contradictions. Too many obsolete interest groups will be crumbled. Many people will die.

"The virtue of a general is not to keep his every soldier safe but to try to make every soldier die a worthwhile death so that their lives can be traded for the greatest results and the eventual victory. In many cases, even if the general knows that the troop leading the charge or covering the retreat will be wiped out, he will still have to ruthlessly send his most beloved and most elite troop into inevitable doom. Is the general an unpardonable murderer? No, it is the responsibility of a commander!"

Li Yao couldn't hold it anymore and roared, "Soldiers are all braced for death, but what you are going to kill are not soldiers but civilians!"

"Wrong! The classification between 'soldiers' and 'civilians' only applies for the civil wars of mankind. In the wars in the grand universe that we will soon face, in the infinite battles of infinite civilizations, everybody will be a warrior who has to devote their everything to the whole civilization!"

Wuying Qi's voice also became aggressive. "Think about it. If one day we truly march out of the grand universe, only to be faced with the full scourge of a certain alien civilization, would it tell the difference between 'soldiers' and 'civilians' within mankind? Or, if we are faced with a civilization where each planet is a magnificent weapon, and it is crushing at our world, about to tear our planets apart with the tidal force, will it tell the difference between 'soldiers' and 'civilians'? If a mysterious force above the three-dimension world arrives and is about to raise or lower the dimension of our world, transforming us into a certain weird, indescribable state, will it care about the difference between 'soldiers' and 'civilians'?

"Don't be silly. Since the moment we discovered the grand universe, the old morals are already gone. The whole civilization has naturally entered a new age where everybody is a soldier. In order to survive in the dark universe, everybody can and must be sacrificed! One wouldn't deserve to be the commander of mankind without such a cold and clear understanding!"

"I-I can't do it!"

Covering his head, Li Yao replied in agony, "I can't treat so many living individuals as 'fuel'. I can't accept the death of billions of people casually. I can't view the problem from the perspective of billions of years. I can't do it in any case!"

"Yes, 99% of people can't. They cannot break themselves away as individuals and view the problem from the perspective of the whole civilization. Therefore, Cultivator Li Yao, no matter how strong you are, you can't be a qualified leader. If the civilization of mankind is led by a cowardly man full of petty kindness like you, it will perish within several hundred years!"

Wuying Qi smiled casually. "However, it doesn't matter. I can achieve what you can't. That's why I am the most perfect commander of the civilization of mankind, and all you need to do is to help me.

"Your rationality obviously tells you that what I say and do are right, but you can't accept it emotionally. You can't be a leader, and you are stopping me from becoming one. What's the reasoning in that? The civilization of mankind always requires an absolutely rational and absolutely cruel commander. Who else can it be if not me? Would it be better if the commander is as 'kind, vulnerable, and sympathetic' as you, who will cry or shout useless slogans whenever anything happens?"

Li Yao bulged his eyes, not knowing what to say for a moment.

"Your kindness that is limited by personal feelings is only petty kindness, if not hypocrisy. You only see that tens of billions of people may die before your eyes, but you have never considered the lives of the hundreds of billions of other people in the Imperium!"

Wuying Qi's voice grew tough again although it was mild for a while. "Do you not understand? The tens of billions of people in the Empyreal Terminus Sector are destined to die. If I do not activate the 'Next Sun Plan', the four families' ace fleets will occupy the capital planet tomorrow, and there will be as many victims who will die of massacre, famine, and plague!

"To make things worse, the four families are too useless and unorganized to resist the main force of the Covenant Alliance. The Imperium will definitely collapse under the Covenant Alliance's ambush!

"By then, not only will the number of victims be multiple times higher than 'tens of billions', but the survivors will inevitably be brainwashed by the Covenant Alliance in the worst way!

"See? Your 'kindness' can't change anything and will only make things worse. If hundreds of billions of people are truly killed in the massacre of the four families and the invasion of the Covenant Alliance, that will be your crime. They will be killed by your hypocrisy and your ridiculous sense of justice as well as your moral values!"

Dumbfounded, Li Yao was lost for words.

He only wanted to buy more time in the beginning and "pretended" that he was brainwashed by Wuying Qi.

However, his beliefs were truly corrupted bit by bit, and countless cold and dark things were injected into his mind.

"With everything coming to this point, countless people will die and countless people will be brainwashed one way or another. There's nothing that you or I can do to stop it."

Wuying Qi sighed and said, "I can only prevent the death of hundreds of billions of people by the death of tens of billions of people. I can only prevent the pale and evil brainwashing of the Covenant Alliance through righteous brainwashing. I can only try to make the sacrifices of countless individuals to be more worthwhile so that their deaths would not be for nothing. Even though I will be cursed for all eternity after doing that, I will not hesitate to do it as long as it is in the best interest of the future of mankind. I may kill a million innocent women and children today, but a billion innocent women and children will be saved tomorrow by what I did today. That's the greater kindness and justice. That's what might be called 'boundless love'!

"Cultivator Li Yao, I have been so patient with you because I do appreciate your talents. Are you still being pigheaded? If you insist on your beliefs, you'd better come up with a solid reason that disapproves of what I said and a way to resolve the four families and even the Alliance of the Holy Covenant with no bloodshed!"

Li Yao was stunned for a long time, and his voice became weaker and weaker. "But… But the whole Empyreal Terminus Sector will be destroyed by you! How can the Imperium deal with the Covenant Alliance after its heart is destroyed?"

"Haha. Hahaha. It seems that you have accepted my opinion despite the details that still need discussing, but these trivial problems are easy to settle!"

Wuying Qi laughed aloud and waved his hands. "You've made the same mistake on the matter, which is that you are viewing the new world with obsolete eyes. The so-called 'capital' is only necessary for a political entity on a planet. A real interstellar civilization does not need to, nor should it, set up a capital. It is not the heart of the Imperium but a cancer and the greatest redundancy of the country!

"Or rather, even if the interstellar civilization does need a political center, it should not be fixed to a certain planet but should be mobile. That is the only way to project the influence of the political center to the local places in time."

Chapter 2621: Wuying Qi's Greatest Secret!

"An interstellar civilization does not need a capital?"

The theory shattered Li Yao's mindset again and opened a whole new gate for him.

"That's right. In the age of the ancient Cultivators, the methods of communication and transportation were relatively underdeveloped, and the territory of the regime was not huge. That's why the rulers concentrated most of the leaders and the experts in one place, known as the 'court' in the 'capital', which is the epicenter of politics, economy, and military. At that time, it was a rather reasonable approach."

Wuying Qi elaborated, "But when it comes to this day, a united country of mankind can easily govern hundreds of Sectors, with a territory that spans across millions of lightyears. If you continue to rely on the experience of politics in the past, hoping to rule over the whole universe with one planet, wouldn't it be too absurd?

"You can tell it if you look at the capital planet today. In order to govern the whole universe, too many government agencies have been set up in this place. As a result, the population is booming, the agencies are bloated, and the environment is polluted. The departments and the major forces hamstring each other. This place has completely lost the functions as the brain and the heart.

"When the nobles and the strongmen at the remote border of the Imperium grow insubordinate, it is beyond the capital's reach, and it is impossible to mobilize troops in time to restrain and suppress them. As a result, the four Kurfürsten and other local warlords have arisen, making the Imperium fall apart.

"How does such a capital help with anything?

"As a matter of fact, since the first time that mankind embarked on the journey into space, they should've given up the obsolete way of administration. They shouldn't have established a fixed capital at all. It's a pity that from the demons to the Imperium a thousand years ago, none ever saw through it, which resulted in tragedies again and again.

"Let's talk about the collapse of the Star Ocean Imperium, the greatest catastrophe ten thousand years ago. At that time, the brilliant Supreme Emperor stayed on the capital planet but asked the Blood God to command the strongest expedition army and crush the remnant demons in space. As a result, the political force and the military force of the Star Ocean Imperium were completely separated. It was absolutely impossible for the capital planet to command the expedition army millions of lightyears away effectively. There were too many variables in the expedition that took decades to consider. Even if the Blood God was the Supreme Emperor's clone, so what? A rebellion is the inevitable outcome!

"If the Supreme Emperor had seen through it in time and moved all the government agencies of the Star Ocean Imperium into the expedition army, regarding the expedition fleet as a 'mobile capital', so that he could command the troops in person, how could the Blood God have had the chance to rebel, and how could the Star Ocean Imperium have collapsed?"

Attracted, Li Yao felt that what Wuying Qi said was so reasonable that he could not come up with any argument.

"In fact, many knowledgeable people since ancient times have vaguely realized the problem, and they made certain efforts intentionally or unintentionally."

Wuying Qi went on and said, "From the Demon Beast Empire to the Imperium of True Human Beings, a lot of people who were in power established supermassive space fortresses. Sometimes, they even hollowed a whole planet and modified it into a military fortress, naming it as 'Death Star', 'Destruction Star', 'Doom Star', 'Dark Star', or the likes.

"As a matter of fact, from the military point of view, the battle efficiency of such superspace fortresses, which put all the eggs into the same basket, was terribly low. Not only was their mobility close to zero, but they could also be attacked easily once the enemy broke in. It was a better option to devote the abundant resources that were spent on them to the construction of fleets or the armed forces on the surface of the planet!

"However, even if the 'Death Star' of the previous regime had poor performance on the battlefield and got destroyed instantly, the new regime would continue to establish new 'Death Stars'. Why was that? Were the military experts and technicians all idiots who could not see the fatal shortcoming of such supermassive space fortresses?

"No, the key is not military but politics. The purpose in building those super space fortresses with a huge fortune was not for their performance on the battlefield, but to absorb the resources of the local places incessantly during the construction so that the power of the local magnates would be diminished. When the local magnates could not resist the order of the court, the long-term peace and prosperity of the regime would be ensured.

"Of course, the local magnates understood the intention of the court very well. Therefore, they slandered such supermassive space fortresses through all kinds of channels, even by depicting the destructions of those super Death Stars in the literary works. They mocked such splendid-looking but actually useless weapons. But the truth was that they just did not want to be drained by the court with the excuse of the construction work.

"In the past, the counterattack of the local forces all worked out. Not a single regime had persisted on the strategy of weakening the local forces. Besides, the capital planet was too prosperous and convenient. Not a single emperor could bear to leave this enjoyable world and start from zero.

"A thousand years ago, I did not see through it and couldn't bear to depart from it, but today, I have clearly realized how ridiculous it is to have a fixed capital. I've made up my mind to move the capital to a fleet that is large in scale and has a high combat ability, transforming the Imperium into some sort of 'grand starship civilization'.

"However, the issue is too huge. There are too many stubborn interest groups on the capital planet. Not only do the four Kurfürst families hold tremendous interests in this place, but the reformists including the Wuying family are also deeply rooted too. If I propose the motion to relocate the capital, it will definitely raise the objection of countless people. It can't be helped. The capital has been the capital of the civilization of mankind for almost fifty thousand years. Those shortsighted and obstinate guys are too used to it to realize the problem.

"Therefore, I've decided to resolve the problem from the source by destroying the Empyreal Terminus Sector. In such a way, those noisy flies will have to shut their mouths. There's nothing they can do even if they do not want to move the capital!

"While a great price will be paid in the short run, it will be of great help for the sustainable development of the civilization of mankind in the long term. A short pain is definitely better than a lasting pain!

"By sacrificing the Empyreal Terminus Sector, I will receive a super fleet that has an extremely high combat ability where everybody is fair and clean. Then, I will move the entire court to this new fleet, which can be named the 'capital fleet'.

"Henceforth, the capital fleet will cruise in the whole universe regularly. Whenever it reaches a Sector, particularly one that is under the control of the powerful lords, it will use the local resources to strengthen the fleet and deal with the local corruption. It will also select the local talents and bring the best geniuses into the fleet, strengthening the court and weakening the local powers. After every twenty to thirty years, it will be possible to cruise across the Imperium. During the thirty years, even if the local powers intend to rebel, there won't be enough time for them to develop new rebellious troops. Even though corruptions take place at the bottom level, they will not spread out too severely, and most importantly, they can be excised and treated soon.

"In such a way, not just a thousand years, my Imperium will be steady, peaceful, and uncorrupted even ten thousand years later!

"The scale and combat ability of the capital fleet, including the generations of experts living in it, will be stronger and stronger. When the time is ripe and enough resources and technologies are stashed, the capital fleet will change into an expedition army to march at the grand universe!

"Li Yao, what do you think about the future that I described? Do you not believe that it is the only practical solution?"

Li Yao licked his lips again and again, only to feel that they were still dry.

The more he listened, the more he felt that Wuying Qi had a point—at least partly.

"Just like what I said, if you find any flaws or mistakes in the details, you are free to speak."

Wuying Qi said tolerantly, "As long as your point is valid, I will definitely follow your suggestion."

"Yes, there is one flaw!"

His head in a mess, Li Yao asked about one detail, "Your 'Next Sun Plan' will not kill tens of billions of people instantly but will completely disrupt their heads, transforming them into beasts that are full of killing desires. Infinite horror will be created on the capital planet. Would it cause a massive arrival of the extraterrestrial devils?"

After hearing that, Wuying Qi couldn't help but put on a gloating smile.

"It is very considerate of you to think about the extraterrestrial devils, Li Yao, but…"

Wuying Qi smiled. "There's no need to worry about the extraterrestrial devils. I have taken them into the calculation. I can even control all the devilized people and make them work for the Imperium."

"You can control the devilized people?"

Li Yao was shocked again. No, something was greatly wrong. He had neglected a very important thing.

What was it exactly? Think about it! That was Wuying Qi's greatest secret!

Yes! That's it! It doesn't make any sense!

"Speaking of which, where did you learn the secrets of the 'Gold Crystal Pyramid', Your Majesty? Not only do you know the accurate coordinates, you even know the methods to transform the Gold Crystal Pyramid into a 'gigantic brainwave enhancer', don't you?"

Li Yao mumbled in the lowest voice, "Although you spent hundreds of years in the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum, you couldn't have learned it from the Supreme Emperor because the other half of the key to open the most central part of the mausoleum wasn't found by Your Majesty, but instead, it had fallen into my hands.

"Since Your Majesty never really opened the core of the mausoleum, it was naturally impossible for you to get the whole heritages of the Supreme Imperium. Besides, I don't believe that the Supreme Emperor was the kind of person who would modify the Gold Crystal Pyramid to massively brainwash all human beings. Furthermore, the Supreme Emperor was the extraterrestrial devils' greatest enemy. There was no reason why he could control billions of devils.

"Considering the battle on the capital planet ten thousand years ago, only two people were aware of the secrets of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Apart from the Supreme Emperor, it was 'Blood God, the Mad Armageddon'. Also, the Blood God was corrupted by the extraterrestrial devils and turned into a demonic Cultivator before he betrayed the Supreme Emperor. The rebelling army that the Blood God commanded was a coalition army of humans, demons, and devils!

"Is it possible—

"Having grown into Blackstar the Great of the Imperium of True Human Beings in only several hundred years from a crippled refugee who escaped from the Martial Meritocrats Sector, Your Majesty is somewhat related to the Blood God, the legendary strongest demonic Cultivator?"

Chapter 2622: One Stone That Kills Four Birds

After he said that, the atmosphere in the whole illusionary realm immediately became intense.

Behind Wuying Qi's image where black smoke was popping up, tides of blood were soaring higher and higher.

An army of thousands of monsters was gathering into a new source of strength for him.

"What… a keen observation. You have exceeded my evaluation for you again and again. I'm truly appreciating you right now, Li Yao. I'm more and more inclined to kill you but finding it harder and harder to kill you!"

Wuying Qi said with a smile, "That's right. You wouldn't believe it even if I deny it. The Blood God and I were indeed in a master-disciple relationship for a while a thousand years ago. I learned a lot from the 'strongest demonic Cultivator in history'. The experience changed both me and the future of this universe.

"However, it's a very long time, and I'll tell you when there is more time. Right now, you only need to know that the Blood God is no more. He will cause no trouble to our plan."

"Did you… kill him?"

Observing his face, Li Yao rolled his eyes and said, "The Blood God taught you everything, hoping that you could grow into his tool and achieve a certain purpose for him. But out of his expectation, you did not grow into what he wanted you to be but created a unique path that is 'Immortal Cultivator'. You killed the Blood God and then established your own everlasting enterprise, right?"

Wuying Qi nodded his head and said, "You don't need to know the specific details. All you need to know is that I am absolutely not the kind of insane puppets that are controlled by the extraterrestrial devils."

"Of course, I can tell you that Your Majesty is brilliant and meticulous. However, I'm still baffled by one thing. What are the so-called 'tenebrum energy' and 'extraterrestrial devils'? Do they really only have negative influences such as destruction, slaughter, and chaos?"

"Definitely not. The tenebrum energy and the extraterrestrial devils are all very valuable. They can totally improve the civilization of mankind by ten thousand years. However, the powers of the energy and the thoughts are too advanced for most human beings to bear. That's why most ordinary people will go mad."

Wuying Qi said, "When I learned about the Blood God, I heard a lot of things about the extraterrestrial devils and the things beyond. By beyond, I mean the universe beyond the universe. It's…"

Li Yao blurted out, "Multiverse!"

"Yes, multiverse, that's a very accurate word. It seems that you do know a lot of things, Li Yao!"

Wuying Qi stared at Li Yao and continued, "Since you are aware of 'multiverse', you should know that countless bizarre civilizations exist in the multiverse. Many of the civilizations are vastly different from ours, and many are tens of thousands of years ahead of the civilization of mankind. They are truly like the legendary gods.

"However, no matter how powerful and advanced a civilization is, they cannot avoid their destiny of death. Most of the civilizations will launch the essence of their whole civilizations in a certain way before their destruction, like a dying man leaving the precious last words.

"The last words of a civilization are often launched and transmitted in the form of a certain 'wave'. They contain the legacy of the entire civilization as well as the anger and regret when it is faced with destruction. That's the prototype of 'extraterrestrial devils'."

Deeply attracted, Li Yao couldn't help but ask, "But…"

"But how do the last words of a civilization turn into ferocious 'extraterrestrial devils', right?"

Wuying Qi smiled. "It's very simple. Most civilizations did not die of natural cause, but suffered unexpected disasters and perished abruptly. In the world of ancient Cultivators, if a man died in fury under torture, it would be possible that he would turn into a vicious ghost. If a civilization dies abruptly, it is also possible that its last words are filled with negative feelings and the thought of revenge.

"They hope that their legacy is found by a new civilization and that the new civilization will follow their last words and avenge them in the future. Think about it, can there be any love or beauty in those last words? They are definitely the most extreme and atrocious stuff.

"On the other hand, any legacy will be twisted and eroded during the transmission in space. Waves are never a steady means of storage in the first place. After drifting for hundreds of thousands of lightyears, it is only reasonable that they are riddled with holes and only have the mottled pieces left. Even though they record the most neutral and peaceful techniques, it is still possible that somebody will be deranged if they only practice part of the technique.

"More importantly, the civilizations and the individuals in the multiverse are mostly so different from mankind that it is impossible for us to understand each other.

"Think about it. If a civilization of bugs that only has communities but not individuals, or if an 'individual civilization' where one life form occupies a whole planet, leaves last words, even though the last words are not malicious at all but only contain their soul marks, the ordinary people will consider themselves to be a bug in a community of bugs, or a behemothic creature that covers a whole planet, after hearing the words. How could the ordinary people not be collapsed and deranged?"

Li Yao nodded his head. "You do have a point, Your Majesty."

Wuying Qi's words fitted Long Yangjun's speculation in the past. It was very likely to be the truth.

Overly-expanding strength tended to enchant people in the first place, not to mention that it was from a completely different civilization that was thousands of years more advanced.

It was perfectly normal for the ordinary people to be crushed by such "last words".

"While the extraterrestrial devils are highly dangerous, we still need to study and analyze them, because they are too valuable. The simplest example will be the usage of the tenebrum energy, which has been brought into our universe by different extraterrestrial devils and is likely to help us break out of this universe."

Wuying Qi said, "Of course, the study and analysis on the extraterrestrial devils have to wait until I get back the Imperium. There's no need to talk about that for now. All you need to know right now, Li Yao, is that I have absolute confidence to control the extraterrestrial devils and the devilized people.

"In conclusion, my 'Next Sun Plan' can achieve four purposes!

"First of all, the four Kurfürst families' ace fleets will be crushed, and the Imperium will be regathered into an impenetrable whole!

"Secondly, all the suspicions and disputes about the relocation of the capital will be cut from the source. The political center of the Imperium will be moved from a planet to a group of starships. The civilization of mankind will be upgraded from an interstellar civilization into a grand-universe civilization!

"Thirdly, with the Deep Sea Fleet as the center, the Imperial Guards and the Astounding Thunder Fleet as the bones, and the four Kurfürst families' ace fleets as the flesh and blood, the brand-new 'capital fleet' will become the prototype of the future expedition army of the grand universe. Nothing can stop us from pressing forward.

"Fourthly, it is also very possible for us to harvest billions of devilized people. While those devilized people are ignorant slaughter machines, they can be projected to the frontline under my control as the first defense line to resist the attack of the Covenant Alliance. The extraterrestrial devils and the Covenant Alliance are sworn enemies in the first place. I believe that the devilized people will stall the Covenant Alliance long enough for me to mobilize the troops and gather my strength. The Covenant Alliance's 'ambush' will only become a joke!

"This plan is a stone that kills four birds. I've taken every detail into consideration. Every problem has corresponding solutions. I can definitely lead the civilization of mankind to a rerise. Li Yao, do you have anything else to say?"

"I do have some questions."

Li Yao said, "Please forgive my dumbness, but I haven't quite understood many details. Should we sort everything through from the beginning, Your Majesty?"

"Of course."

Wuying Qi's smile remained unchanged, but a demonic brilliance beamed out of his eyes. "However, I have better ways to show my sincerity for you, Li Yao!"

Hardly had he concluded his sentence when the black nerves, which had been as thick as vipers in the beginning, further expanded, injecting Wuying Qi's powerful mental force into the depths of Li Yao's soul as if they were transmitting blood.

The black stripes that enshrouded Li Yao were immediately connected and expanded into a gigantic black water drop, completely swallowing Li Yao.

BOOM!

Li Yao saw all kinds of illusions.

He seemed to have become the commander of the Pangu Clan who was in a dilemma, the fish who led his clan to leave the ocean and the monkey that escaped from the forest. He became a member of the primordial civilizations that had been born and died in this tiny universe billions of years ago.

Suddenly, he heard countless restless souls who died miserably berating him, claiming that he caused their deaths. It was exactly because he stopped Wuying Qi's plan that the capital fell and the Imperium collapsed. Countless people had been killed because of his mistake.

Suddenly, he saw the new Imperium and the future civilization of mankind a long time after the Next Sun Plan was enforced.

What a magnificent civilization it was! The clean and prosperous planets, the splendid capital fleet, and the tall and handsome Immortal Cultivators. Because enough living space had been found in the multiverse, even the ordinary people had gained resources that were more than what they could enjoy in their entire lives.

The pictures, the voices, the thoughts, and the marks surged into Li Yao's soul like surging blood and a swarm of beasts, crushing all the defense lines, polluting all the will, and expanding all the gaps into devastating wounds. They then further pressed into the deeper consciousness along the wounds, about to transform Li Yao's subconsciousness into a terrifying monster!

Li Yao spent a long time inside the black drop.

In the end, the black water drop blossomed like a bud, and Li Yao, whose eyes were entirely black, flew out gradually, with an unprecedented epiphany on his face.

"Li Yao, you've woken up."

Wuying Qi looked at Li Yao with a smile.

"Yes, Your Majesty, I've woken up."

Li Yao's voice couldn't have sounded more gloomy and creepy.

"How do you feel right now? Can you see the road ahead of you?"

Wuying Qi asked again.

"It's crystal clear. To think that I was unable to resolve the simple arithmetic to compare billions with trillions! What an idiot I was in the past."

Li Yao stared at Wuying Qi. With the most intense brilliance beaming out of his eyes, he slowly extended his arm and clenched his fist, before he said in the most sincere way, "Long live Your Majesty! Long live Blackstar the Great! Long live the Imperium of True Human Beings!"

Chapter 2623: Complete Submission!

Inside the Gold Crystal Tower in reality, Li Yao's slightly closed eyelids shivered, and he slowly opened his eyes. The whole dark night seemed to have been condensed inside.

There was still a hint of confusion on his face, as if he just had a long and profound dream. While rubbing his temples, he thought in a trance. He also scratched his skin so hard that red marks were left on it, as if he were trying to break his shell and reveal his brand-new self below.

Then, he saw Wuying Qi who was standing not far away from him.

Like a black mountain, Wuying Qi occupied his entire vision.

Li Yao's eyebrows were furrowed. Two massive forces were having a fierce competition inside his heart.

His eyelids, lips, and fingers were all cramping crazily.

A moment later, with ambiguous noises echoing from the depths of his throat, he fell on one of his knees as a gesture of submission to Wuying Qi.

"Li Yao, this is exactly the effect of 'enlightenment'. How about it? It does not affect your thinking ability and your free will, does it?"

Wuying Qi asked casually.

"… It doesn't."

Li Yao's face flushed as he thought carefully for a moment and replied, "I am still a Cultivator who persists in the path of the Cultivators and loves my hometown. I still believe that the lives of so many people shouldn't be sacrificed recklessly if there is a better option. However… however—

"However, we don't have an option right now. While Your Majesty's solution seems cruel, it is the 'least bad' and the 'most effective' plan with the highest odds of success.

"Therefore, 'I am a Cultivator' and 'I support Your Majesty' are not contradictory; in fact, they can only coexist. If I am a real Cultivator who cares about the maximal interests of mankind instead of my own hypocritical moral mysophobia, I should support Your Majesty! If I don't support Your Majesty, I'll be a sanctimonious false Cultivator, as much of a scumbag as the false Immortal Cultivators, if not even worse than them!

"Everything above is the conclusion that I've made through independent and rational thinking with a clear head. It is absolutely not controlled by any force or any kind of 'evil brainwashing'. I have merely been… convinced by Your Majesty's reason."

The more Li Yao talked, the more determined and calm he appeared, as if he had truly dissected and analyzed his own brain.

"Hahahaha. It's great to know that you can think in such a way, Li Yao!"

Wuying Qi laughed aloud. "Exactly, the so-called 'brainwashing' is just reasoning in a more advanced and effective way. Is it evil and terrifying at all?"

"If Your Majesty is going to perform massive enlightenment on the four families' ace fleets so that they can understand the reasons too—"

Li Yao said solemnly, "I swear to god that I'll definitely support Your Majesty with my everything!"

"Huh…"

Wuying Qi narrowed his eyes and looked at Li Yao. Then he suddenly found it odd and asked, "Why is your face red and perspiring?"

"Because I… am excited!"

Li Yao wiped the sweat on his neck and looked at it. He said, "Just now, Your Majesty showed me the future of the new Imperium and mankind. What a magnificent fleet, what a glorious civilization, and what happy people! After watching it, I feel so excited that it is impossible for me to calm myself down. I almost hate that I cannot time travel to the golden age in the future. Ah, Your Majesty! Ah, the Imperium! Ah…"

Li Yao suddenly kneeled on the ground and vomited so hard that his stomach acid was almost thrown out.

Wuying Qi was stunned. "Li Yao, what was that about?"

"I-I don't know. I just feel that the world around me is shaking, and I cannot control myself."

Li Yao looked at Wuying Qi and spurted out acidic fluids again, making quite a fuss. "It's probably the information flooding into my head is too much for me to process. Does the technology of enlightenment have such side effects? I'll have to ask for your advice, Your Majesty."

Wuying Qi was silent.

The technology of enlightenment naturally had plenty of side effects. Not just the shock on the brain and nausea, even schizophrenia, madness, and brain explosion shouldn't come as any surprise.

Also, Wuying Qi had sensed one thing or two about the powerfulness and weirdness of Li Yao's soul too. Therefore, he could not foresee what kind of negative effects would be raised during the collision of souls.

Vomiting was a natural reaction to brain concussion. That should be the side effect with minimal influence, right?

"Please rest assured, Your Majesty, I'll be fine. I'm getting used to the vomiting…"

His face pale, Li Yao held his stomach and said, "Let's not care about such unimportant things. There are a lot of things, including the whole plan I had in mind and all the traps that I set up, that I would like to confess to Your Majesty frankly, but first of all, allow me to help Your Majesty to capture the other two rebels. I hope that Your Majesty gives them a chance to return from the lost journey so that they can enjoy… enjoy the unprecedented epiphany just like I did through your enlightenment!"

…

Li Jialing rushed crazily among the caves that looked like a maze. Holding back his beating heart that was ripping his chest apart, he did not dare to look back at the stormy barrage behind him. Suddenly, his left foot accidentally stepped into a gap and cracked. He was in such agony that his tears almost surged out. However, he did not dare to stay any longer but rolled into a branch that extended downward. It was not until he rolled into the darkness at the end and heard no sound from behind for a long time that he finally moaned in a low voice.

"Sister Long—"

He called Long Yangjun in a point-to-point communicator. "Where are you? I can't hold on any longer. A whole crystal suit legion is after me!"

"I'm in the cave seven hundred meters to your left, but it's very dangerous here. Don't come close!"

Long Yangjun gnashed her teeth in the secret communication channel. "Wuying Qi has kept so many experts under his command. They are also fearless and determined to die with me the moment they meet me, entirely different from the cowards of the four families. I'm trapped here too!"

"I wonder what's happening on Brother Yao's side."

Li Jialing squeezed rapidly-freezing gels and high-density bone stimulators on his ankle that was as swollen as a ball. While activating his spiritual energy to accelerate the healing of his bone, he said desperately, "Wuying Qi has entered the Gold Crystal Pyramid and spent hours with Brother Yao inside. What are they doing inside exactly? Would anything happen to Brother Yao?"

"Rest assured about that. Bad guys always outlive good guys. Your Brother Yao can't die so easily!"

Long Yangjun grunted, as if she had been hit by the enemy brutally. "However, if the bad guy does not come to help us, we two good guys will certainly be outlived by him!"

While the two of them were talking, a thunderous explosion that sounded as if the core of the planet had collapsed echoed from the depths of the ground.

They thought that it was the explosion of a super crystal bomb, but the explosion lingered on like a surging tide and gradually approached them, becoming louder and louder and resulting in greater and greater quakes.

"An earthquake? A super-massive earthquake?"

Li Jialing and Long Yangjun who were several hundred meters away both changed their expressions greatly.

The tectonic structure in this area had been ripped apart and sabotaged by the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God again and again ten thousand years ago. Now, it was riddled with holes by Wuying Qi's Imperial Guards again.

Such an intense earthquake immediately raised the collapse of tunnels and caves. The rocks weighing billions of tons fell from the sky and completely changed the terrain.

Even though most of the Imperial Guards had stayed in the safe area that was calculated in advance, some of them were still away from the safe area chasing after Li Jialing and Long Yangjun. They were large in number but weak. As a result, the falling rocks gave them quite a heavy blow.

Li Jialing and Long Yangjun were both experts above average and wouldn't be trapped by the rocks for now. However, the drastic changes around as well as the incessant quakes each more intense than the last made the two of them look more awful than ever. They thought of a possibility at the same time.

The Gold Crystal Pyramid had been activated!

Li Yao couldn't stop Wuying Qi's plan after all. The supermassive brainwashing attempt conducted through the solar storm had begun!

Right then, a third voice—a calm voice that belonged to Li Yao—interjected in their secret communication channel.

"Where are you hiding?"

Li Yao asked.

"Brother Yao!"

Li Jialing was both surprised and delighted. "You've escaped! Where are you? What happened inside the Gold Crystal Pyramid?"

"There's no time for any explanation. I'm here."

Li Yao sent a pair of coordinates to them, which were not far away from them. "Let's meet here, and I'll tell you everything."

"Okay!"

Not suspecting anything, Li Jialing immediately rushed toward Li Yao.

"Wait…"

Long Yangjun, however, grew skeptical. "Li Yao, did anything happen to you?"

"Not at all. I did have many problems a few hours ago, but I don't have any of them right now. Trust me. I've found a solution. Although it is very risky and may result in many problems, it is certainly worth a try."

Li Yao said, "You will understand it when you see me."

"But…"

Long Yangjun was still hesitating.

Li Jialing, however, hopped to the coordinates given by Li Yao and searched for him in the dispersing smoke.

Very soon, he found a dark shadow in the smoke that was as dense as a fog.

"Brother Yao!"

Li Jialing strode forward and greeted him, not noticing anything wrong until he saw the black hair that was dancing crazily like tentacles on the back of the stranger's head. However, there was already no time. It was his mother—no, some kind of dark existence that lurked inside his mother's body and controlled her face. A face with a mysterious smile gradually surfaced from the mist.

It was Wuying Qi, Blackstar the Great.

Hiss—

The blood all over Li Jialing's body as well his brain nerves was entirely frozen, and he gasped in desperation.

Long Yangjun's shocked and furious shout was echoing in the communication channel too, mixed with the noises as she fought Li Yao. "Li Yao, you have betrayed us!"

Chapter 2624: As Vile As Such!

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Li Yao and Long Yangjun, two dominating experts who had reached the peak of the Divinity Transformation Stage if not beyond, blew out 120% of their strength, raising noises that sounded like collisions of meteorites and blasts that constituted visible tsunamis.

Even the shock from the Gold Crystal Pyramid's abrupt rise from the bottom of the planet seemed to have been dwarfed by the battle of the two experts.

Now, even without the point-to-point communicator, it was already possible to hear Li Yao and Long Yangjun's desperate roars in the stones, mist, smoke, and blasts.

"Li Yao, what are you doing? You're out of your mind! Have you forgotten who you are? You are a Cultivator!"

"I am not out of my mind, and I certainly haven't forgotten a Cultivator's destiny, which is to protect the interests of mankind as much as possible, and not to protect my own reputation and moral values! I cannot ruin the whole civilization because of a certain ridiculous code of conduct in my heart!"

"What did Wuying Qi do to you? Did he completely brainwash you? Hurry and wake up!"

"I am wide awake right now. The so-called 'brainwashing' is absolutely different from what we imagined. His Majesty did not inculcate any thoughts into my head by force but merely enlightened me. What I think and do right now are based on absolute rationality. You, on the other hand, need to calm down, Long Yangjun!"

"You—"

"What? Do you want to ask why I speak of your real name? That's because hiding is absolutely meaningless right now. You've been surrounded by thousands of soldiers and His Majesty himself. Where can you possibly hide? Your only way out and the only solution to the trouble right now is to kneel before His Majesty like me and cheer 'long live Blackstar the Great' from the bottom of your heart. Trust me. You have to trust me that it is the only way to rescue all the people!"

"I'll be damned if I trust you! You traitor! I must've been blind to regard you as a friend!"

"Hehe, if you are unwilling to take my kind advice, don't blame me for my ruthlessness! Friend? Hehehehe. We aren't friends since the very beginning; instead, we are sworn enemies, right? On the Nuwa warship in the Ancient Sages Sector, after we came to the Imperium, or even below the ground of the capital planet, you tricked me more times than I can count! Now, I've let go of the grudge and offered to enlighten you, but you do not appreciate my favor at all? If you don't know what's good for yourself, you might as well die!"

"Li Yao, you—you—"

Their intermittent talks were interrupted by the deafening explosions now and then, but their voices were gradually filled with fury.

The two experts were fighting for real without any disguise.

Wuying Qi stopped the Imperial Guards from moving forward. He even asked the experts among the Imperial Guards to evacuate to the safe area. He stood in the mist alone and sensed the spiritual energy from Li Yao and Long Yangjun, analyzing whether or not they were really fighting.

It appeared that they were fighting for real.

Li Jialing stood before Wuying Qi, his heart half frozen, but he did not dare to move at all under the suppression of the overwhelming vibe. He merely touched the back of his head while Wuying Qi was completely focused on the battle between Li Yao and Long Yangjun.

But out of his expectation, he sensed dozens of tentacles of spiritual energy from Wuying Qi the moment he moved his arm. They tied him up, leaving bloody marks on his flesh!

"Don't move."

Wuying Qi did not bother to look at the young lion at all. Staring at Li Yao and Long Yangjun without blinking, he said casually, "Your body is mine. If you behave, I will spare your soul; otherwise, I will draw your soul and crumble it into pieces!"

Li Jialing found himself flying into Wuying Qi's hand, which grabbed his neck tightly as the two of them jumped into Li Yao and Long Yangjun's battlefield.

Now, Li Yao and Long Yangjun were on Wuying Qi's two sides.

If the two of them had been acting up until so far, it was actually an excellent spot to attack him.

However, Li Yao showed no signs of attacking Wuying Qi at all. Instead, he activated hundreds of scorching streaks of brilliance from his Cosmos Ring, which rained upon Long Yangjun overwhelmingly. He also roared, "Your Majesty, watch out!"

He stopped before Wuying Qi fearlessly like a loyal dog.

Long Yangjun dodged Li Yao's barrage in a hurry. Her face blushing, she cursed aloud again.

In the meantime, the dark mental force, like a flood that had just broken the dam, poured out of Wuying Qi's eyebrows and swept at Long Yangjun's brain.

Long Yangjun and Li Yao were on par with each other in the first place. Their previous battle had exhausted most of their physical and mental strength. Now that Wuying Qi's mental attack in the Divinity Branching Stage had come, how could she resist it?

Her expression became tranced, and her spiritual energy was disordered. Even though she was not hypnotized yet, she was at least in some sort of confusion.

Blood was also spreading out of Li Yao's pores, making him look even more hideously. He panted hard and strode at Long Yangjun while he grinned hideously. "Even though you are a dual successor of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan, so what? Even though you are a duplicate of a primeval warrior, so what? Even though you know countless secrets of the primeval age and the locations of relics, how can they help you? You are going to be killed by me after all! Let me tell me, I've been trying to figure out for years which of us is stronger and whether or not I can kill you. It seems that I can! Haha! Hahahaha!"

Li Yao raised his fiery saber high; the brilliance of the saber bouncing off Long Yangjun's tranced and desperate face.

"Wait—"

Wuying Qi frowned hard with a weird look. "Don't attack just yet. What did you just say? Is she a primeval successor?"

Li Yao blinked and said quickly, "It's not a big deal, Your Majesty. This is a highly cunning and dangerous person. There will be great risks if she is kept alive. Allow me to execute her first and tell her background to you later!"

After he said that, he raised his saber and was about to chop, only to be stopped by an overwhelming force.

"Did you not hear my words?"

Wuying Qi became grave. "If I want anybody to die, they will die; if I want anybody alive, they will stay alive! Tell me about her in detail!"

"About that…"

Li Yao looked at Long Yangjun in embarrassment and coughed. "I dare not lie to you, Your Majesty. This woman who is under the name of 'Dongfang Mingyue'—well, she is not exactly a woman. All in all, this person's true name is 'Long Yangjun'. She is in fact a 'primitive' and was born after being frozen for hundreds of thousands of years as an embryo of a certain elite warrior of the primeval age. She more or less grasps some of the secrets of the primeval age, but it is not a big deal. I know most of her secrets. Let's just kill her to be safe!"

"There are… such things?"

Even Wuying Qi was surprised. He observed Long Yangjun for a long time and said, "Tell me the details. Do not leave out anything!"

"… Okay."

Li Yao informed Wuying Qi of abundant information about the Ancient Sages Sector, including the Nuwa warship and the Pangu's laboratory.

The more Wuying Qi listened, the more astounded he became. "If that's true, it explains why she could start a 'Nepenthe' as the 'Serenity Seraph' below the ground."

Yue Wushuang's "Tribunal of Extraterrestrial Devils" was certainly not useless. Naturally, a lot of evidence about Nepenthe had been found and reported to Wuying Qi.

Li Yao said with a bitter smile, "Your Majesty, do you still not trust my loyalty? If it isn't the truth, how could I have come up with such mind-boggling things in such a short time?"

"Don't overthink, Li Yao. I have absolute confidence in the enlightenment technology. I certainly believe that you have understood something new and will not sabotage the magnificent cause of the civilization of mankind."

Wuying Qi said, "I'm only thinking that, if you were speaking the truth, this woman must be in possession of abundant secrets of the primeval age, which are of great help for the future Imperium and mankind."

Li Yao hesitated for a moment and said, "Probably, but she is simply too stubborn to be changed. What choice do I have? Let's just kill her and eliminate the trouble once and for all!"

Hardly had he concluded his sentence when Long Yangjun lunged at Li Yao like an infuriated lizard. Even though she was enshrouded by Wuying Qi's invisible force field, she still shrieked devastatingly, "Li Yao, you piece of crap! I treated you as the only friend in the whole universe and I told you my greatest secrets, and this is how you repay me?

"You snuck into the Imperium to strike a balance between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance in order to save your motherland Star Glory Federation in the end, but what about me? Is the Star Glory Federation or the civilization of mankind my business at all? Why did I risk my life with you? Would it hurt me even if all of you are dead? The only reason is that I regarded you as a friend! I was doing it for a friend!

"But as it turns out, you have stabbed me right in the back when I'm willing to do anything for you? Haha, hahahaha. Great! This is too great! I'll let you pay in the most devastating way!

"Your Majesty, I am willing to surrender to you and confess all the secrets of the primeval civilizations to you on one condition—Kill him. Kill this worthless beast and then destroy his motherland, the wretched place named 'Star Glory Federation'!

"You don't have a star map and you don't know the accurate coordinates? That's alright. I have them. I have the rough coordinates of the Star Glory Federation and the detailed intelligence about their military, politics, economy, etc. I can submit all of them to Your Majesty as long as Your Majesty helps me destroy his motherland. I'm going to enjoy the look on your face when it is illuminated by the very fire that destroys the Star Glory Federation!

"Stop glaring at me. It's too late to feel scared now. You are forcing me to do this. 'Those who are not of my own kind are bound to think differently'. That's well said. Then, open your eyes and see clearly how terrible an 'alien' can be when they completely lose hope!"

Like a burning, vengeful ghost, Long Yangjun burst into laughter with a twisted face and a headful of messy hair.

Chapter 2625: Dog Bites Dog!

"How vicious of you!"

His face pale, Li Yao lost his calmness and pointed at the tip of Long Yangjun's nose, cursing aloud, "I knew that you were a brutal, insane villain the moment I met you. You have been manipulating me to gradually approach the center of the cosmos since the very beginning! At first, you came and fought with me only because you felt that the Star Glory Federation was strong and you hoped you could get enough resources to repair the Nuwa warship. Now, after realizing that His Majesty's thigh is even thicker and more worth clinging to, you are naturally kicking me and the federation away, hoping to achieve your purpose by manipulating His Majesty!

"Your Majesty, don't trust her at all. She is only lying to you! She is just pretending! Really, Your Majesty, I swear to god that she is pretending! Don't believe anything she says! Everything will be over as long as she is killed, Your Majesty!"

"You know very well which one between us is pretending!"

With gloomy fire bouncing on her body, Long Yangjun chuckled miserably. "Li Yao, it was you who started such a ruthless game! It's too late to regret it now! I am in possession of so many secrets of the primeval age, the coordinates of so many relics, the ways to control the Nuwa warships, the upgrade plans of Colossi, and the extraordinary arts from the primeval age. Given enough time and resources, I can recover all of them and dedicate them to His Majesty. I am definitely much more valuable to His Majesty than a scoundrel like you and the wretched federation behind you!

"Like you said, I'm traveling in the universe by myself, and in order to survive, I have to find a thick thigh to cling to. Before, since we were more or less friends, I decided to cling to the thigh of the federation. Now that you are so heartless, and His Majesty is the only great man in history who can compare to the Supreme Emperor, why shouldn't I work for His Majesty? I'll be an idiot if I don't!

"I think that you are in such a hurry to kill me not just because you are scared of my revenge, but also because you are scared that I will reveal the secrets about the Star Glory Federation to His Majesty, right? You haven't even told the coordinates of the federation to His Majesty, yet you claim that you are loyal to His Majesty? That is downright absurd!"

"You—You—"

Li Yao seemed to have been infuriated after Long Yangjun pointed out what was on his mind. Brutality as intense as fire beamed out of his pale face, and he rolled his eyes crazily, as he considered how to break Wuying Qi's control and kill Long Yangjun.

"Your Majesty, do you see his cunning eyes? This scoundrel must not be trusted!"

Long Yangjun said coldly, "Back in his hometown, he was best known for his acting skills, his tactics that knew no boundaries, and his shameless style. Whoever believes half a word that he says will be buried in regrets. I swear to god that he must be acting. Chances are that he hasn't been brainwashed by Your Majesty at all, or at least not as badly as he appears. That's because he spoke to us in person before that he received special soul training that made his brain immune to most mental attacks.

"Therefore, Your Majesty, there's no need to hesitate now. Whatever this shameless guy says, as long as you turn a blind ear to it and simply chop his head, the whole world will be in peace!"

"Your Majesty, Your Majesty, we had a deal!"

Li Yao was so exasperated that he was soaked in sweat. "You promised that the independency of the Cultivators will be partly retained, and that I could join the 'Enlightenment Committee'. You're not… well… you're not…"

"Independency of the Cultivators? That's hilarious. How is an insignificant Star Glory Federation qualified to compare to His Majesty's Imperium of True Human Beings?"

Long Yangjun sneered in disdain, "I'll make it much simpler. All I want is to figure out the mysteries of my origins and find out the truth of the primeval war. I believe that His Majesty must have a lot of primeval treasures that he cannot crack. On the other hand, I am capable of cracking the treasures although I don't have any. Therefore, His Majesty and I are much more complementary!"

"Your Majesty, kill her!"

Lost for words, Li Yao shouted angrily.

"Your Majesty, kill him!"

Long Yangjun echoed equally aggressively.

"Enough! Who do you think I am? An executioner at your command?"

Wuying Qi had been observing the two of them biting each other with a casual smile. Now that the two of them were truly squabbling, he finally turned grave and roared to calm them down.

"Li Yao, a monarch does not joke. Now that I've promised that you and most Cultivators will be pardoned as long as you recognize your mistakes, why would I launch another expedition? Even the 'false Immortal Cultivators' in the four Kurfürst families, who are sworn enemies to be, can be pardoned by me. I have fewer reasons to attack the Cultivators at the edge of the cosmos whom I barely know. As long as you don't ask to be killed and accept my 'enlightenment' obediently, you will be safe and sound. Rest assured about that."

Wuying Qi reassured Li Yao and then looked at Long Yangjun. "Long Yangjun, you don't need to be too bothered by your identity either. Although your background is truly special, I always recruit people for their talents, not for their origins.

"In my eyes, the thirteen carbon-based lives in the Pangu civilizations' alliance in the past extracted the essence of their genes and injected it into a carefully-selected ape on a blue planet, producing the most perfect carbon-based life in the whole universe that is known as mankind.

"Therefore, there is no clear boundary between the Pangu Clan and human beings. They are just different evolutionary phases of the same life. We are all 'human beings' and dominators of this universe in a general sense.

"Furthermore, you were a human being hundreds of thousands of years ago, except that you received certain special genetic modifications and soul training. I don't think you are any different from other people. In fact, due to certain experiences of mine, I find you rather similar to me!"

"Really?"

Long Yangjun was overjoyed.

"Of course. We are the same type of people. I, you, and Li Yao; we are the people who stand on the summit of evolution."

Wuying Qi waved his hands and declared resolutely, "Our history and past don't matter; what matters is the future. As long as we stick together, we will definitely create a new-generation, real human beings in the near future!

"Since you two were good friends and are now fighting for a common purpose, why do you have to fight each other? I believe both of you, but I'm still waiting for your next performance."

"The next performance?"

Li Yao and Long Yangjun looked at each other.

Shadows of sabers and swords raged in their eyes.

"Your Majesty, I have a confession to make!"

Long Yangjun suddenly said, "Did you know that Li Yao had set a malicious trap on Li Jialing? As a matter of fact, our main purpose on this trip to the underworld of the capital planet is to deliver this trap for Your Majesty to step in!"

"Oh?"

Wuying Qi asked with great interest, "What kind of trap?"

"Before we left, Li Yao did a minor surgery on Li Jialing and planted a tiny Cosmos Ring on the back of his neck. Inside the Cosmos Ring was a piece of confining magical equipment that Li Yao invented himself. He claimed that it could suppress even the gods."

Long Yangjun glanced at Li Yao cockily and continued, "He knew that Your Majesty was determined to get Li Jialing's body and therefore transformed it into a trap. Then, if Your Majesty injected your soul into Li Jialing's body carelessly, you would be caught by his magical equipment!

"With Your Majesty's strength in the Divinity Branching Stage, such a piece of minor magical equipment naturally cannot trap you. However, as long as Your Majesty is somewhat affected, it will facilitate his following attacks.

"The Cosmos Ring could both be controlled by Li Yao's telepathic thoughts remotely and by Li Jialing manually by pressing the back of his hand. He couldn't have told Your Majesty such a vicious trap, could he?"

Li Yao shouted even before she concluded her accusation, "Long Yangjun, you can't be more wrong. I've joined His Majesty sincerely, and I naturally informed His Majesty of the matter as soon as possible. Why else do you think His Majesty has been grabbing the back of Li Jialing's neck?"

"What?"

Long Yangjun found it hard to believe.

Observing their subtle reactions, Wuying Qi absorbed the Cosmos Ring inside Li Jialing's back with his fingers.

Li Jialing had been watching their performance in shock. Now that the back of his neck was torn apart, he screamed miserably and almost passed out. There was no telling whether it was because of fury or because of pain.

Wuying Qi played with the fingernail-sized Cosmos Ring for a while and tossed it back to Li Yao.

Li Yao opened his hands, and a shackle that looked like a glittering scorpion immediately flew out of the Cosmos Ring.

"What a piece of delicate confining magical equipment. Only the deftest hands could've crafted such a spectacular toy."

Wuying Qi said with a smile, "That explains my confusion why you brought Li Jialing with you when you were going to such a dangerous place. It was highly illogical. I wouldn't have touched his body recklessly in the first place, but now that you are so candid, you have saved me tremendous time for a full body check."

Li Yao grinned and bared the sharp teeth at Long Yangjun.

Long Yangjun somewhat lost her patience. She continued, "Fine, even though Li Yao has informed Your Majesty of the matter, did he tell Your Majesty that he has a piece of portable magical equipment named 'Neltharion' that he has been baptizing with his blood. It has an unstoppable drill of mystic rays and carries countless Cosmos Rings that are fully loaded with crystal bombs, designed to blow up the critical parts in a target?"

Before Wuying Qi replied, however, Li Yao simply offered Neltharion with both hands and chuckled. "I confessed to His Majesty that I had such a piece of magical equipment a long time ago, and I just drilled through the shell of the mainframe crystal processors of the Gold Crystal Pyramid with it, planning to sneak in and plant crystal bombs. Now that I came to the realization at the critical moment, I have naturally recalled it as well as all the crystal bombs. Do I even need you to inform His Majesty?"

Chapter 2626: More and More Critical!

"You—"

Caught unprepared, Long Yangjun was unable to say anything for a long time, as if her throat were stuffed with a crystal bomb.

Seeing her blushing face, Li Yao gloated even more. Rubbing a Cosmos Ring on his little finger, he retrieved a tiny object and delivered it to Wuying Qi with both hands respectfully. He said, "Now that I have agreed with Your Majesty's ideology, there is no need to hide anything now. Not only have I confessed the secrets about Neltharion, but I am also willing to offer the critical item to Your Majesty. This is exactly the half key to the central room in the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum!"

On Li Yao's palm, the half black metal stick floated and emanated deep brilliance.

"What!"

Long Yangjun gasped hard and said subconsciously, "It's fake! It must be fake! Don't fall for it, Your Majesty!"

Li Yao sniffed. "His Majesty's eyes are too keen to be fooled by any trick. Your warning was highly redundant!"

Greed flashed in Wuying Qi's eyes as he opened his fingers and attracted the half key that Li Yao offered to himself. He examined the key in his palm carefully.

Then, he took out Li Linghai's half key. After the two parts of the key were stuck together, they were truly combined in tiny cracking sounds!

Wuying Qi injected a stream of feeble spiritual energy into the key to inspect the cohesion of the key, only to detect no error at all. The key was such a complete piece that he could not stab his spiritual energy into it at all.

"This is indeed the critical key to open the last part of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum!"

Wuying Qi grabbed the complete key in his hands tightly. Eyeing Li Yao with even more compliments and trust, he said, "Well done, Li Yao. I appreciate your judiciousness and your loyalty."
