"Thank you, Your Majesty!"

Li Yao cut Long Yangjun with his eyes again.

Long Yangjun's cheeks were puffing like bloated dolphins. Gnashing her teeth for a long time, she finally unleashed her ultimate move. "Fine, fine, you are truly His Majesty's loyal subject, but I wonder, have you told His Majesty the things about Lei Chenghu?"

Slightly stunned, Li Yao immediately showed embarrassment.

Wuying Qi had been scanning the look on their faces and the changes of their biophysical parameters. Detecting Li Yao's anomaly, he slightly frowned and asked, "Huh?"

"Ha. It seems that you haven't told His Majesty such an important matter yet. Why, do you want to keep the last trump card to negotiate with His Majesty?"

Long Yangjun grew cocky again now that she hit Li Yao's weakness. She grimaced and said, "Are you going to confess yourself, or do you want me to tell everything to His Majesty?"

"Who… Who's keeping any trump card? I just didn't have the time to say it."

Li Yao glared at Long Yangjun brutally, before he said to Wuying Qi while blushing. "Your Majesty, as a matter of fact, we—we had a minor agreement with Lei Chenghu…"

Narrowing his eyes, Wuying Qi asked expressionlessly, "What kind of agreement?"

"I wonder whether or not Lei Chenghu reached out to Your Majesty and reported that I snuck into his flagship and tried to persuade him."

Scratching his messy hair, Li Yao said rather hesitatingly, "Well, if he did report it to Your Majesty, he must've claimed that he refused me determinedly and even released abundant crystal suits and Colossi to kill me, but I still escaped in the end. However, his story was fake. It was just a play that he and I acted together."

"Is that so?"

Terrible fire was surging inside Wuying Qi's eyes. "So to speak, even Lei Chenghu, the God of War and 'the last Immortal Cultivator of the Imperium', has also betrayed me?"

"Not exactly. Please make no mistake, Your Majesty. Lei Chenghu is the toughest Immortal Cultivator and will never betray Your Majesty easily for the Cultivators."

Li Yao waved his hands quickly and said, "However, I stupidly told Lei Chenghu that Your Majesty might be doing something that… jeopardized the true path of immortality, say erasing the free will of the Immortal Cultivators through brainwashing or destroying the whole Empyreal Terminus Sector.

"For Lei Chenghu, the Imperium seems more important than Your Majesty. Getting to the bottom of it, he is loyal to the Imperium but not to Your Majesty, to the true path of immortality but not to the one who created it, and to the free will of mankind but not the order of a certain supreme being. That is where his belief lies and must not be corrupted.

"We talked for such a long time that my mouth almost went dry, but in the end, he was only skeptical and claimed that he would never betray Your Majesty without concrete evidence.

"Besides, the Seven Seas Space Zone was still an utter mess. Most of Yun Xuefeng's remaining troops had been taken over by Song Yushi, who was still capable of dealing with the Astounding Thunder Fleet. Lei Chenghu needed time to finish Song Yushi's fleet. Even if he planned to interfere with the battle in the capital, he was still incapable of that!

"Therefore, we reached an agreement with Lei Chenghu and split. We came to the capital to look for evidence, and he would finish Song Yushi and the likes as soon as possible, hoping to crush all the enemies in the Seven Seas Space Zone in ten to fifteen days. Then, he would gather an elite fleet and wait for our message.

"Also, I believe that Lei Chenghu never meant to betray Your Majesty. Once he perceives the amazement of enlightenment, he will definitely support Your Majesty too.

"Therefore, please don't punish General Lei Chenghu, Your Majesty. He… He is truly a great loyal man, much more loyal than me!"

Wuying Qi smiled and said, "That explains a lot. So to speak, you were sent here by Lei Chenghu's information boat, weren't you? How did you plan to search for evidence at the beginning?"

"Yes, we came here on Lei Chenghu's boat."

Li Yao said, "After Nepenthe's uprising last time, we accidentally discovered the Gold Crystal Pyramid. At that time, the Gold Crystal Pyramid was not unearthed yet, but we already detected that it was of paramount importance for Your Majesty and that it must've been your ultimate secret weapon. Therefore, we reached this place again this time.

Chapter 2627: It's You Who Crossed the Line!

Long Yangjun's declaration broke the atmosphere between her and Li Yao like lightning on a sunny day.

Li Yao's expression of "loyalty and devotion" was frozen on his face, and cold sweat leaked out of his forehead again.

Long Yangjun hadn't been seated in the cockpit of the Heavenly Crystal yet. When her closed eyes were suddenly opened, she saw Wuying Qi staring at her thoughtfully.

It was the most awkward phase for a Colossus. She needed another few seconds to be fully equipped and to carry out the maximal combat ability.

Wuying Qi, on the other hand, wouldn't give her a few seconds.

The Colossus "Arsonist" arrived from the void!

"Is this the superweapon that crushed three experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage and three Colossi in a row? It is truly marvelous. However, let's study it later when we have more time. Chapter 2628: The Art of Lies

Wuying Qi and Long Yangjun both put on the expression of disbelief after hearing what Li Yao said.

Long Yangjun was dumbfounded for a long time by what he said.

Wuying Qi was also deep in thought, with ripples spreading out of his eyes.

Both Cultivators and Immortal Cultivators needed the support of strong beliefs when they reached high levels. Even if Wuying Qi was a lunatic, he was still a lunatic with dreams, ambitions, and beliefs.

It must be noted that the greatest enemies right now are still the ace fleets of the four families as well as the main force of the Covenant Alliance that hasn't shown up yet!

Long Yangjun blushed hard under Li Yao's barrage. It was not until a long time later that she finally said, "Unless—"

Without the space gate, how is Lei Chenghu's fleet going to jump over in an organized manner? He'll be committing suicide if the starships jump over one by one!

Li Yao sneered, "His Majesty's magnificent plan that has been worked on for a hundred years can be sabotaged by two Colossi?"

Wuying Qi was silent. The brilliance in his eyes was bouncing like fire in a wild wind.

"Of course—"

Li Yao offered to give the Cosmos Ring that stored the Arsonist to Wuying Qi as he said, "Now that you have vilified me again and again, in order to prove my loyalty, I would like to ask His Majesty to protect my Colossus for me. Does it really matter? I believe that His Majesty will do me justice!"

Long Yangjun scorned and presented her own Cosmos Ring too.

Li Yao and Long Yangjun gritted their teeth tightly. Under the astonishing self-control, their biophysical parameters did not change much, but their pupils and their nostrils were widened by 5% at the same time.

Holding the two Cosmos Rings in his hands, Wuying Qi filled them with spiritual energy and made them chase after each other like two minor shooting stars. While playing with them casually, he observed Li Yao and Long Yangjun, as if he were trying to dig out their hearts and see what was on them.

In the end, he smiled and absorbed the two Cosmos Rings back into his palm. Clenching his fist, he tossed the Cosmos Rings back to the two of them.

"A gentleman does not take somebody else's love. The whole universe is my possession. Why would I seize two Colossi that belong to you?"

His hands on his back, Wuying Qi said casually with the most intense confidence beaming out of his eyes, "Li Yao is right. There can't possibly be any flaw in my deduction. The Next Sun Plan is the only solution to save the Imperium and the civilization of mankind. Whatever stance you may have, you should support me without any reservation!

"The technologies of enlightenment cannot be resolved so easily. So, neither Li Yao nor Long Yangjun can betray me.

"Take back the Cosmos Rings that store your Colossi. Look, the sky is already open, and we're about to be embraced by sunshine. Now, enter the Gold Crystal Pyramid with me and appreciate the most glorious sun!"

Chapter 2629: White and Black

Together with Wuying Qi's laughter, the noises of fractures and explosions in the rocks above the dome were more and more intense. There truly seemed to be a pair of godly hands that were tearing apart the hard crust of the planet brutally.

Even the so-called "safe area" was no longer safe. Massive rocks were falling nonstop, making the Imperial Guards cry and flee in panic. One moment of carelessness, and they would be heavily wounded, if they were not smashed into pieces in the first place.

Even though the Imperial Guards had minds as tough as iron due to Wuying Qi's repetitive brainwashing, they were still greatly shocked at the magnificent changes.

It was not the fear of death but the perception of their own insignificance when faced with the enormous nature.

Li Yao and Long Yangjun looked at each other and were both appalled, wondering what kind of terrifying influences that underground movements would cause to the surface of the planet.

However, there was absolutely nothing they could do under Wuying Qi's intimidation. In order to retrieve their Colossi from their Cosmos Rings and be ready for battle, it would take them quite a few seconds.

For a horrifying expert in the Divinity Branching Stage such as Wuying Qi, a few seconds were more than enough for him to have breakfast, smoke a cigarette, and read a newspaper before blowing them up!

Thankfully, the collapse of the crust did not last. After reaching the maximal quake, it gradually died down.

The Gold Crystal Pyramid was not lifted to the ground but still lay at the bottom steadily.

However, a glittering crack had been opened in the rocks where the sharp tip of the Gold Crystal Pyramid pointed at. As they watched through the gap, the spot of light that was only the size of a needle could be seen, implying that the crack led to the surface of the planet.

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao was greatly enlightened and realized how foolish he was in the past.

Since the Gold Crystal Pyramid did not sink for natural reasons but was hidden by the zealots of the Houyi Clan below the ground on purpose, they had naturally established all kinds of facilities around it so that it could be activated immediately after it was triggered. Tearing apart the whole crust and raising it to the surface of the planet was too complicated.

All that needed to be done was to tear a tiny crack through the rocks. No, there should've been some sort of vertical tube in the first place that was aimed at the tip of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, which could let the waves shot by the Gold Crystal Pyramid to pierce through the rocks without any hindrance and reach the sky!

Considering such a structure, the place was actually a well-hidden "underground silo"!

Li Yao was half delighted and half worried.

He was delighted that such a structure ensured the stability of the tectonic structure. No drastic changes would be caused to the surface of the planet. Then most of the towns on and below the ground nearby could probably be saved.

What worried him, on the other hand, was that the activation of the Gold Crystal Pyramid had been greatly advanced. Li Yao estimated that it would take at least half a day for the Gold Crystal Pyramid to be completely raised to the surface of the planet. He did not know that it also had a silo, which made it possible for Wuying Qi to carry out his "Next Sun Plan" immediately!

So, who was delaying whom just now?

Wuying Qi was only fearless because he knew that his plan could start any minute, right?

Li Yao thought quickly, but on the surface, he could only put on an overjoyed expression and said, "As you wish, Your Majesty!"

Long Yangjun said nothing but also nodded her head.

Very satisfied with their performances, Wuying Qi smiled. Then he recalled that he was suppressing somebody else next to him.

Waving his tentacles of spiritual energy, he picked up Li Jialing who had almost passed out and grabbed him to his face.

Li Jialing looked even more awful than a dead person. With fury and desperation beaming out of his eyes, he stared at Wuying Qi without blinking.

When he realized that the face belonged to his birth father, his eyes became sorrowful and helpless again.

"How do you feel right now?"

Wuying Qi smiled and said, "You must keep a good mood. Only if you are in a good mood will this body be vigorous and healthy enough."

His lips twitching, Li Jialing glared at Li Yao and Long Yangjun. Then, he looked at the vague light on the rocks and declared stubbornly, "Let me down!"

"You do have some guts, but that's absolutely useless."

Observing the young man carefully, Wuying Qi said, "I'm very curious about one thing. I can understand Li Yao and Long Yangjun's attempt to sneak into the capital and sabotage my plan. After all, they are both above the peak of the Divinity Transformation Stage. Together with the two extraordinary Colossi, it is possible that they may hurt me one way or another. Also, they are either burdened with great responsibilities or boast mysterious backgrounds. They had to come here.

"But what about you?

"Even though you have been modified in 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky' for years and you experienced a series of fortuitous incidents, I don't think you are higher than the Nascent Soul Stage, are you?

"A twenty-year-old in the Nascent Soul Stage is indeed awe-inspiring, but you are no more than an ant in my eyes, which you know clearly too. Then, why did you step into this fatal game? Just because of the tiny trap on the back of your neck?"

Wuying Qi intentionally jabbed the wound on the back of Li Jialing's neck with a tentacle of spiritual energy, until Li Jialing was quivering and sweating.

However, the scene triggered an insignificant wave inside Wuying Qi's eyes as they saw it.

"I…"

Gritting his teeth, Li Jialing stood straight and shouted, holding back the pain, "I'm here for the queen!"

"Is that so?"

Wuying Qi said, "Why do you want to meet the queen?"

"I want to ask her why she did not destroy me in time when I was an embryo!"

Li Jialing exclaimed, "Even though she did not have the time for it years ago, she had ten thousand opportunities to kill me in the Martial Meritocrats Sector a year ago and after I returned to the Deep Sea Fleet with her, but why didn't she do it?"

"What's the problem with that?"

Wuying Qi was amused. "You were deeply associated with me. You are the best carrier for my soul that I carefully prepared. Naturally, the queen cannot kill you."

Li Jialing's eyes became dim. After a brief silence, he raised his head and roared again, "I don't want to hear it from you! I want the queen to say it herself! Queen, come out and tell me why you gave birth to me at the beginning, and why you now tried to kill me and now let go of me! Did you consider me as a 100% soul container, or was it something else? Come out and tell me in person! What am I? What am I in your heart?"

"How bold of you!"

A hint of struggle appeared in the eyes of Wuying Qi—or Li Linghai, which was immediately replaced by fury. He closed his right hand, and Li Jialing's neck immediately collapsed so deeply that the young man's nerves, bones, and veins were almost cut off.

"Ugh… Ugh…"

Li Jialing was unable to move at all while being choked by Wuying Qi, but a wild smile suddenly appeared on his face. He somehow squeezed his voice out of the unbelievable gap and said, "You… You won't kill me! You still need my body! Hehe, that's why you can't kill me just yet!

"Your Highness, do you hear me? I know that you can hear me! Perhaps… Perhaps I don't have many hours left. Life has been a nightmare and a torture for me in the first place, and death is not so horrible. I only want to know before my death… What am I exactly? Is my life of the slightest significance for you at all? Tell me, alright? Please tell me!"

"You…"

Wuying Qi's hands were shaking.

Or rather, it was Li Linghai's hands that were shaking.

His—Her pure black hair was revealing whiteness at the end again.

Even her black eyes that looked like abysses were bursting out white stars that exploded like brilliant fireworks.

There were too many indescribable feelings hidden in the fireworks.

"You—You were the one who gave me life."

Li Jialing cried miserably, "If my body can help you get rid of the poor life of being possessed by this old ghost in the past hundred years and earn real freedom, it couldn't be better. I only… I only want to see the real you before my death. You seem to have never revealed 100% of your true self before.

"I want to know what the real Li Linghai looks like below the mask of 'queen'. Years ago, in the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum, even an emotionless native of the Covenant Alliance was influenced by you and liberated his humanity. What was the Li Linghai at that time like? I hope that such a Li Linghai can show up before me and tell me the truth. Am I only a product of a cold plan, or am I something else?

"Don't lie to me! You can't trick me! In the Martial Meritocrats Sector, in the Deep Sea Fleet, in the Godly Might Prison, and in the capital, during our so many contacts, I can feel the real you! You are my… mother! Are you going to hide and watch me die without giving me a single answer? What am I? What am I to you?"

The white hair on Wuying Qi—Li Linghai's head grew more and more, swallowing the blackness like surging white tides.

The brilliance in his black eyes almost burst out of his eye sockets.

He was an expert in the Divinity Branching Stage, and even Li Yao and Long Yangjun could not compare to him, but there seemed to be nothing he could do about an insignificant Li Jialing.

Furthermore, he could've grabbed Li Jialing's throat and prevented him from making any sound, but he failed to do it although veins were bulging on the back of his head!

In the end, he finally roared. With appalling brilliance beaming out of his eyes, he knocked out the young man directly, who fell into his arms.

The movement seemed to have consumed his tremendous soul power and physical energy, making him more exhausted than when he had recruited Li Yao and Long Yangjun consecutively just now.

The white brilliance in Wuying Qi's eyes and the white hair on his head gradually vanished, ebbing into the black resembling an abyss again.

He looked at Li Yao and Long Yangjun in fury and embarrassment, only to discover that the two of them were staring at the ground solemnly, as if they did not catch his disgrace a moment ago at all.

Gloomily, Wuying Qi thought for a long time and placed the unconscious Li Jialing under his armpit, before he said to the two of them coldly, "Come with me. Although it isn't the best time yet, we cannot afford to wait anymore and have to activate the plan right now!"

Chapter 2630: The Only True God!

Inside the Gold Crystal Pyramid, holding Li Jialing under his armpit and leading Li Yao and Long Yangjun, Wuying Qi went forward on a side road, not toward the control room that Li Yao saw just now but a place below the control room that was even vaster.

Although it seemed inappropriate to describe an underground, enclosed space as "vast", it was exactly what Li Yao was feeling right now.

As far as the eyes could reach, there was not the slightest sign of boundary. The wall had been made out of certain black, energy-absorbing materials, allowing them to swallow all the light like a black hole. The place looked as deep and profound as the night sky.

Only the diamonds embedded on the wall in the shape of eggs were emanating demonic brilliance, both like the stars in a night sky and countless weird eyes that were staring at everybody in the room without blinking.

When Li Yao walked in, a hundred people were already waiting respectfully inside.

Everybody had shaved their hair and cleaned their body. With a solemn expression on their faces, they were only wearing a black robe while surrounded by a holy vibe, as if they were devout monks.

Li Yao glanced at the sacred men and women and recognized them to be the most famous experts in the Imperium of True Human Beings. Most of them were the core forces of the reformists, but of course, some were assassins and mercenaries who were after money too.

It seemed that they were Wuying Qi's most trusted bodyguards.

When he observed them more carefully, he discovered that everybody was both zealous and tranced. They watched Wuying Qi with such fascination as if he were a magnificent statue that rose to the sky.

Below their feet were complicated rune arrays that bit them hard like lotus flowers with tusks. The strokes of the rune arrays were spreading out and entangling other people's rune arrays. In the end, all the rune arrays were gathered at the very center of the black space.

At the center, a throne made of black crystals was standing like an exuberant tree. The black crystals were piled high and reached all the way to the dome, spreading out an abundance of what appeared to be roots or tentacles.

Li Yao remembered the angle and distance when he walked in. He was certain that the gigantic black space was right below the control room earlier.

In other words, the facility above the throne of black crystals was likely to be the mainframe crystal processor that led to the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

While he was pondering, Wuying Qi had already directed Li Yao and Long Yangjun to the rune arrays closest to the throne, asking them to sit cross-legged like other people and enter the meditative state.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

The bald experts glanced between Li Yao and Long Yangjun both warily and enviously, as if they were jealous that they were given such an excellent spot that was so close to His Majesty.

Wuying Qi, on the other hand, chuckled. He took out a piece of magical equipment that looked like a black cocoon from his Cosmos Ring and put the unconscious Li Jialing inside. Then, he closed it and placed it next to the throne of black crystals.

Thousands of tentacles immediately slithered and tied up the black cocoon. Then, through the holes on the black cocoon that represented the acupoints of humans, they crawled in.

It appeared to be a piece of ancillary magical equipment that he crafted for the body replacement.

Li Yao was rather anxious as he watched, but there was little he could do.

Wuying Qi turned around and looked at Li Yao and Long Yangjun thoughtfully before he strode to the throne.

The seat of the throne looked like a hole in the gigantic tree. As Wuying Qi was ensconced on it, the black crystals around surged at him as if they were alive, until he was entirely immersed in them.

Inside the throne, there were countless tubes as if it were a real tree. Wuying Qi's black hair proliferated and reached the dome along the tubes before he controlled the "roots" and "tentacles" on the dome to wriggle at every expert in weird noises.

"The sacred moment is about to come. The Imperium will be reborn under our will. What are you waiting for, my dear subjects?"

Wuying Qi, who had been embedded inside the black crystals, did not say anything, but his voice came from Li Yao's back—no, from inside Li Yao's head.

A tentacle made of black crystals dangled behind Li Yao slowly, and its front end split like a flower, presenting a few things that looked like pistils—those were exactly Wuying Qi's hair.

The same tentacles dangled behind every bald expert. Everybody connected themselves to the tentacles in wild joy as if it were the most exhilarating thing.

Li Yao thought of something and finally realized what was going on.

This place must be Wuying Qi's massive "brainwashing room", and the start of the whole "Next Sun Plan".

Wuying Qi's most intense brainwaves would fill the black space and reverberate with the brains of the many experts here before they were shot out by the Gold Crystal Pyramid and enhanced by the sun.

In such a way, he could use the soul power of so many experts to enhance his brainwaves. It was the same as the Meditation Healers who had been drained into living corpses.

Of course, the experts here were mostly very useful for Wuying Qi's reign later, so they wouldn't be as miserably exploited as the Meditation Healers were.

On the other hand, in doing so, he could perform the brainwashing of a deeper level on those experts. They were the most intimate and trustworthy partners for Wuying Qi and would be entrusted with great responsibilities later. It went without saying that the more brainwashing he could do, the better.

As for Li Yao and Long Yangjun, Wuying Qi did not care whether or not they lied or kept any secret at the beginning, because he was absolutely confident about his brainwashing technology. He believed that, when the two of them were in the massive brainwashing, their other thoughts deep inside their hearts would be entirely washed off.

Wuying Qi's conclusion was not exactly wrong.

With everything coming to this point, even Li Yao had no idea whether or not his "absolute mental defense" could've resisted such dangerous brainwashing. He also did not know if the mental devil, which was still lurking inside the mainframe crystal processor of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, had found a solution. He couldn't tell if the people that he looked forward to could arrive in time!

However, it was impossible not to step on the rune arrays.

Not only was Wuying Qi observing them dangerously, but hundreds of experts were also staring at them!

Li Yao glanced at Long Yangjun with the corner of his eye and stepped on the rune array without any expression, sitting cross-legged.

The tentacle of black crystals on his back bit his neck softly and pierced a few hairs or maybe nerves into his spine.

Long Yangjun did the same and simply allowed a tentacle to tie up her neck like a viper.

"Great! Great! Great!"

Wuying Qi's voice that contained a mixture of insanity and euphoria came from all directions, high and low. "You are the pillars of the Imperium tomorrow! You are the ancestors of the new generation of mankind! You are the hope for our civilization to break out of this narrow universe! Come on! Summon your courage, boost your souls, and transform your willpower into the purest spiritual force! Roar at the sun as a new human being, and let the unstoppable storm rage in the whole universe!"

BOOM!

Every word he said bombarded the heart and brain of every expert like the most deafening thunder.

Including Li Yao, everybody felt that the real world around them was shattered and that they fell from a cliff into an endless black hole, reaching a brand-new space.

This space was entirely empty save the gargantuan black sun that seemed to be swallowing the whole universe. It filled their horizons, their minds, and their souls.

When the black sun slowly turned around, they saw Wuying Qi's face.

Wuying Qi was light and heat, Wuying Qi was the sun, Wuying Qi was their only master, and Wuying Qi was the supreme commander of mankind—no, he was the one true god that was a hundred times wiser and loftier than a commander!

Wuying Qi was the sun, and they were mere dust in a dim, dying, noiseless universe.

Only when they were illuminated by the sun would the cosmic dust like them release the brief splendor for a while. Without Wuying Qi, they would be absolutely nothing.

The magnificence and the insignificance were a major contrast. They stood no chance to, nor should they, resist Wuying Qi. They were destined to be attracted by Wuying Qi's force field and become part of him—like the glittering radiance on the sun.

The brilliance that contained astronomical information flooded into their souls.

They were the exact same "reasoning" Li Yao received from Wuying Qi a moment ago.

However, Wuying Qi was better prepared this time, and he had the support of a gigantic array and the resonation of countless experts. His reasoning was clearer and more detailed, making Li Yao could not think or refuse it. They crawled into the deepest part of Li Yao's brain more than easily.

The decision of the Pangu Clan's commander, the limits of a universal country's expansion, and the conflicts between finite resources and infinite ambitions.

Choiceless. Choiceless. They were absolutely choiceless. This was the only solution.

A short pain was better than a long pain. The best way to untie a knot was to cut it apart.

Somebody had to pay sacrifices. It was the throes of reform and the price for evolution.

March out, march out. No matter what it might take, and no matter what they changed into, they had to march out and not repeat the same mistakes of so many civilizations in history!

Only His Majesty could lead the civilization of mankind to march out. Blackstar the Great was the only commander, the only monarch, and the one true god of the civilization of mankind!

BOOM!

Such ideas were boosted among Li Yao, Long Yangjun, the hundreds of experts, and Wuying Qi, triggering a resonation of their souls. Their powers congregated into the most scorching torrent that soared along the tube above the throne of black crystals before heading toward the sky!

Chapter 2631: Dark Delirium!

When Wuying Qi soared with the will of hundreds of experts and ten times more Imperial Guards, the energy supply center next to the Gold Crystal Pyramid was functioning at the highest efficiency too.

Tons of crystals were instantly transformed into the purest spiritual energy and transmitted into the Gold Crystal Pyramid incessantly through the tubes as thick as boas.

For a moment, the whole Gold Crystal Pyramid seemed to be transparent, unleashing the dazzling brilliance like a supernova outbreak.

The primeval runes embedded on the surface of the Gold Crystal Pyramid seemed to be revived from hundreds of thousands of years of dormancy, fully stretching out their strokes. They were then entwined into the appearance of vivid faces.

At first, the faces seemed to belong to the hundreds of experts inside the Gold Crystal Pyramid, including Li Yao and Long Yangjun.

However, as their brains echoed under Wuying Qi's guidance, all the faces gradually wiggled into Wuying Qi's face!

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

The waves surging out of the top of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, enhanced by the torrent of spiritual energy, evolved into a visible black pillar of light that pierced at the "silo" up above.

The silo was not an absolutely vertical tube but more like a hollow tree that sprawled all over the place. The branches of the tree had been dyed silver and were as smooth as the mirror.

After the black pillar of light entered it, it was even more concrete and hideous after repetitive boosts, collisions, and reflections. Like a dragon that could swallow the planet, it lunged out of the surface and soared to the sky!

On the ground, the people within ten thousand kilometers all saw a black dragon rising from the ground and tearing a swirl-like gap in the thick clouds, darting into space.

The black dragon had no end at all. It simply loomed between the sky and the earth, declaring its magnificence to the world blatantly.

Immediately, the world was dark, and the clouds were gathering, foreshadowing a storm. Blackstar the Great's intimidating oppression filled up the whole capital planet.

All the people were so astounded by the splendid view that they could not utter a word.

Some people even kneeled down and prayed that such a "miracle" could help them resist the upcoming enemy.

The soldiers of the reformists on the ground, in the meantime, had formed neat battle formations faced with the black dragon under the command of their superiors. They had activated their public communication channel, ready to listen to the speech of their top commander.

Wuying Qi's primary target was not the people on the capital planet. This was merely the collateral influence caused by his dispersing brainwaves.

The people on the capital planet did not know yet how terrifying the black dragon was exactly, but they would very soon.

The black dragon lunged at the sun.

…

On the shortest route between the No. 5 planet and the No. 4 planet, which was the capital planet, a most glorious coalition fleet was brutally and gracefully marching at its enemy—no, its prey—no, a "toy" that was about to be crushed by it.

As far as one's eyes could reach, the exhaust flames of starships were everywhere. The overwhelming rivers of light had even eclipsed the stars in the universe.

The starships were so dense that their spiritual shields were rubbing and colliding all the time, raising colorful ripples that seemed to be donning the unprecedented army with colorful ribbons and armors. After every step forward, it would leave traces that were as intense as fire behind. The hundreds of thousands of exhaust flames looked like burning arrows that had been shot at the capital!

At this moment, from the commanders on the bridge to the sailors inside the soggy cabins, nobody had any doubt about the upcoming victory.

The whole Empyreal Terminus Sector had been razed by the four families. Almost every space fortress and planetary base had fallen into their hands.

Also, they had been dispersed into the most aggressive battle formation, which allowed them to unleash their maximal firepower instantly.

The reformists' troops, however, were still hiding on the orbit of the capital planet or even within the atmosphere. They were like the ostriches that buried their heads into the sand, thinking that the enemy would be gone if they did not see the enemy.

How hilarious!

Many Immortal Cultivators that belonged to the four families already started making plans about how to enjoy themselves after they conquered the capital.

Right then, the black ray shot from the capital planet passed the vast space and shot at the sun.

At first, nobody and no equipment detected the ray.

After all, its target was not the four families' ace fleets. Besides, it was even less eye-catching than a narrow line in the boundless space.

However…

The distance between the capital planet and the sun was about six hundred light seconds.

Ten minutes after the black ray was shot at the sun, the sun reacted.

At this moment, countless soldiers of the four families' ace fleets were staring at the brilliant sun.

For the soldiers who were stuck in the narrow cabins and unable to participate in slaughters yet, the sun was almost their only reference. The capital planet was too small and too dim. They couldn't see it from the space yet.

There was no telling who discovered it first, but a tiny black spot appeared on the sun. It looked like a noise in the signal, or a grain of dust on the crystal camera, or even just their imagination.

However, after a long wait, the black spot did not disappear. Instead, there were more and more of them, and they grew larger and larger. It was almost like…

It was almost like the sun had opened a hundred pairs of black eyes and was staring at the most powerful and most arrogant fleet of mankind.

The most absurd and horrifying scene should've raised the exclamations of the soldiers.

However, both the bottom-level soldiers and the commanders on the bridge were so deeply awed by the nightmarish picture that they forgot to react. Even their heartbeats and breaths had been stolen.

The black eyes on the sun were still expanding. Several black eyes were melted into a wriggling, twisted black spot, which then turned into hideous, thoughtful, furious, and peaceful faces. Eventually, all the faces congregated into one enormous face that looked like a huge abyss.

It was exactly Blackstar the Great.

The Gold Crystal Pyramid was naturally far from enough to cause such unbelievable changes to a star. However, as the magnifier of mental attacks, it had cast everybody who basked in the sunlight into the most horrible nightmare. By affecting their retina and their optical nerves, they could no longer distinguish reality from illusion, and they were completely lost in Wuying Qi's will.

In everybody's delirium, the sun continued expanding. It had been completely contaminated and had turned black, and it was extending hundreds of black clusters of fire to all directions. Every cluster of fire was millions of kilometers long. They could tie up the asteroids around more than easily, not to mention the starships that were as insignificant as dust. Before the fire arrived, the solar storm had already turned into destructive waves and highly-penetrative particles that swept their starships and their bases in the rear, filling every cabin, every pathway, every space shuttle, and every crystal suit on every starship and even occupying the ears, eyes, brains, and minds of those who saw them.

Nobody, regardless of whether or not they stared at the sun, could resist its power.

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

All the starships were shrieking earsplittingly, and panicked ripples were surging on every light beam. The crazily bouncing figures on the control panel reminded the starships that the energy and radiation output of the nearest star had been improved exponentially after only five minutes, and a paralyzing strike was about to be launched on the spiritual shields as well as the magical equipment units on every starship. Even the soldiers, who had not taken level-nine anti-radiation measures, might be exposed to the fatal exposure dose and suffer irreversible injuries.

However, no soldiers or commanders bothered with the minor injuries anymore, because everybody was dwelling in the illumination of the black sun, as well as the profound, magnificent movement of the universe that seemed to be flowing from the history into the future.

Every one of them was a water drop in the river of civilization and a minor note in the marvelous movement. They couldn't change anything or control anything. All they could do was to melt themselves into the river and the movement, singing, surging, sticking, and submitting.

As the river surged and the movement echoed, their brains seemed to be gathered into an unprecedentedly huge web with the radiance of the sun, and they gained indescribable epiphany.

The gushes of information that Wuying Qi filled into Li Yao's head inside the Gold Crystal Pyramid below the ground of the capital planet were filled into the heads of those people too.

However, compared to Wuying Qi's "lecture" that was reasonable and persuasive, his session this time was much simpler and more straightforward, with the air of dominance and irrefutability.

In the first batch of soldiers who were indoctrinated, many people's brains couldn't take the violent waves that were enhanced by the sun. In a trance, their noses were bleeding, their eyes were popping out, and their eardrums were torn apart until their cerebral vessels exploded in the end. They collapsed on the ground, dying miserably on the spot.

Because Wuying Qi's inculcation was in serious conflict with their original belief, some of them could neither resist Wuying Qi's will that had been magnified thousands of times by the sun nor change their beliefs promptly. They were immediately mentally deranged, turning into lunatics and howling beasts who only knew how to kill.

But most people appeared to be drowned in a black swamp. After passing the initial panic and confusion, the frenzy of hypnotization popped up on their faces. Under the might of the sun, their brains were stamped with the will of Wuying Qi, which forced them to have brand-new, unquestionable, and even unthinkable ideas.

Long live Blackstar the Great! Long live the new Imperium! Long live the real civilization of mankind!

Chapter 2632: Mental Plague!

The eyes of those people all turned into black swirls.

Their whole lives, will, and souls were absorbed by this swirl into some sort of… frantic zombies.

Even their brain cells had qualitative changes. The old brain cells perished nonstop, and the new brain cells established weird sulcus and gelatins in a completely different way, imprinting the indestructible ideas on their souls.

Those people who dwell in the dark will, unable to free themselves from it, were completely melted in the network of soul resonation. They unleashed their mental power and released brainwaves that contained black venom to affect more and more people.

It was like the spread of a virus.

Wuying Qi was not hypnotizing everybody on his own. Rather, he began from the weakest low-level Immortal Cultivators whose souls were most unsteady in the massive spread. After their brains were conquered, they were developed into his black pawns, and the pawns could contaminate more pawns. When a few low-level chess pieces reverberated with each other, they were able to corrupt the higher-level pawns.

Just like that, a gargantuan "mental plague", boarding on the ubiquitous but ever-changing solar storm, swept across all the starships of the four families' ace fleets. Not a single brain could survive it!

Half an hour after the massive enlightenment, the first batch of Immortal Cultivators who had been completely brainwashed gradually woke up from their trance.

At first look, they were no different from half an hour ago, except for the weird smiles on their lips and the shimmering darkness in their eyes.

However, deep in their hearts, they had already come to an epiphany. They had realized the truth of the universe and seen the ultimate future of the civilization of mankind.

They communicated with each other with their thoughtful eyes, immediately understanding each other's identities and locations. Then, they cast their rigorous eyes to other directions, sensing the people whose brainwaves were of utterly different frequencies.

Most of the Immortal Cultivators were still in a trance. Their minds were in conflict, and they were not the targets.

There were always people who were extremely determined and loyal to the four families and who were unable to be brainwashed.

Those people were mostly the commanders or advisors on the starships, at least above the middle-level officers.

They also realized that their starships were under some sort of highly-dangerous mental attack. They all scuttled along the pathways, trying their best to wake up their subordinates.

"What's going on? Why are you not moving? Get back to yourself!"

"Raise the spiritual shield! Raise the anti-radiation and anti-particle shields of the highest level!"

"Release the sedatives of the highest density to the cabins of the starships. No, no, no. Just release the powerful anesthetics. I would rather all the crew to go to sleep than watch them in this goddamn state!"

"Carry out my order! Is there anybody that can carry out my order? Damn it!"

The officers tried to hold back their headaches and their mental shock, jumping up and down like desperate gorillas.

In the harmonious symphony of the reverberation of the souls, they were like the loudest noises that were recognized by the deeply-brainwashed soldiers easily.

With creepy smiles, the soldiers wobbled rigidly but firmly at the officers.

"What are you doing? You are not allowed to come here. Stop! Stop there, or I will shoot!"

"Are you out of your mind? Do you have any idea who I am or who you are?"

Bam! Bam!

The officers who were still awake wielded their storm bolters, blowing the heads of the few brainwashed soldiers in the lead into broken watermelons, but it did not quench the courage of the other soldiers. Very soon, the few officers who were still conscious were surrounded by the soldiers and vanished in the zealous vibe.

Of course, on many starships, because of the thorough defense, the radiation and the high-energy particles had decayed after penetrating through the shields and the plate armor, and they failed to achieve 100% brainwashing.

However, even if only twenty to thirty percent of the soldiers were brainwashed, and another twenty to thirty percent were turned into bloodthirsty monsters after their derangement, it still would be an unbearable strike for the starships.

The places like engine compartments and arsenals were mostly defended by the bottom-level soldiers. They were heavily struck by the brainwashing. As long as twenty to thirty percent of the soldiers obeyed Wuying Qi's will, they would be able to cause major trouble to the starships.

The first thing they did after their "epiphany" was to sabotage the engine compartments. Then, they retrieved abundant crystals from the arsenals and transformed them into crystal bombs. Then, they held each other's hands and marched at the bridge, demanding their commanders to listen to their "reasoning".

It was needless to say that a lot of human-shaped monsters, who had lost their sanity and who had been given excessive strength, were running amok among the dark and profound pathways, killing and destroying everything they saw. Nightmares that were beyond the description of any words were going on.

Even though the soldiers who were still conscious quelled the internal conflicts with great efforts, the starships were mostly riddled with holes and boarded on destruction. After the engine compartments, the fuel cabins, and the arsenals exploded, even the largest starship would be heavily damaged and could not sail in the sea of stars again without a couple of months' of major maintenance.

During the first hour of the solar storm's rampage, riots large and small almost burst out on every starship in the four families' ace fleets.

In the second and third hours, after the internal conflicts on some of the starships were finally settled, even more terrible things happened.

On some other starships, abundant starship commanders, advisors, and fire control officers had already accepted Wuying Qi's "enlightenment", understood the "truth of evolution", recognized the highest commander and the one true god of the Imperium, and became Wuying Qi's most loyal accomplices.

Darkness was burning intimidatingly inside the eyes of those commanders and fire control officers.

Hollowness appeared on their faces again, and their lips were curled into vague smiles.

Through the intermittent, seriously-disrupted channels, they sent messages to the neighboring starships and communicated with the commanders nearby.

"The Imperium is embracing a new life."

Those commanders spoke to the commanders of the neighboring starships, "The highest commander is back. The savior of mankind has finally arrived!"

Some of the commanders, after receiving such a message, would respond to them, "Long live Blackstar the Great! Long live the Imperium of True Human Beings!"

Then, they would mark each other's starships as black—the color of allies—in the identification system.

But some of the commanders would roar in panic and fury, "What the heck? What nonsense are you talking about? Hey? Hello?"

The commanders who had been awakened would cut off the communication quickly and mark those starships as white—the color of foes—without any expression.

Then, they would blow out all their firepowers and launch a suicide attack, not bothering with their own defense or the gap between themselves and the hostile starships.

Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom!

Almost at the same time, countless dazzling ribbons of light and abnormally expanding balls of light glittered in the iron tide made of more than a hundred thousand starships.

The starships that were close to each other bit each other like hyenas. Both the spiritual shields and the composite armor were torn into shreds like paper.

Tremendous materials, swallowed by the fire of spiritual energy, spurted out like the artery blood onto other starships, raising colorful ripples.

The starships that were "polluted" were mostly rapid warships and arsenal warships of smaller sizes. Those starships had weaker spiritual shields and armor, and their commanders' levels were not high. Naturally, they became the first batch of Wuying Qi's devout believers.

The leaders, including the highest commander, were gathered on the massive, thick motherships and comprehensive supply starships, and those starships were still under control.

However, the gap between their sizes and their firepower did not stop the rapid warships and the arsenal warships to output the most violent firepower while they accelerated to the highest speed and crashed into their command starships, motherships, and comprehensive supply ships. In world-shaking explosions, they blew themselves up into pieces and left devastating holes on their command starships and their motherships.

Before the sun radiated black light, not a single commander had foreseen such an unbelievable scenario. Therefore, they were in the densest battle formation, and there was little space for them to adjust their positions.

After the solar storm swept across the whole fleet, the engine units, the fire control system, and the mainframe crystal processors of a lot of starships were completely paralyzed. Many bottom-level soldiers had accepted Wuying Qi's brainwashing and turned into monsters or zombies, making them lose effective control over the starships.

It was often the case that they could only watch their arsenal warships crash into them like crazy fireballs without being able to do anything about it.

The overwhelming space fleet completely went into chaos.

Some starships intended to run away, only to be dragged by the debris and the fire back into the vortex of destruction.

Some starships intended to march forward and take over the situation by taking advantage of their tough starships and awe-inspiring cannons, but before long, a fatal "mental plague" would burst out from within those starships. Tremendous soldiers with deep, dark eyes would march into the bridge and surround the commander and the fire control officer, biting them until nothing was left.

Some other starships completely lost their momentum; their spiritual shield and exhaust flames gradually dimmed, as if they were cold iron coffins. It was impossible to imagine what was happening inside—nor did anybody have the courage to.

The iron torrent that was confident in destroying anything a few hours ago seemed to have hit invisible reefs and was on the brink of collapsing!

Chapter 2633: Unharmonious Noises

"This is a trap!"

"Fall back! We have to jump out of this wretched space zone and the radiation range of the star!"

"Help! Help!"

"No. 4 base, do you copy? Please navigate us to a suitable jump point immediately. We—"

From the main warships of the ace fleets of the four families that were as magnificent as mountains, panicked orders and even cries for help were echoing.

But their responses came too late.

Long before they realized that they were caught in the dark abyss, the solar storm had swept across all the reinforcing fleets, space fortresses, and planetary bases they had at the edge of the Empyreal Terminus Sector indiscriminately, creating "mental plagues" in those places as well and transforming the Immortal Cultivators who were previously loyal to the four families into some sort of zombies, except that these zombies did not feed on other people's brains; instead, they merely changed, assimilated, and corrupted them.

The fifth planetary base that the ace fleets of the four families just left looked like the end of the world too.

Countless people ran about holding their heads that had abnormally expanded, and while they were running, their brains exploded and the blood rose up into the sky like springs. Some of them even left the range of the artificial atmosphere and the gravity field, turning into bizarre-looking statues in the cold vacuum, which were then obliterated under the illumination of the sun.

Some people intended to flee out of the planets in starships, space shuttles, and crystal suits, only to be shot down by the soldiers who had been "awakened" with the anti-air cannons. They fell like flies with a helical tail behind them.

The soldiers who were still loyal to the four families gathered into islands and resisted the bottom-level soldiers' attack in vain. However, due to the exhaustion of their souls and their extreme dread, their brains could be corrupted easily too. It was often the case that a "traitor" would emerge among them as they fought on. After a moment of stun, their eyes would turn deep dark, and they would stab their allies in the back.

BOOM!

There was no telling if it was done by the mad soldiers or the desperate officers, but the largest arsenal on the No. 5 planet was detonated, which caused the explosion of the crystals. The deafening noises split everyone's ears. Materials weighing hundreds of thousands of tones were tossed into the sky, before they splashed down like burning meteorites, raising even greater damage.

The whole planet seemed to be on fire and ignited by the brilliance of the black sun.

The same scene was happening in all the areas of the space zone that had been occupied by the four families.

Not a single starship could launch a space jump and escape the swamp of the black sun in time.

Although only 20% of the "Next Sun Plan" had been carried out, the four families' ace fleets already did not exist anymore. The iron tide had been degenerated into spreading mud. Every starship had been turned into a lone island of death. Every Immortal Cultivator was in hypnotization, panic, or desperation; their combat abilities gone!

The same thing happened to the Imperial Guards and the Deep Sea Fleet that belonged to the reformists.

The Imperial Guards and the Deep Sea Fleet were already prepared for it. Before the Next Sun Plan was activated, they had already docked their starships in the atmosphere and turned off most of the magical equipment units. Thanks to the protection of the atmosphere, they avoided the ultra-powerful radiations and particles caused by the solar storm.

However, the public communication channels on their starships were completely open to Wuying Qi, allowing Wuying Qi's will to march in without any blockage.

The soldiers and officers who were wearing the black military uniforms of the Imperium a thousand years ago, like neat rows of statues, listened to His Majesty's tutelage in silence. In peace and order, they received unprecedented epiphany, and hollow and creepy smiles appeared on their faces.

When they looked at space again, their moods were entirely different.

A moment ago, they might still be worrying about the upcoming catastrophe, but right now, they had absolutely no doubt about their victory and already couldn't wait to accept the collapsed fleets in a few days.

They would be the first members of the glorious "capital fleet".

They would be the contributors to the new Imperium, the ancestors of new human beings, and the first people to break out of the tiny universe.

Long live the Imperium! Long live His Majesty! Long live Blackstar the Great!

On the capital planet, the unparalleled might of the solar storm had consumed the place too.

Thanks to the protection of the thick, dense atmosphere, more than 90% of the radiations, powerful particles, and low-frequency electromagnetic waves were blocked. Therefore, the delirium and destruction in this place would be delayed twelve to twenty-four hours.

Even so, all kinds of anomalies were already presaging a doom.

The first to be affected were the sensitive birds. Since the organs in their brains that sensed the magnetic field were seriously disrupted, thousands of birds were soaring into the sky and flying around like headless flies. There were even tides of crows that raged among the skyscrapers and crashed into the buildings.

When those crows flapped their wings and tried to hover in the narrow rooms, their brains exploded all of a sudden. Such a nightmare would be an unforgettable memory for all the eyewitnesses.

The beasts were just like the birds. The residents who lived close to forests and mountains saw the most unbelievable torrents—the torrents made of snakes, rats, ants, wolves, tigers, leopards, and so on. They fled from their homes in panic and ran toward the cities where humans lived.

The beasts who were supposed to be natural enemies had no time to bother with each other or where they were going to. In many cases, they were simply running around the cities or following their own tails until they died of exhaustion.

Abundant rats also congregated into an overwhelming army that marched at the wilderness until they reached the cliff which was hundreds of meters higher than the ocean. Then, they jumped off and disappeared into the dark tide.

After accepting the offerings of those living creatures, the tide was fiercer and fiercer.

On the Silver Moon Ocean, an unprecedented level-twelve waterspout had already taken shape. Like a gigantic drinking straw that reached the sky, it was going to absorb all the ocean water into the atmosphere.

Super tornadoes that had never happened on the capital planet in the past ten thousand years slowly spread out their tentacles too, baring their sharpest tusks at the cities next to the seashore. Even without the detection-magical equipment of the meteorological agencies, one could see with their naked eye that half of the sky had been contaminated and completely turned into an astounding black sky. It seemed that the gigantic black spot was going to swallow the three-dimensional space very soon.

Such anomalies should've cast everybody in fear and mental collapse.

However, all the residents on the capital planet heard a mysterious voice.

The voice calmed them down and made them more focused than ever. They realized a certain grandeur destiny beyond their insignificant lives, and they were gradually transformed into literal ants. As long as the grandeur destiny could be fulfilled, their insignificant lives could be sacrificed at any moment. For the Imperium! For the ants!

Countless people trembled out of their rooms and shelters and gazed at the sun that was full of black spots. They accepted the bath of the black light without blinking, rejoicing even though their retinas were entirely burnt up.

Almost all the Immortal Cultivators and the senior lackeys of the Immortal Cultivators, who lived in the cities on the surface of the planet, were enshrouded by Wuying Qi's will. They were either turned into abnormal monsters or patients suffering from radiation.

The "hominoids" who lived two hundred meters to twenty thousand meters below the ground were in a relatively better shape.

The atmosphere was the first protection, and the thick rocks were the second shell. Even the most powerful radiations and particles could barely pierce through the crust of the planet that was almost ten thousand meters thick. Even if they could, they would be too decayed after that.

Therefore, when the best experts on the capital planet and their lackeys were caught in the mental plague, those "humble" underground hominoids could still enjoy brief consciousness.

The Next Sun Plan required the radiance of the sun, but thanks to the "privileges" offered by the Immortal Cultivators, 90% of the underground hominoids had never seen real sunlight in their entire lives. Naturally, they could barely be corrupted by the Next Sun Plan.

Of course, Wuying Qi's original plan never took the underground hominoids into consideration either.

After all, they were just weeds and ants that couldn't change or influence anything.

Whether they were awake or delirious, they would be dead for sure in the upcoming disaster. As the fuel to propel the civilization, they could not raise the tiniest spark.

Inside the Gold Crystal Pyramid below the capital planet…

"I am the universe."

Wuying Qi's soul was boosted to the maximum, stretching out to almost the entire Empyreal Terminus Sector by the power of the sun. Under the reverberation of the souls of billions of people, he earned unprecedented epiphany too. He had never been more assured of it—that he was the sun and the universe, his victory was mankind's victory, his future was mankind's future, and he was the highest will of mankind… No, he was mankind!

"Hehehehe!"

Sensing the obedience and admiration that billions of people fed back into his soul, Wuying Qi was more confident than ever too. The pictures and information streams that he imprinted on those people had been washing and hypnotizing himself too, making him absolutely in no doubt of his destiny.

30% of the Next Sun Plan had been carried out. The most difficult part had passed.

The four families' ace fleets had completely fallen into chaos, and it was impossible for them to recover in a couple of days. Besides, the four families did not have the courage to send any reinforcements into the Empyreal Terminus Sector that was enshrouded in the black sun.

The Imperial Guards and the Deep Sea Fleet were also being "awakened" in order. Very soon, their loyalty would be improved to a new level. Then, they would be able to accept the four families' ace fleets.

Even though Lei Chenghu might be insubordinate, the chaos in the Seven Seas Space Zone would take at least ten to fifteen days to be cleaned up. By then, whether or not the Astounding Thunder Fleet jumped over, the new "Capital Fleet" would rise above the sea of stars under his personal governance!

Nothing could possibly stop him.

Nothing could possibly stop the renaissance of the civilization of mankind!

Wuying Qi dwelled in the soul resonation of billions of people and the worships and cries from them, unable to get away from the glorious and brilliant movement.

However…

The magnificent movement that covered from the beginning of life to all eternity seemed to be mixed with two minor noises.

The movement was so smooth and pleasant that the two noises were particularly earsplitting, as if they were mocking him.

"Huh?"

The black sun slightly opened its eyes and stared at the dust named "Li Yao" in the most rigorous way.

Chapter 2634: I Have Never Heard Such a Request Before!

"Li Yao, I have sensed that the frequency of your soul is highly unsteady. It seems that every once in a while, particularly when everybody is cheering for the Imperium, your soul will suffer a certain intense, mysterious stimulation."

Wuying Qi's soul sent such a thought to Li Yao's soul. "You seem to be reserving something to yourself. Do you have any other secrets?"

"Well—"

Li Yao's soul was as insignificant as a grain of dust before the black sun, yet it was glowing with uncertainties. "N-No. Long live the Imperium! Long Live your Majesty! Long live Divinity Transformation Stage! Ew…"

"Have you not understood the magnitude of the Next Sun Plan to this moment? You are still unwilling to obey my will?"

From the black sun, the tentacles of light as tiny as hair stabbed into Li Yao's soul after zigzagging for a while, imposing the most intense mental attacks again. "I am the country, I am the sun, I am the universe, and nobody should keep anything to themselves before me. Come on, spit it out. Give me your secrets!"

As Wuying Qi spoke, the grains of dust all around that represented the consciousness of the individuals also spread out countless rays that looked like nerves, surging into Li Yao's brain in a hurry and connecting to his soul.

"Come on! Spit it out!"

"There's no need to keep anything to yourself! Tell all the secrets to His Majesty like us!"

"His Majesty is our true god and the only dominator. Whoever keeps any secret from His Majesty will be the greatest traitor of mankind!"

"Come on! Come on! Speak it out! Speak it out like the rest of us!"

They were the people who had been hypnotized by Wuying Qi and who intended to further "corrupt Li Yao" in the resonance of absolute obedience.

Such collective hysteria, like the cross-infection of viruses, made it impossible for anybody in the network to escape.

Of course, if a certain unconventional, highly-provocative thing appeared on the network, it would spread to every terminal on the network and be received by every soul and every brain.

Li Yao continued his pale resistance and talked nonsense as if he had been cornered. "Really. I have truly 100% surrendered to Your Majesty and realized that Your Majesty is the sun, the moon, and the stars. However, no matter how submissive I am, I am entitled to a personal life, am I not? Really, Your Majesty, you wouldn't want to see my ultimate secret. It's just one of my quirks that are not worth mentioning at all. Please allow me to… collect and appreciate them on my own!"

"Huh?"

Wuying Qi's voice turned even colder and more indifferent. The rays of light that pierced into Li Yao's soul groped for Li Yao's ultimate secret like a snake that was spurting venom. "Li Yao, you are such a disappointment. I thought you were a smart guy who was capable of making a sensible choice. It never occurred to me that…"

"I am indeed not stupid, but this secret is simply too… too terrible!"

Li Yao sounded both wronged and upset. "If Your Majesty learns it, Your Majesty will definitely be infuriated and execute me. That's why I dare not invite Your Majesty to appreciate them."

"Hehe. What kind of secrets are worth my thunderous fury?"

Wuying Qi's voice was filled with the laughter of disdain. "The best they can be are just some other schemes and boring tricks that you want to play.

"However, you have seen and even sensed with your own soul that my 'Next Sun Plan' has succeeded. The best soldiers of the entire Imperium have all recognized the correct path and are obeying my will! Starting from this second, a new epoch of the civilization of mankind has begun. Even though all the gods and devils in the entire universe arrive, they cannot stop my determination! What can you accomplish with your petty tricks?

"Come on. Publicize your ultimate secrets, clear the last obstacles in your mind, and kneel before my feet sincerely just like other people and be the sharpest saber in my hands. I guarantee that I will pardon all of your crimes and your boring tricks!"

"Come on! Come on!"

The souls around that had been deeply brainwashed also shouted, "Reveal your secrets! His Majesty will benevolently pardon all your crimes! His Majesty is too invincible to bother with any tricks!"

"F-Fine!"

Li Yao's soul seemed to have been mired in a swamp under the interference of so many waves. He mumbled to himself, "Since Your Majesty has put it that way, it seems inappropriate if I still don't open my mind. However, I have to prepare myself and adjust my mood first. To be honest, I have never seen such an outrageous request before in my entire life. This is rather embarrassing for me!"

"… Your soul is resisting my investigation. In such massive enlightenment and collective reverberation of the souls, your soul still retains such high independence. What did you do? What are you hiding deep inside your soul?"

The black sun glowed ominously as Wuying Qi's keen senses detected a few abnormal waves. He spoke warily, "You are only stalling me. You have been stalling me since the beginning. What exactly are you waiting for, Vulture Li Yao?"

The black sun constricted violently and spat out hundreds of tentacles of light, which surged at Li Yao's soul together and tried to tear apart Li Yao's soul to expose the secret.

But at such a moment, the illusionary universe that had been pure black was torn apart by the brilliance that was as red as blood in a corner. It appeared that a red supernova had burst out on the opposite side of the black sun!

A new red star, which was small but equally dazzling, had risen!

Hardly had the red star been born when its light spread across the whole illusionary universe, or the brainwashing waves released by the Gold Crystal Pyramid, like a bloody tide. With the enhancement of the real star, it swept across the brains of all the Immortal Cultivators in the Empyreal Terminus Sector, standing on par with the black sun, which was Wuying Qi's will!

The red star also yielded countless red stripes, which pierced into the supermassive nerval network that Wuying Qi established through his hard work, somewhat "hijacking" the Gold Crystal Pyramid and the Next Sun Plan!

"Excuse me, but he has been waiting for me!"

A cocky and gloating face with an identical look to Li Yao's surfaced on the red star. Who else could it have been if not the mental devil that snuck into the mainframe crystal processor of the Gold Crystal Pyramid earlier?

The mental devil grimaced at Wuying Qi first before it shouted loudly at Li Yao, "What are you waiting for? Now is the time, Li Yao! I have temporarily compromised the defense system of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, and it can be hijacked in about ten minutes. It is all yours in ten minutes. Release the filthiest stuff in your heart to the universe!"

"… Although your words can be quite misleading, I understand what you mean. Then let's bring it on!"

The mental devil's arrival made Li Yao go through drastic changes. It seemed that under the illumination of the red star, even the insignificant dust had emanated the light that eclipsed the dark sun.

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao sent the most grievous thought to Wuying Qi. "Your Majesty, whatever happens later, and no matter how you hate, despise, beat me, and treat me like a shameless scumbag, you must believe that this is not part of my plan at all. I only thought to construct a minor mental defense for myself. Who could've foreseen that it would end up like this? Really, nobody wanted to go this far. It is you who insisted on reading my greatest secret. In that case, just let… just let everybody watch it until they are satisfied!"

Instantly, the insignificant dust that was Li Yao was melted with the mental devil, the rising red star. Like fuel that was poured onto fire, it raised astounding changes again.

Countless information streams, like raging floods and rivers, swept across everyone's brain instantly.

They were all the most vivid, detailed action movies without any traces of modification, vigorous, and alluring.

Wuying Qi's solemn face that was worshipped by billions of people had been neatly transplanted to the graceful bodies, presenting the scenes of primitive nudity.

The despicable pictures, the thought-provoking sound effects, and the lines that Li Yao wrote for Wuying Qi himself had all been transformed into the purest information that was branded into everyone's brain, including Wuying Qi's!

"Ahhhhhhhhhhhhhhhh!"

Wuying Qi was the first one to take the hit. Caught unprepared, it was like somebody had stabbed a burning stick into his back, lifted him up, and smacked him in the face nonstop.

Even though he was the ancestor of the Immortal Cultivators, even though he was the heir to the Blood God, and even though he was the visionary, ambitious first commander, one true god, and the highest dominator… he had never foreseen, nor could he bear, such intense stimulations at such a critical moment on such an occasion!

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Deafening explosions echoed from deep inside the black sun. It was the sounds that Wuying Qi's soul had been seriously damaged, and that his brain was bleeding hard.

"What is that?!"

His screams were more twisted than before, sounding more shocked than angry, if not somewhat stunned. He seemed unaware of what was happening. "What is that exactly?"

"Nothing. Like I said, it's just my personal hobby and my private life!"

Li Yao replied innocently, "I really never meant to take them out. They were only for my own appreciation. But you were so aggressive and demanded me to present them. We have so many eyewitnesses here who can testify that it was you who asked me to present them. Also, you had pardoned me in advance!"

Chapter 2635: Despicable Consequences!

"You… You… You… You…"

The black sun spurted out surging flames nonstop, but the flames were not as rampant and dominating as a moment ago. They were so disordered and feeble that they did not look like the extension of Wuying Qi's iron will but more like he was vomiting blood in fury.

It was not exactly because Wuying Qi's soul was too "weak". It was mainly because the "Next Sun Plan" had been pushed forward to the most critical moment. He was subduing millions of people and winning their loyalty purely on his own. Even though he had the sun as the magnifier, the blast and the recoil on his own soul were still inestimable.

Besides, nobody could've foreseen that in a battle that would decide the future of the Imperium, the civilization of mankind, and even the Pangu universe on such an occasion that should be solemn if not blood-stirring, Li Yao would resort to such a shameless tactic!

It was quite understandable that even Wuying Qi's tough mind was cracking in weird sounds.

Blackstar the Great, the ancestor of all Immortal Cultivators and the Blood God's heir, couldn't keep his mind steady!

Even though he devoted all of his computational ability in the Divinity Branching State and inferred the whole plan a million times, he couldn't have predicted the action of such a freak as Li Yao!

"What are you exclaiming about? If you can't tolerate a few pure-love movies, how can you be an emperor?"

Li Yao grinned as the red star expanded nonstop, sending out even more thrilling and despicable information torrents to all directions. "If you can't handle this, wouldn't the heavy-taste movies blind you?"

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

The information, thanks to the power of the Gold Crystal Pyramid and the sun, was shot into the brains of millions of Immortal Cultivators in the whole space zone.

The first to take the hit was naturally the hundreds of bald experts around Li Yao and Wuying Qi.

Those experts were the "right-hand bodyguards" that Wuying Qi had selected carefully. After repetitive "enlightenment sessions", they believed in Wuying Qi's ideology, and their loyalty was absolutely unquestionable.

However, even if their loyalty were doubled, the stimulation of the unbelievable pictures and moans that were stuffed into their heads all of a sudden would still be too… stimulating.

One second earlier, their heads were still filled with the grandeur, glorious, and profound things. Inside their heads were the future of mankind, the fate of the universe, and the sacred and dominating image of Wuying Qi who was wielding his longsword and chopping the thorns on his way.

However, in the next second, such a magnificent image, along with the vision of the universe, was shattered into pieces and replaced by… certain things that could not be described.

The highest commander, the supreme master, and the one true god of mankind, like a hairless monkey, was messing with certain other hairless monkeys, as if they were fighting.

Also, the commander, master, and god was the losing one.

"Ahhhhhhhhhhhhh!"

The souls of the hundreds of bald experts all screamed miserably.

Together with the exclamations of disbelief, the marks that Wuying Qi had imprinted on their brains repetitively earlier were gradually loosened, shaken, and collapsed!

"Fake! Fake! This is all fake! This is the enemy's scam!"

"Of course it is fake and scam, but this is our supreme and almighty His Majesty! Why did you allow such goddamn illusions to appear at the most critical moment of our magnificent cause?"

"How can you allow it? How can you tolerate it?"

Such suspicion wandered among the hundreds of heads like a shining electric arc.

It was true that Wuying Qi did not wash away the bald experts' normal logic thinking.

What he needed was the elite soldiers who were loyal but could make decisions on their own, instead of puppets made of flesh and blood with empty minds. Logical thinking abilities were necessary things that could not be entirely washed off.

However, he had brought the minds of those experts into his pace by way of "enlightenment" before, only allowing them to think according to the patterns he set. Naturally, they would reach the conclusion to surrender to him however they considered.

Now, his pace had been disrupted by Li Yao, and the patterns he set earlier were broken by Li Yao's shameless and contemptible trick too!

The bald experts who were qualified to be chosen by Wuying Qi were at least above the peak of the Core Formation Stage. Their abilities in logical thinking were unquestionable. When they jumped out of the framework that Wuying Qi set for themselves and reviewed the situation on their own, they immediately discovered a natural contradiction.

Was it possible for a scrupulous commander and an almighty god to have overlooked the enemy's tricks?

Even the "righthand bodyguards" that had been brainwashed by Wuying Qi for a long time began to have doubt, not to mention the Immortal Cultivators of the four families who had just been brainwashed and whose stance was not steady yet.

Speaking of which, those Immortal Cultivators of the four families were quite poor. They suffered heavy losses during the rampage of the solar storm first. Then, some of them went mad, and many of their brains exploded during the massive brainwashing. After that, many more were killed in their self-brutalization. Now, they were finally pacified and ready to listen to His Majesty's tutelage, but all of a sudden, their heads were filled with such stimulating, violent, and filthy stuff!

Their brains that had just been scourged were tormented again, and their hearts that had just taken a 180-degree turn took yet another 180-degree turn, except that the second turn was a vertical one. All in all, the Immortal Cultivators' minds were too twisted, and their admiration and belief in Wuying Qi had been too severely blasphemed for them to return to the previous state.

"Eyes! My eyes!"

Countless Immortal Cultivators screamed while covering their eyes, with thick blood darting out of the gaps of their fingers.

"Ahhhhhhhhhhhh!"

Even the lunatics whose minds had just collapsed were also wailing and writhing on the ground while holding their heads.

"This is—"

Many senior commanders, brainwashed by Wuying Qi, just ordered their starships to kill their fellow starships who were too obstinate to change five minutes ago as a gesture of their loyalty, only to watch Wuying Qi's alluring bed battles.

After being stunned for a long time, their darkened eyes gradually turned normal, and they took out the storm bolters on their waists in disbelief. Putting it into their mouths, they cursed vaguely and pulled the triggers.

The four families' ace fleets that were extremely chaotic in the first place became even more chaotic.

The same thing was happening in every asteroid base, the Deep Sea Fleet, every troop of the Imperial Guards, and the hundreds of cities on the surface of the capital planet. There had never been such a ludicrous folly in the history of the Imperium—no, in the history of mankind!

None of the words that human beings had ever used could describe what Wuying Qi was feeling.

The surprise, shock, fear, and fury of the billions of souls were fed back to his soul clearly, turning into twisted ripples on the surface of the black sun.

The magnificent "Next Sun Plan" was suffering an avalanche of failures.

His "mental plague" encountered an even more powerful "mental plague". His splendid plan had been overpowered by primitive instincts!

After Wuying Qi finally held back his urge of vomiting blood and calmed himself down, the first thing he attempted to do was to stop the Gold Crystal Pyramid from further sending the twisted waves to the sun.

However, things were too tricky for him to get back under control.
