But his smile had been frozen, and beads of cold sweat were popping up on his forehead.

Wuying Qi looked down at him pitifully, as if he were a poor idiot.

Chapter 2644: Wuying Qi's Fatal Weakness!

"How is it possible?"

Activating the Cosmos Ring and retrieving the Colossus were the simplest moves. Li Yao couldn't have done it wrong even if his hands were cramping. However, there was absolutely no response no matter how he poured his spiritual energy into the Cosmos Ring.

No, there was a response. When he blew tremendous spiritual energy into the Cosmos Ring, trying to tear it apart, thorny electric arcs dashed out from the Cosmos Ring and shocked him. He couldn't help but scream.

He noticed that Long Yangjun was waving her arm too. Her Colossus, "Heavenly Crystal", had also been locked in her Cosmos Ring!

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Li Yao recalled what happened just now and soon figured out the reason. Not long ago, in order to win Wuying Qi's trust, they had offered the Cosmos Rings that stored their Colossi to Wuying Qi.

Wuying Qi seemed to be convinced of their loyalty, but actually, he did not throw the Cosmos Rings back to them until after he clenched the Cosmos Rings in his hand.

Damn it! They were tricked!

The clench of someone in the Divinity Branching Stage was certainly not a joke. The retrieval rune arrays of the Cosmos Rings must've been ruined back then!

No wonder Wuying Qi had been so confident. From his perspective, he destroyed Li Yao and Long Yangjun's biggest reliance first and then attracted them to a brainwashing space with hundreds of other experts. Even if they were pretending to surrender at the start, they would have to surrender for real in the end.

Of course, Wuying Qi did not expect Li Yao to be so cunning that he would disrupt the massive brainwashing so skillfully. He was a victim of his own scheme.

Now that the supervillain was infuriated and dropped his disguise, Li Yao and Long Yangjun were caught in an awkward situation!

"How could you do that?"

Realizing everything, Li Yao pointed at Wuying Qi's nose and scolded, "How can you be so shameless as an emperor? You said you gave the Colossi back to us because you trusted us, but you actually played tricks on our Cosmos Rings?

"I despise the lying, stealthy people who never dare to put up a fight and can only resort to tricks and schemes most. If you think you're good, just unlock our Cosmos Rings, and let's have a fight. If you win, we will… Aya!"

Before Li Yao finished, Wuying Qi had gathered a blood-red hand and slapped Li Yao right in the face, spinning Li Yao in midair. He vomited blood crazily, and half of his face was swollen, making it impossible for him to speak anymore.

"Li Yao!"

Long Yangjun jumped forward, trying to flee with Li Yao. But the place had been dimmed by shadows and blood, and it was impossible to find a way out. Long Yangjun was more than anxious. She said angrily when she saw Li Yao's swollen face, "Just stop talking? Didn't you notice that the more you talk, the stronger our enemy is? You're only getting us killed!"

"What are you talking about? Is it really my fault?"

Li Yao said, "If you hadn't insisted on submitting our Colossi to him, we wouldn't have been caught in such a weird situation!"

"We had nothing but eye contact. What more did you expect me to do when you hinted that I should work with you?"

Long Yangjun said angrily, "Besides, do you really think he's an idiot? He knew that we carried Colossi, and yours had crushed the Colossi of the three Divinity Transformation Stage experts. Do you think he wouldn't do anything about it? Even if I hadn't submitted the Colossi, he would've still played tricks on the Colossi, or he might even ask to examine them!"

"All in all, the bottom line is…"

While vomiting blood, Li Yao squeezed a creepy smile with his swollen face. Staring at Wuying Qi who was approaching, Li Yao declared, "Wuying Qi, I was not wrong. You're not confident in your Next Sun Plan!

"Even you do not believe in your brainwashing technology or that you are an omnipotent god. That's why you played a trick on our Cosmos Rings, in case anything went wrong.

"It's the greatest irony that you've done to yourself and the biggest strike for your belief!

"No wonder you were defeated by us in the mental battlefield. If you don't believe in the Next Sun Plan yourself, how can you convince us, the Immortal Cultivators, and mankind that your tomorrow will arrive?

"From that perspective, you've completely failed. You've completely destroyed your belief!"

Wuying Qi's hideous face that was laden with thick veins cramped, as if he were struggling. But soon, he calmed down in the bloody aura.

"Whatever you say, the best response to someone as talkative as you is to not respond."

Wuying Qi said casually, "Anyway, you are going to die no matter how you struggle today!"

"What are you doing?"

Supporting Li Yao, Long Yangjun said in a low voice, "Why are you talking to him instead of treating your wounds?"

"I'm criticizing him from the perspective of justice and belief!"

Li Yao said, "Don't you feel that we have triumphed and crushed in the aspect of belief? Even if we are physically and spiritually destroyed by him, our belief will exist forever, and even if his evil plan works, he will only be a poor empty shell that does not have any belief. So, we've already won!"

Long Yangjun: "…"

"Of course, I also need more time to think. Don't be intimidated by his bluff. The situation might not be as bad as it appears!"

Li Yao vomited blood less frequently now. He said calmly, "First of all, he only clenched our Cosmos Rings once just now. How many tricks could he have played? If he had ruined the Cosmos Rings too badly and it was discovered by us immediately, it would've been very awkward.

"So, I'm certain that our Cosmos Rings are only damaged on the surface, and their cores are fine.

"I mastered the marvelous skills on Cosmos Ring maintenance when I just jumped to the Imperium. As long as you buy five minutes for me, I guarantee that I can recover the Cosmos Rings!"

"Huh? Five minutes?"

Long Yangjun looked at Wuying Qi, who was near at hand and enshrouded in intimidating blood, and then at Li Yao. "Isn't five minutes too short? Why don't I stall this supervillain, who was in the Divinity Branching Stage in the first place and is now boosted by the heritage of the Mad Armageddon, for five days, so that you can take your time to repair the Cosmos Rings?"

Li Yao: "… Is this a subtle way of telling me that you can't hold him back for five minutes?"

Long Yangjun: "Of course! Look at his appearance and sense his fury! I don't think I can block him for five seconds!"

"He's only bluffing!"

Li Yao stared at Wuying Qi and said coldly, "Haven't you realized that he's still walking unhurriedly when we've talked for such a long time? He's also stopping now and then as if he's waiting for us. Why? Does he think it's a movie? Should we play some background music for him?

"Also, don't you find it odd? If the heritage of the Mad Armageddon were as awesome as the treasures of the Supreme Emperor, he could've crushed us a long time ago. Why would he give us an opportunity to play tricks?

"So, here comes the question. Why did he never resort to the mysterious power at the beginning until the situation is now worse than ever and he has to try for one last time?

"Of course, it can't be because he's benevolent or he's arrogant. If my guess is correct, he must be incapable of fully controlling the Blood God's power yet. Or maybe the power itself will seriously hurt him or even swallow his soul.

"While Wuying Qi is the ancestor of the Immortal Cultivators, the Mad Armageddon was the strongest Demonic Cultivator in history. Immortal Cultivators and Demonic Cultivators are on different paths. So, his power and the Mad Armageddon's must have serious compatibility issues.

"Yes, that's right. That's why he would rather not use the Blood God's power unless it's absolutely necessary. When he uses it, he needs a long time to address the incompatibility issue of the two powers. So, he's still striking poses when I've analyzed so much!

"No matter how cool he looks, he is not as strong as we imagine. It's possible that he's more weakened and divided than ever. This is the best opportunity to kill him while the two powers haven't been integrated with his body yet!"

Li Yao struggled to stand up and raised the shapeless blade made of fire and light at Wuying Qi.

"Wuying Qi, I've seen through your weakness. You're doomed!" Li Yao declared resolutely while spitting bloodstained saliva.

"Oh? Is that so?"

Wuying Qi smiled, and dozens of bald experts that were manipulated by him jumped over. Like a thorny wall, they stood in between them and gazed at Li Yao and Long Yangjun with their bloodshot, hollowed eyes.

Chapter 2645: Unstoppable Jump!

"Here's my Cosmos Ring!"

Surrounded by the enemies, Long Yangjun quickly threw her Cosmos Ring at Li Yao. "Three minutes! I can buy three minutes for you at best! Make it fast!"

"Alright!"

Li Yao's fingers moved deftly, and dozens of thin and weirdly-shaped maintenance tools rolled on his fingertips like liquid metal, enshrouding the two Cosmos Rings in silver light.

Repairing a Cosmos Ring would be a piece of cake if Li Yao had molecular-level scanning magic equipment. But in such a shabby and dangerous environment, he could only try his best now!

"Kill them!"

It seemed that Li Yao was right about the conflict of powers inside Wuying Qi. Wuying Qi did not attack or summon his Colossus. He simply instructed the experts who were possessed by him to attack.

The dozens of bald experts lunged at Li Yao like hungry wolves in bloody auras, trying to rob him of the Cosmos Rings, or at least to disrupt the emergency maintenance.

Their eyes had turned into springs of blood. While shrieking, they blatantly unleashed frantic brainwaves, which was a type of virus on the mental level.

Normal people would've been assimilated by them and degenerated into uncontrollable devils.

Regretfully, neither Li Yao nor Long Yangjun were normal people.

Li Yao had a mental devil and Mo Xuan's heritage. He was highly adapted and immune to tenebrum energy.

Long Yangjun, on the other hand, as a successor of the Nuwa Clan, was a great controller of tenebrum energy herself.

"Tenebrum energy?"

Long Yangjun smiled and covered herself in a dazzling glass suit. She was holding a crystal whip in each hand, but the soft whips turned into sharp swords under the boost of her spiritual energy.

She was not affected by the tenebrum energy of the bald experts at all. Instead, she dashed among them agilely and went on a killing spree, cutting off their limbs and heads.

Brutal as the experts might have been, they could not fight anymore after they lost their heads or limbs.

"Such a power!"

Wuying Qi's face changed as he sensed the energy around Long Yangjun that was more evil and chaotic than the spiritual energy. He said gloomily, "If you also cultivate the tenebrum energy, why are you stopping my plan?"

"Because you do not understand the true meaning of tenebrum energy!"

Long Yangjun crossed her arms, and her whips minced the last few bald experts into pieces. "It's exactly because of guys like you, whose desires and ambitions expand infinitely because of the corruption of the tenebrum energy, that Pangu and mankind regarded tenebrum energy as a forbidden power and kept a distance from it!

"In order to introduce tenebrum energy into our civilization, I have to eradicate people like you first. Is there a problem?"

Long Yangjun squatted and stomped on the ground with her powerful legs before she threw herself at Wuying Qi in a streak of light.

The blood and the light collided a thousand times in only several seconds. It was impossible to see them clearly. The only indication of their collisions was the shocking cracks on the ground.

"Ahhhhhhhhhhh!"

On one of his knees not far away, Li Yao moved his hands so fast that their temperatures were almost a thousand degrees. He devoted his lifelong knowledge to the recovery of the rune arrays that were destroyed by Wuying Qi.

However, Wuying Qi's method was quite tricky. He left two hibernating powers on the two Cosmos Rings, which were like sensitive crystal bombs and would destroy the retrieval mechanism of the Cosmos Rings if anything went wrong.

No matter how anxious Li Yao was, he had to control his shivering nerves and dismantle the bombs first.

Time went by. When Long Yangjun and Wuying Qi were separated, it felt that a year had passed.

However, it was actually only one minute in reality. Li Yao had just figured out the basics of Wuying Qi's remaining power.

Long Yangjun was flung back into him, almost breaking the bones of both of them.

"Watch out!"

Li Yao protected the two Cosmos Rings instead of himself. They would have to surrender if they lost the last trump cards. "Didn't you say that you can buy three minutes for me?"

"Didn't you say that your plan would be easy and impeccable?"

Long Yangjun's suit had fallen apart. Her suit was not a piece of magical equipment but part of her body. It was a natural exoskeleton that had sharp senses and high trafficability of spiritual energy. She was under a lot of pain now that it was broken.

She was almost vomiting her internal organs out. "Things did not turn out as I planned. What can I do? Why don't we swap our roles? You can deal with the maddened Wuying Qi, and I can repair the Cosmos Rings!"

"There's no need to argue. It's all the same."

Wuying Qi's twisted and evil shadow that looked like burning blood emerged from the black fog again. His voice was lower and hoarser, like the whisper from the deepest level of hell. "You're both going to die anyway, and so will everybody in the Empyreal Terminus Sector!"

As he spoke, the redness in his hair clambered and made his head red. The black nerves, which had been withered and broken, bulged again in devastating red.

Wuying Qi's mental network was being repaired, sending out the craziest and uncontrollable power again.

Dozens of bald experts were slain by Long Yangjun, but more were coming and baring their fangs at Li Yao and Long Yangjun.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

With their brainwaves as the fuel, Wuying Qi launched his brainwaves together with the delirious tenebrum energy by using the Gold Crystal Pyramid again. The bloody bolt of lightning struck the sky at the sun!

…

In the space battlefield, the black spots on the sun had been dimming and freezing, but when they were boosted by the bloody light, a destructive chain reaction was restarted. The intensity of radiations and high-energy particles soared again!

The sun just now was like a black eye that was coldly staring at the universe.

The sun at this moment, however, was a black eye that had been broken, with blood splashing everywhere like a spring!

Many starships in the ace fleet of the four families had already used up their spiritual shields in the first place. They could not resist the intense solar storms and turned into intimidating incinerators. The fire licked every cabin through the aisles and burned up the Immortal Cultivators who were not crazy, who were crazy, and who were about to be crazy into ashes.

The starships whose spiritual shields were still minimally on weren't any better. Solar radiations pierced into the crew members' brains like invisible blades, churning their rationality and collapsing their mental defenses. Laughing crazily, they degenerated into Demonic Cultivators of the lowest level who knew nothing but destruction and slaughter!

After the consecutive strikes, it was impossible for the ace fleet of the four families to regain its combat ability.

However, it did not mean that the situation was in Li Yao's favor, because Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King, as well as the spiritual puppets under their command, were being corrupted by the solar storm too.

Information lives were highly dependent on crystal processors and Spiritual Nexus in the first place. They were also prone to indiscriminate interference.

In the beginning, Wuying Qi was mainly attacking the brains of humans with the interferences boosted by the sun. Thanks to the optimization of their physical systems, Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King managed to resist the first solar storm.

However, now that Wuying Qi had gone mad, he was infuriating the sun without caring about anything and unleashing a storm that would destroy anything. Naturally, Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King can barely stay safe from it.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

Nothing but noises could be heard in the communication channel. Xiaoming and Wenwen, who were on the ground, lost touch with the Fist King who was in space. Also, they felt that they were being suffocated, as if they were in a room full of lethal gas!

"I'm very uncomfortable…"

Xiaoming struggled to say, "Our virtual nervous network is suffering a massive indiscriminate attack. Interaction is barely possible now. We're now offline!"

"If it's so bad for us when we are protected by the atmosphere, it will be even worse for Uncle Fist King."

Wenwen said worriedly, "I wonder if he can keep the space gate under control."

"Even if he can, it won't help."

Xiaoming said, "The solar storm is escalating. The environment in the Empyreal Terminus Sector is too complicated now. Jumping here is a dead end. Even the most glorious fleet will be capsized by the solar storm!"

The space gate was only a lighthouse, which could provide navigation, but if the tides were too strong, it would still be hard for a fleet to reach the port.

"We have to inform Boss Bai and General Lei and ask them to stop the jump."

Wenwen said, "The starships and the crew will be in the weakest state after the jump. Unless the intensity of the solar storm is lowered by 30%, they won't be able to resist it!"

"That's impossible. The jump has already begun. I've detected unusual space ripples near the space gate."

Xiaoming said, "The interferences are too powerful for us to send a message to Boss Bai and General Lei. They're already here!"

Chapter 2646: A Competition of Willpower!

In the underground Gold Crystal Pyramid on the capital planet…

"Hehe! Hehehehe!"

Intimidating laughter burst out from the depths of Wuying Qi's throat. Brilliant red streaks were popping up on his bulging if not swollen body. He seemed to be soaked in fire and blood.

His red long hair was like strings that controlled puppets when they stabbed into the bald experts, making the bald experts three times faster and more agile.

A hundred bald experts fought Long Yangjun in turns, and there were more that were showing up in the black fog with their rigid limbs after they were possessed.

Though Long Yangjun was not affected by tenebrum energy, her wounds still significantly weakened her, making it barely possible for her to defend herself.

"Are you done or not?"

While vomiting blood and brandishing her whips that might break at any moment, Long Yangjun roared at Li Yao, "How much longer do you need?"

"Right away! Right away!"

Li Yao's finger bones were almost fracturing when he finally bypassed the traps that Wuying Qi set up. He took a long breath and cried, "Half a minute. Just another—"

Hardly had he concluded when Wuying Qi waved his head, and a thousand hairs darted at Li Yao's eyes like crimson snakes!

Li Yao sensed that his eyes were hit by dozens of lightning bolts. He subconsciously tilted his head and blinked his eyes.

Exactly when he blinked his eyes, Wuying Qi's bloody hair stabbed his hands, and he dropped the Cosmos Rings.

Secretly cursing, Li Yao hurried to reach for the Cosmos Rings. He finally caught the two Cosmos Rings, although Wuying Qi left dozens of deep wounds on his arms.

However, all the maintenance tools had been shattered and scattered by Wuying Qi's long hair.

"That is…"

Li Yao was dumbfounded. Greatly disturbed, he couldn't resist Wuying Qi's bloody tides anymore and was hit in the chest more than thirty times in a row. He flew back like a kite whose string was broken, before he smashed into the wall.

The remaining power of Wuying Qi's attack slithered into Li Yao's body like venomous scorpions, nibbling his flesh and even his soul.

Li Yao felt that his every pore had turned into a widely-opened mouth that was vomiting blood. Half of the blood in his body was emptied within half a second.

More dreadfully, Wuying Qi dashed to Li Yao's front right after the blood tides like a shooting star chasing the moon.

With a creepy smile on his face, Wuying Qi raised his right arm high. The red streaks on his arm left the skin and entangled with each other into a complicated and luxurious blade.

Zhi! Zhi! Zhi! Zhi! Zhi! Zhi!

The bloody blade seemed to be alive. It seemed to be mocking, or maybe it was singing, when it dug out the last drop of blood in Li Yao's heart.

"I'm screwed!"

Li Yao's brain went blank. He could not come up with any solution.

The Gold Crystal Pyramid could not be stopped. The solar storm was escalating. Boss Bai and Lei Chenghu's fleets were jumping over ignorantly. They were as doomed as the residents in the capital.

The only way out was to kill Wuying Qi and stop the Gold Crystal Pyramid!

However…

Li Yao glanced at Long Yangjun, who was still haunted by dozens of bald experts. The enemies were fearlessly exhausting her with their own lives. It was impossible for her to finish them instantly and reinforce him.

On Li Yao's side, he only had one last step to repair the Cosmos Rings, but his tools had been blown away by Wuying Qi. Li Yao had been whacked as well. It was impossible for him to finish the procedure when Wuying Qi was gazing at him.

This time, there was really nothing Li Yao could do when the weird bloody saber cut him.

"Die now!"

Watching Li Yao's eyes that were still rolling quickly, like a rat that was looking for a hole when it was cornered, Wuying Qi did not waste time talking to him at all, even though he might have more secrets or enticing offers. Wuying Qi focused all his power on the blade, and then—

The most unbelievable thing happened!

The rigidity that happened to Li Yao just now happened to Wuying Qi again!

He had summoned all his tenebrum energy when he cut Li Yao's neck with the saber, but the saber was frozen in midair. The noises from his shoulders and his bones indicated that they were broken, but he still could not move, as if he were a statue.

"What's going on now?"

Li Yao gazed at Wuying Qi, too stunned to wipe the cold sweat on his nose.

Wuying Qi did not sense the pain from his broken arm at all. His face was full of hesitation and struggle, as if an iced lake were unfreezing and the water below the ice was springing vigorously.

His solid legs quivered, suggesting that Wuying Qi had lost some of the control over his body. His eyes were rolling toward different directions at the same time. It was rather appalling.

It felt like there were two powers in his body. One of them wanted to kill Li Yao as soon as possible, and the other was trying to save him no matter the cost!

The end of his raging hair became clear again, like a frozen waterfall. The astonishing white was spreading out to his head too!

"No…"

Another angry and regretful howl burst out from Wuying Qi's throat.

"No!"

The second howl, however, turned into a familiar yet strange voice, which stabbed out of his chest, heart, and throat like a white sword!

"This is…"

Li Yao's eyes constricted quickly. He immediately realized it and said, "It's the queen. It's Li Linghai, queen of the Imperium!"

Huala!

A thunder rumbled in Li Yao's head, breaking all his confusion.

He understood why Wuying Qi dared not to use the magnificent power of the Mad Armageddon!

His speculation was correct. Wuying Qi, as an Immortal Cultivator, and the Blood God, as a Demonic Cultivator, had irreconcilable conflicts although they had something in common.

When he talked to Wuying Qi at the beginning, Li Yao asked Wuying Qi if he killed his teacher, the Mad Armageddon. Wuying Qi did not deny it back then.

However, assuming that Wuying Qi did kill the Mad Armageddon, he must've paid a price. Perhaps something from the Mad Armageddon lingered in Wuying Qi's soul and always threatened him.

At that time, Li Yao had miscalculated one factor—Li Linghai!

Wuying Qi was not controlling his own body right now; he was residing in Li Linghai's body, which was incompletely possessed.

Under normal circumstances, he could hypnotize and even suppress Li Linghai's soul so that Li Linghai would be his puppet.

However, Li Linghai was a super expert close to the peak of the Divinity Transformation Stage herself. When Wuying Qi had to tame the threat left by the Blood God with most of his power, would Li Linghai be given a window to regather her free will?

That was the greatest obstacle for Wuying Qi to make use of the Blood God's heritage!

It was also why Wuying Qi, or Li Linghai, looked so weird right now!

"No… No… No!"

Wuying Qi, or Li Linghai, was like conjoined twins. Each of them was controlling half of their face, making it half angry and half regretful.

The left hand was clutching the uncontrollable right hand so hard that it was almost tearing the right hand off.

The left leg was also blocking the right leg, preventing the body from approaching Li Yao. As a result, Wuying Qi, or Li Linghai, hopped weirdly with the half red and half white hair.

"Your Highness, is that really you?"

Li Yao was touched by Li Linghai's courage and determination. He clenched his fists, but he did not know how to give Li Linghai a hand. He could only shout, "I know that this is not your intention. You were fooled and threatened by Wuying Qi. I don't believe that you're crazy enough to destroy the capital!

"I believe that you really meant to renew the Imperium, and I speculate that you're only working with Wuying Qi because he threatens you with your children.

"But look around and see what Wuying Qi has done! He's not a real reformist. He's only trying to turn the Imperium from a country of Immortal Cultivators into his own country, which will be worse than right now!

"If he's threatening you with your children, you don't need to fear him. Li Jialing is confined in the tank behind us, and your other children must be in the royal palace. If the solar storm escalates infinitely, all your children will die!

"To save your children, there's only one thing you can do; kick him out from your head as best as you can!

"Wake up, Your Highness, wake up! The fate of all your children and the Imperium is really up to you now! I still remember how confident you were when you described the New Imperium to me when we just met. I was fascinated by your ideology even though I was a Cultivator. It is your belief, isn't it? You didn't lie to me, did you? You are still the Li Linghai one year ago, as well as the Li Linghai from a hundred years ago, aren't you?

"If you are, it's time to wake up now!"

Chapter 2647: Flower on the Cliff

As Li Yao roared, the face of Wuying Qi, or Li Linghai, was more and more twisted, until it was impossible to tell who he or she was anymore!

The domination and cruelty of an emperor on the face where veins were bulging were replaced by demonic madness, and then by the gentleness and firmness of a mother.

His, or her, left hand grabbed the right hand and reached for Li Yao's neck, as if it was trying to choke Li Yao.

However, the right arm cracked and dangled down, unwilling to attack Li Yao at all.

Even the raging long hair was divided into two armies, one red and the other white. Like snakes of different groups, they bit each other hard.

"You're betraying me too?"

Wuying Qi's roar of disbelief burst out, "You ungrateful wench! It was me who rescued you, it was me who taught you your skills, it was me who enabled you to seek revenge, and it was me who allowed you to dominate the royal palace, and this is how you're repaying me?"

Li Linghai's high-pitched voice burst out from the same throat as she snapped, "You promised me that you would let go of Li Jialing's soul! You promised me!"

"That was if everything went well!"

Wuying Qi's devastating voice came again. "But our plan has been ruined by Li Yao. It's all his fault. If you want to preserve your son's soul, don't stand in my way as I try to kill him!"

"You can kiss my a*s!"

While Wuying Qi and Li Linghai were struggling with each other, Li Yao gathered the last bit of his strength and crashed into their chest. His spiritual energy surged into their body like sharp daggers!

"Ah!"

Wuying Qi and Li Linghai's chest collapsed deeply, and the two souls both screamed.

"Well…"

Blinking his eyes quickly, Li Yao was anxious. "Your Highness, tell me what I can do to help you kill this jerk!"

"Hehehehe, that's impossible!"

The cruelest countenance emerged on the hideous face again. Wuying Qi laughed. "My soul and this bi*ch's have been so deeply entangled that killing me means killing her. What will you do, Cultivator Li Yao?"

Then, Li Linghai took charge of the face again. She stammered, "Kill… Kill… Kill me. Kill me!"

Li Yao's face changed again and again. He had just grabbed three sabers from the ground through remote manipulation, but he couldn't cut down.

He was an orphan who had no family except his foster father. Now, he had truly regarded Li Jialing as his little brother.

Although Li Linghai and he weren't very close, she was the only woman that his pop ever loved, and she was Li Jialing's mother.

Li Jialing risked everything to sneak into the capital just to save his mother, didn't he?

It would've been easier if Li Linghai weren't woken up, but now that she had free will again, Li Yao hesitated whether or not he should kill Wuying Qi and Li Linghai right now.

While he hesitated, his chest was pummeled again, as Wuying Qi had regained control over the body and flung him away.

"Hahahahahaha. This is exactly the Cultivators' biggest problem. You're too softhearted and undetermined!"

The bloody hair on the back of Wuying Qi and Li Linghai's head was winning the competition of willpower and swallowing the newly-grown white hair. Wuying Qi laughed aloud. "You are too weak to lead mankind!"

"You're right. He's exactly a softhearted idiot."

Someone called from Wuying Qi's back, "But I'm not!"

It was Long Yangjun, who had congregated hundreds of sabers, swords, and magic items and darted them at Wuying Qi.

The struggle between Wuying Qi and Li Linghai significantly weakened Wuying Qi's control over the bald experts who were not saturated with tenebrum energy yet. Without Wuying Qi's control, they were crying in pain and could not resist Long Yangjun at all.

Long Yangjun finished the bald experts easily and attacked Wuying Qi's critical parts with their weapons like a furious storm.

Long Yangjun was technically not a Cultivator. There was no bond between her and Li Linghai either. Naturally, she launched the deadliest moves as a swordmaster without any hesitation.

Wuying Qi was too busy resisting Li Linghai and Li Yao to take precautions against Long Yangjun on his back. By the time he sensed Long Yangjun's array of blades, it was already too late. He could only wave his left arm and unleash a spring of blood from his body, which stood before him like a shield and stopped the incoming weapons.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

Dazzling electric arcs roamed in the fog. The flying blades were frozen, but their tips were shivering crazily, as if they would break the obstacle and proceed to Wuying Qi anytime.

Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

The fog of blood, as if scorched under high temperature, was dimming and restricting, exactly like Wuying Qi's soul.

Long Yangjun and Wuying Qi immediately became pale. Wounds exploded near their eyes and spread out, making them look like two statues that were falling apart.

It was a pure competition of soul, energy, and vitality that was devoid of any trick. They were tearing at each other as they rolled to the abyss of doom.

Li Yao could even sense that their vitality was fading from not far away—no, it was not fading, but cascading!

After three seconds that felt as long as three years, the bloody fog and the flying swords between them finally exploded after a rapid constriction. The blast mixed with broken pieces of blades filled the place. Even Li Yao was blown back too.

"Long Yangjun!"

It was an aftermath after all and did not really hurt Li Yao. He searched and soon found Long Yangjun, only to discover that she was pale and her suit had cracked. She was barely breathing.

Li Yao hurried to take out a dose of medicine from his Cosmos Ring and injected it into Long Yangjun before he helped her digest the medical power with his spiritual energy.

Then, he looked back at Wuying Qi, only to see the most unforgettable scene.

The bloody fog was dispersing, and Wuying Qi, or Li Linghai, seemed to be standing on a cliff, with indescribable regret and resolution on her face.

Her long hair became flawlessly white again without the tiniest impurities, containing neither black nor red.

Her eyes were as pure as before too, which could see beauty and kindness in the dirtiest and darkest world.

It was not the eyes of the Li Linghai from a year ago, but those from a hundred years ago when she was still a girl who did not know her destiny in the next hundred years yet.

She smiled, like a flower that was falling from a cliff.

There was a certain power struggling at her throat and her chest, trying to regain control over her body, but she managed to use her left hand to grab a broken blade that was still sharp.

She looked at Li Yao thoughtfully, with indescribable regret and apology in her eyes.

"No—"

His blood freezing, Li Yao shouted subconsciously.

Li Linghai, however, turned a deaf ear to it. Her fingers were cut by the blade and hot blood flowed out, but she simply clenched its edge and stabbed it into her chest.

Chi!

She did not leave any hope for herself. The blade entered her chest and protruded from her back. She even twisted the blade to ensure that her heart was completely destroyed.

Her vitality had been greatly consumed to power the Gold Crystal Pyramid in the first place. Now that the heart was destroyed by her, her body was no longer usable for Wuying Qi even if the other organs were still intact.

"Ah!"

The serenity on her face was suddenly replaced by miseries. Wuying Qi's scream burst out from the throat.

All the bulging veins seemed to be breaking free from the muscles. They were falling apart and spewing out pillars of blood.

Even the blade that minced her heart had been pulled out, and tremendous blood squirted from her chest.

The blood gradually gathered into a mist in midair in the shape of Wuying Qi's angry face.

"This is Wuying Qi's remaining soul!"

Long Yangjun struggled to shout, "Kill him! Don't let him run away!"

Before she finished, the bloody face had glared at Li Yao and split into hundreds of streaks of light, dashing into the depths of the Gold Crystal Pyramid.

Li Yao reacted fast enough. He darted thousands of pieces of blades to the bloody streaks but only managed to block some fifty of them.

He was about to chase after Wuying Qi when the last batch of the bald experts arrived. They had lost Wuying Qi's control, but they could still stall Li Yao for a while.

Li Yao was quick enough to kick them away and got back Li Linghai's broken body.

"Your Highness, you can't die like this!"

Holding Li Linghai's body, Li Yao inputted spiritual energy into her crazily. "Li Jialing needs you. The future of the Imperium needs you. There's also the secret of the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum… Come on, hang in there. It's just a minor heart explosion. Not a big deal!"

Chapter 2648: Cut the Darkness of the Whole Universe!

Crazily, Li Yao retrieved medicines and medical tools from his Cosmos Ring.

Firstly, he injected a lot of fragrant green gel into the hole on Li Linghai's chest with a glittering syringe to stop the blood from squirting out.

Then, he injected a nutrition drug around Li Linghai's neck, which could provide oxygen and nutrition for the brain as a microcycle.

In such a way, even if her body was destroyed, her brain would still be intact.

After everything was done, he pressed Li Linghai's temples softly and stimulated her brain cells with feeble electricity so that her soul would not fall into eternal sleep.

Li Linghai's eyebrows finally shivered, and she opened her eyes.

When she saw Li Yao, shame, regret, worry, and relief flashed in turns in her eyes.

"Don't talk. Take a rest!"

Li Yao read Li Linghai's eyes and declared resolutely, "I'm definitely going to save Li Jialing and the Empyreal Terminus Sector!"

The ripples in Li Linghai's eyes gathered into two tears that dripped onto her cheeks. She said weakly with her last strength, "I was wrong. I was… really wrong!"

"It's true that you made an unforgivable mistake."

Li Yao took out a freezing spray from his Cosmos Ring, which could temporarily hibernate the brain of the injured until reinforcements arrived. "So, you better make up for it for the rest of your life!"

Li Yao softly put Li Linghai on the ground and covered her wounds with a broken robe, before he rose and gazed at where Wuying Qi's soul disappeared.

At this moment, Long Yangjun had also finished the last of the bald experts. They searched every inch of the Gold Crystal Pyramid with their telepathic thoughts.

They did not find Wuying Qi's remaining soul or any sign of it.

But they found a terrible thing—the cocoon where Li Jialing was imprisoned had exploded.

Like a blooming flower, its tough iron shell was curled and dangled down, but Li Jialing was no longer inside.

Li Jialing's body had been taken away by Wuying Qi's remaining soul!

Li Yao and Long Yangjun looked at each other. They both saw the fear and the burning fighting will in each other's eyes.

"In any case, we must repair the Cosmos Rings first!"

Long Yangjun said, "Even if he has truly possessed Li Jialing, he can't stabilize himself so quickly!"

Li Yao nodded quickly. He searched for the maintenance tools that had been scattered just now.

Every maintenance tool had been baptized with his blood and marked with his soul. Thanks to the mysterious connection between them, he was able to retrieve the tools very soon.

While Long Yangjun stayed on alert, Li Yao sat cross-legged with the two Cosmos Rings floating before him and glowing more and more dazzlingly.

Low laughter came from the depths of the Gold Crystal Pyramid again.

It sounded both like Li Jialing and more and more like Wuying Qi.

The infinite bloody and black fog came back again.

Li Yao, however, ignored it. He focused his full attention on the tiny Cosmos Rings while he dredged the retrieval rune arrays and constructed new spatial circuits with telepathic thoughts.

Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom!

The whole Gold Crystal Pyramid was trembling, as if a gargantuan beast had crawled out of the tower and was about to swallow the whole universe.

"This power can't be just possession. It's…"

Sensing the ripples from the depths of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, Long Yangjun couldn't have looked more awful. "It's a Colossus!"

A weird scene happened in Li Yao and Long Yangjun's visions.

The depths of the Gold Crystal Pyramid was twisted, and indescribable darkness was appearing.

The edge of the darkness was blurry, and it was still expanding. Wherever it reached, it would contaminate everything into darkness, the darkness that even light would be absorbed and no matter could escape!

It was like the world had been bitten by an invisible animal, and the missing part turned into a… black hole!

When Li Yao and Long Yangjun scanned the dark area, their eyes, senses, and telepathic thoughts could not detect anything in the darkness at all. Or maybe, there was nothing in it at all. It was merely absolute darkness!

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Long Yangjun stomped on the ground, and hundreds of broken pieces of blades were darted into the darkness. But they were also instantly absorbed when they touched the darkness, as if they had sunk into the sticky gel.

The phenomenon reminded Li Yao and Long Yangjun a super Colossus, one that was definitely among the top five in the history of war. It was Blackhole's Heart, Blackstar the Great's special weapon!

Blackhole's Heart was naturally not a real black hole.

Yet, it boasted the best designs in distortion, stealth, and energy absorption, allowing it to block 99.99% of hostile scanning and swallow most of the damage. It was truly like a deadly black hole that would absorb all lives on the battlefield!

A thousand years ago when Wuying Qi was active, the arrival of Blackhole's Heart meant the end of a battle and the start of a slaughter.

A thousand years later, the storm of blood had started again!

"Hehehehehehe!"

Wuying Qi's crazy laughter echoed in the darkness again.

"Nailed it!"

Li Yao sensed nothing about the black hole. He dripped his blood on the two Cosmos Rings and gave one of them back to Long Yangjun. In the meantime, he activated his own Cosmos Ring.

Space ripples raged out. Another two Colossi, "Arsonist" and "Heavenly Crystal", which also ranked top among all Colossi, emerged from the void and arrived at the human world!

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Staying in the spiritual residence of Arsonist, Li Yao allowed Little Black to envelop him as liquid metal. Infinite streams of information flooded in his brain and his body. He felt so comfortable that he was almost roaring.

With Arsonist in his hand, he would blow it up even if a real black hole were in his way!

"You are more stupid than you are shameless. Do you really think I would completely bind my soul to Li Linghai's body?"

From Blackhole's Heart, the mixed voice of Wuying Qi and Li Jialing was coming over. "Powering the Gold Crystal Pyramid will significantly damage the body, particularly the brain cells. Even a Divinity Transformation Stage expert cannot bear the recoil. I was already planning to look for a new body before the massive inculcation, and you brought Li Jialing's body to me in one piece!

"Just now, I was only talking to you so that I can assimilate Li Jialing's body faster. The body was perfectly modified to accommodate my soul in the first place, and thanks to the cleansing of the Transformation Cocoon, it is even more powerful and perfect!

"Hehe, hehehehe! Now that I'm capable of using Blackhole's Heart, your Colossi are nothing but garbage in my eyes!"

"Save your strength. We know each other too well. Let's not insult each other's wisdom with such lowly lies."

Li Yao said casually, "Soul possession is too dangerous. Besides, your soul was not complete in the first place, and it had been brutalized by us before your possession.

"I don't believe that you can 100% occupy Li Jialing's body in half a minute with your incomplete soul. If it's so convenient, you would've done it a long time ago!

"And I don't believe that Li Jialing, my brother, will be suppressed by you so easily. He came to the capital to save his mother even though he might die. He is more determined than anyone. He's our final weapon!

"Jialing, I believe that you can hear us. We won't give up on you. You must not give up on your mother either. You're going to live and reunite. Now, fight with me. Let's blow up this scumbag together!"

As Li Yao roared crazily, Arsonist fell on one of its knees and opened its arms, activating all the offensive magic equipment that could be activated. Hundreds of streams of light gathered into a torrent of destructive and pressed at Blackhole's Heart!

However, the attacks did not cause any damage at all before they were swallowed by the darkness.

"Naive!"

Wuying Qi's scornful laughter echoed in the darkness. "Do you not know the features of Blackhole's Heart? Your attack won't work on Blackhole's Heart even if it's ten times more powerful. This is the strongest Colossus in history!"

"Oh? Is that so?"

Long Yangjun's Heavenly Crystal also joined the battle. As if it had turned into a transparent turret, every facet of the crystals on the Colossus glittered in the darkness. "I don't believe it!"

"You—"

Wuying Qi's voice was obviously more anxious than before. The all-consuming darkness was also shivering, revealing different shades.

"Wuying Qi, you can't brag about yourself anymore!"

Keenly sensing the tremor of the darkness, Li Yao burst into laughter and almost transmitted his blood into Arsonist as fuel. "The fact is obvious that you are not a god but only a mortal, and that what you're equipped with is not a real black hole but a Colossus. A powerful Colossus requires a powerful man to use it, but you are not a powerful man. You are just a scoundrel!

"Even if it's a real black hole that you're using today, it will still be blown up by us. Even if you control all the darkness in the universe, I will cut the darkness apart and let light in. Die now!"

As he roared crazily, a fire that could burn through the firmament rose on Arsonist. He raised his arms, and the two weapons in his hands were combined into a gigantic sword fifty meters long that almost slashed the Gold Crystal Pyramid into halves.

With the gigantic sword in his hand, Li Yao charged at the black hole!

Chapter 2649: Drunk Deity!

The black hole opened its bloody mouth and swallowed Arsonist like a beast that considered the universe as its food.

The blazing Arsonist disappeared from this world all of a sudden after it was swallowed by the darkness. Neither the waves from the Colossus nor the attacks that Li Yao launched could be detected anymore.

Even Long Yangjun could not sense Li Yao and Wuying Qi anymore even though she was nearby, as if they had been teleported into another battlefield through the black hole. Their everything had been wiped off from this world!

In the darkness, Li Yao felt that he had really fallen into a black hole. On one hand, he was torn by furious tidal forces. On the other hand, his spiritual energy and ammunitions were consumed crazily.

More depressingly, neither his attacks with his sword nor the bombardment of crystal cannons received any feedback. It was like he punched heavily a hundred times in a row, only to hit nothing but air. Li Yao felt that he was fighting against the void.

"Have you sensed it? This is the power of Blackhole's Heart. This is my domain!"

Wuying Qi's voice came from every direction in the darkness, making it impossible for Li Yao to locate or lock onto him. "Nothing can escape me in my domain. Your struggle will be futile!"

"You think you can scare me?"

Li Yao waved his sword crazily. Supposedly, his sword should've minced every inch of the space within a thousand meters, but it couldn't touch any armor of Blackhole's Heart.

More critically, he couldn't withdraw from the darkness even though he ran back in the same way!

The absolute darkness that absorbed all light, radiations, and information replaced reality and caged Li Yao. No matter how Arsonist struggled, it could not see any light.

Perceiving the crazy data in his head, Li Yao realized the terrible situation that Arsonist was in. The powerful interference abilities of Blackhole's Heart had ruined the navigation and surveillance systems of Arsonist. Even Li Yao's optical and auditory nerves were twisted too!

Divinity Branching Stage was Divinity Branching Stage anyway, and Blackstar the Great was still Blackstar the Great. However unscrupulous he was, he was definitely the most powerful villain that Li Yao had ever fought. Even his remaining soul could already suppress Li Yao!

"Li Yao, where are you?"

Long Yangjun broke into Wuying Qi's domain in Heavenly Crystal, bringing light to the absolute darkness.

But the brilliant light was swallowed by the darkness after only one moment.

Their communication was corrupted by the darkness before Li Yao was able to reach out to Long Yangjun. He could hear nothing but Wuying Qi's laughter.

"I don't believe it!"

Gritting his teeth, Li Yao launched all the bombs and bullets on the Colossus. "It will consume tremendous energy to maintain such a terrifying domain. Now, it's two against one. Let's see how long you can last!"

He slashed the gigantic sword again, raising ripples that were less dark, but the ripples were restored very soon and were exactly the same as the previous darkness.

In the meantime, Arsonist suffered a strike from the back. Li Yao did not even see the attack when he almost lost balance under the attack.

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

Earsplitting alarms were ringing in Arsonist, not just because of the strike but also because a weird power around was absorbing Arsonist's spiritual shield incessantly.

Blackhole's Heart could both establish absolute darkness as a cover and absorb the spiritual energy from the enemy's Colossus. It deserved to be called one of the most powerful Colossi in history and the national treasure of the Imperium of True Human Beings!

"I don't believe it! I don't believe it! I don't believe it!"

Arsonist charged at the darkness again and again, only to hit nothing at all. Gradually, the ammunitions and fuel were running out. The spiritual shield was weakened by the enemy. Even the gigantic sword was too cracked to be used now.

Huchi… Huchi… Huchi… Huchi…

Li Yao breathed so heavily that he could barely grab the sword.

The Colossus had been fully maintained and renewed by Xiaoming and Wenwen before the battle, but it seemed to be hit by dozens of meteor showers and looked as miserable as Li Yao himself.

Bam!

A weird power came from his back again. Li Yao blocked it with his natural instinct. Although he deflected Blackhole's Heart's critical attack, his sword was knocked away and disappeared into the darkness.

Li Yao had already launched everything on Arsonist that could be launched.

There was not much fuel left either. He could see a red warning.

He had truly used up everything now.

"Now, what else do you have?"

Wuying Qi vaguely chuckled in the darkness.

"I still have this—"

Li Yao roared, turned around, and stepped forward, before he punched at the darkness unstoppably, "My fist that represents justice and hot blood!"

Boom!

Wuying Qi seemed to be amused by Li Yao's words.

Yet his laughter came to an abrupt halt 0.1 seconds later, replaced by a metallic noise and the cracking sound of a fist hitting a face.

Under the interference of Blackhole's Heart and Wuying Qi, all the attacks from Arsonist missed their target. By logic, this unattractive right hook couldn't have hit the target at all.

But it somehow hit the target unbelievably. Li Yao punched Blackhole's Heart's head and grasped the components in the skull of Blackhole's Heart before he pulled them back brutally.

Arsonist leaped and kicked Blackhole's Heart's chest like the barrage of the main gun of a super warship.

"Die now!"

All of Li Yao's fury and gloom were condensed in the roar and transformed into a destructive power that was blown into Blackhole's Heart's chest.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

Blackhole's Heart's head and chest were seriously wrecked. Dazzling electric arcs squirted from the broken chest, outlining the Colossus in the darkness.

The absolute darkness that swallowed everything became clearer, like a fog that was dispersing under the sun!

Without the protection of the absolute darkness, Blackhole's Heart was not as domineering and majestic as Li Yao imagined. It was just a thirty-meter-tall Colossus whose body sparkled like mirrors.

Li Yao had just locked onto it accurately and destroyed the interference and stealth units on the Colossus, thereby significantly impairing its battle performance. Blackhole's Heart was now as frightened as a skinned shrimp!

Thirty meters to the left of Arsonist, Heavenly Crystal got rid of the darkness too. It was also greatly damaged, but it still had fists!

"That's impossible… How did you lock onto Blackhole's Heart? Nobody has ever locked onto Blackhole's Heart or hurt me!"

Wuying Qi paused for a moment and roared hysterically, "My… My body! You played tricks on my body! You shameless cockroach, what have you done to me?"

"Nothing, except that I drugged you with something named 'Drunk Deity'."

Li Yao warmed up the iron fists of Arsonist and said casually, "There's no need to search your memories. It's a drug that the Hundred Smelting Clan invented forty thousand years ago. It was lost after the Hundred Smelting Clan was destroyed. There's no reason why you should know that.

"Rest assured, it's not a poison. If it were poison, you would've realized that something was wrong the moment you possessed Li Jialing's body. Actually, this is a great medication to help you calm down and meditate. Only the leaders and elders of the Hundred Smelting Clan had the privilege to use it. I didn't find enough Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures to refine it until I turned all the warehouses of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors upside down.

"The drug is nothing special, except that it will suppress your spiritual energy, appease your brain cells, and relax your muscles and nerves so that you will be drowsy and want to sleep. Of course, I may have put a higher dose than I should. But rest assured, there won't be any consequence except for a sound sleep. You will even feel particularly refreshed when you wake up!"

"You—"

Wuying Qi was both shocked and angry. "You drugged Li Jialing apart from installing a barrier on the back of his head!"

"Isn't it only logical?"

Li Yao said, "Everybody knows that you're going to seize Li Jialing's body as your carrier. It did not look right at all that we brought Li Jialing to you, did it? You're not stupid, so you must've thoroughly examined him before you possessed him, right?

"Installing a barrier on his body to die with you was a plan that Li Jialing proposed, but I didn't think that you would be fooled so easily. Also, dying with someone like you is not worth it. So, I slightly revised the plan. The barrier on the back of his head was only a distraction that was meant to be found, and this imperceivable 'Drunk Deity' is my real fatal move.

"The most amazing part is that the drug would not be activated at all when Li Jialing was in peace, because the drug was designed for tranquilization, and I had specially adjusted the formula. So, it would feel like a common strengthening drug. People as strong as us swallowed dozens of types of strengthening drugs every day, so you wouldn't notice anything wrong.

"By the time you unleashed the power of the Divinity Transformation Stage and even the Divinity Branching Stage, however, things would change. Li Jialing's body wasn't supposed to wield such power, and his power would be expanding abnormally, so the drug was activated.

"The more furious you are and the more power you release, the faster the drug will circulate in your body. That's why we volunteered to jump into your domain and made embarrassing speeches. We were not trying to get ourselves killed; we were trying to allure you to unleash your maximal power so that the drug would reach every cell in your body, idiot!"

Chapter 2650: Touching Song!

"A*shole! A*shole! A*shole!"

In the spiritual residence of Blackhole's Heart, the fur of the Gold Lion turned into abyssal black at first, before it changed into devastating red. But his hair was shivering furiously whether they were black or red.

Wuying Qi's anger, regret, and desperation popped up on Li Jialing's young face.

He should've thought of it! He had already thought of it!

If the situation were less urgent, and if Li Yao were not stirring trouble, he would've performed a thorough examination on Li Jialing's body and observed him for half a month before he possessed Li Jialing. In such a case, he wouldn't have been tricked like this at all.

However, he did not have the time!

The wretched cockroach, Li Yao, sabotaged his Next Sun Plan first, which messed up his heart and made his computation ability plummet.

Then, Li Yao woke up Li Linghai's suppressed soul, which further injured his remaining soul and hindered his senses. Eventually, he stepped into this trap when he had no other choices!

Wuying Qi sensed strong sleepiness. The boiling blood over his body was calming down. He seemed to be sinking in a warm swamp. His every cell was whispering, "Sleep, sleep, sleep…"

Li Yao was not wrong. It was indeed not any poison but a powerful sleeping aid.

With Wuying Qi's incredible control over his body in the Divinity Branching Stage, he shouldn't have been affected by any hypnotic at all.

However, due to the collapse of the Next Sun Plan and Li Linghai's counterattack, his remaining soul was heavily broken. Also, he had occupied Li Jialing's body in a hurry and hadn't fully mastered the body yet. So, it was rather difficult for him to activate his brain cells and secrete hormones to resist the hypnotic.

He wouldn't really fall asleep, but there was indeed a moment of trance.

The battle of top experts was too fast. One moment of trance was enough for Li Yao to send him back to hell!

"You're screwed, Wuying Qi!" Li Yao roared.

His roar echoed with the noises from Arsonist's joints, and Arsonist was attacking even faster than sound. Before Wuying Qi was back to himself from the drowsiness, Arsonist had stomped on the ground and punched Blackhole's Heart's heart heavily for a second time!

Boom!

It was like the main gun of a super warship was stuck to Blackhole's Heart's breastplate.

Even Arsonist's right arm could not bear Li Yao's ferocious power. It was broken and its components were splashing out.

Blackhole's Heart's breastplate deeply collapsed. It was almost punched through!

Although Wuying Qi was protected by the buffer liquid behind the breastplate, he was still vomiting blood crazily under Li Yao's punch.

The enormous Colossus stepped back, only to be tied up by the iron tentacles of the Hydra System on Arsonist and pulled back again.

"You're bold enough to—"

Wuying Qi's face was full of gloom, before it was replaced by fury. Although Li Yao's punch did not really hurt him, vomiting blood because of an enemy's attack was a humiliation that he had never experienced. "To disrespect me like this!"

"Yes, so what?"

Li Yao bound Arsonist to Blackhole's Heart with the Hydra System. The two super Colossi could only conduct the most intense melee battle in an awkward gesture. Li Yao was obviously more prepared. His left arm punched out again as he shouted, "I'm not just disrespecting you, I'm insulting you. Come on, create more black holes!

"This punch is for all the people living below the ground of the capital planet. What do you wretched Immortal Cultivators consider mankind to be? Bugs?

"This punch is for the Star Ocean Republic you destroyed a thousand years ago. On behalf of all the adherents of Firefly, I'll let you taste their suffering!

"This punch is for all the innocent people who were killed by the Immortal Cultivators. You were the cause of everything. This is just the downpayment. They'll seek revenge from you in person after you die!

"This punch is for Li Linghai and for the love between her and my foster father. I'm giving you this on behalf of my foster father!

"This punch is for Li Jialing, my beloved brother. Nobody can steal his body, and certainly not a scumbag like you. Get out now!

"This punch… this punch… and the next hundred punches are for nothing other than my personal interest. I've been meaning to blow up your head when I heard about you in Kunlun. If I don't deform you today, I will be sorry for my starving fists!"

Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom!

Li Yao controlled Arsonist to punch with both arms, which turned from storms into tsunamis, and from tsunamis into overwhelming meteorites.

At first, Blackhole's Heart's glittering armor could still be seen, and Wuying Qi was gritting his teeth trying to restore his domain of absolute darkness.

However, every time blackness arose on the silver armor, it was interrupted by Li Yao's burning meteorites, until Blackhole's Heart was drowned by Arsonist's fists in the end.

After the infinite punches, the natural, impeccable silver armor of Blackhole's Heart became dented, and the mottled complex materials below the surface were revealed. No matter how many layers of materials the armor was made up of, it could not resist Li Yao's burning fury. Too many parts were exploding and falling apart.

Fury came with a price. Arsonist's arms were also breaking and melting at a visible speed, as if they were meteorites that were rubbing the atmosphere.

However, Li Yao had grabbed tremendous broken pieces of magical equipment and placed them on his empty arms so that he could punch again and again with the dazzling new arms!

"Ahhhhhhhhhhh!"

Ever since Wuying Qi accepted the Blood God's heritage and became Blackstar the Great a thousand years ago, he had never been more humiliated and desperate.

Blackhole's Heart's spiritual residence still kept the last defense and protected his body from being directly exposed to Li Yao's iron fist, but Li Yao's fury was still bombarded at his face, his brain, his mind, and his soul through all the layers of plate armor and buffer liquids!

Wuying Qi's soul was more heavily wounded than ever. It was almost as swollen and devastated as his body.

If Li Yao's attack could last one minute—no, half a minute—it would be possible for him to blow up Blackhole's Heart's breastplate and grab Wuying Qi out of the spiritual residence.

But pitifully—

Shua!

The dull noises of bombardment came to an abrupt halt. Arsonist suddenly tripped itself over.

Its arms had been completely shattered in the bombardment of infinite meteorites and could not bear Li Yao's fury anymore.

Li Yao was so crazy that he had blown up the fists of the Colossus!

Huchi! Huchi! Huchi! Huchi!

Li Yao breathed heavily. His every muscle was quivering, and his every cell was almost drained.

For someone in the Divinity Transformation Stage, they had to pay a dreadful price to suppress someone in the Divinity Branching Stage, even if the latter was just a remaining soul.

Now, Li Yao had used up his strength, and it was time for him to pay the debt.

Apart from the seriously ruined arms, the fuel of Arsonist had almost been drained too. It was impossible for him to activate the Ultra-Galactic Cannon.

Wuying Qi, however, still had the last bit of strength left.

As the defender, he had consumed less spiritual energy than the attacker did.

The effect of Drunk Deity had been weathered through too. He could sense that every drop of blood in him was boiling again.

"Hehe. It seems that…"

Wuying Qi smiled again, and the iron claws of Blackhole's Heart clutched Arsonist's broken arms. "… There's nothing you can do now!"

"Who told you that? I've got one last move!"

Li Yao gritted his teeth, and a piece of round magical equipment that had infinite holes like a beehive was summoned. It spun quickly around Arsonist and Blackhole's Heart.

Wuying Qi was stunned. "This is…"

"A super awesome stereo!"

Li Yao spat a mouthful of blood and laughed. "You didn't see this coming, did you? This is my ultimate move—singing!"

"Singing?"

Wuying Qi couldn't have looked more complicated.

"That's right. Nobody has ever defeated me in my song, and no one has ever not been touched by my passionate singing. I've encouraged people to fight with me with songs countless times, and this time, I will wake up my dear brother, Li Jialing, with a soul-stirring song. We brothers will work together to blow up your head!"

Glamorous light emanated from Li Yao, making him look like a superstar who was performing before the whole universe. A deafening prelude burst out from the stereo and spread into the ears of Wuying Qi, or Li Jialing, under the boost of Li Yao's spiritual energy!

"Jialing, do you hear me? Don't sleep anymore! Come and sing with me! Brother Yao has written this song for you. Aren't you here to rescue your mother? You're almost there. Li Linghai has got rid of Wuying Qi's control. She's waiting to reunite with you!"

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao opened his mouth. His chest bulged high, as if ten thousand crystal bombs had exploded inside. As if a volcano was unleashing the most passionate fire, a powerful and soul-stirring song spread out. "Mom is the best in the world. Nothing can compare to the happiness in mom's arms…"
